śrī: ||
atha caturtha maṇḍalam ||
vāmadevye1 caturthe maṇḍale pañcānuvākāḥ |
tatra prathame'nuvāke daśa sūktāni |
teṣu ‘tvāṃ hyagne madamin’ iti viśaṣṭyacaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—‘tvāṃ hyagne viṃśatiraṣṭatijagatīṣṭataya ādyā upācāścatasro vāruṇyaśca vā’ iti |
mantradraṣṭā vāmadeva ṛṣiḥ |
prathamā catuḥpañcakṣarāṣṭiḥ dvitīyā dvipañcāśadakṣarātijagatī tṛtīyā dvisaptyakṣarā2 dhṛtiścaturthyā anādeśaparibhāṣayā saptadaśa triṣṭubhaḥ |
kṛtsnasya sūktasya ‘maṇḍalādidhvāpreyam’ iti paribhāṣayā'gnidevatā dvitīyādyāścatasro varuṇa- devatyā vā |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
ādyā adhyāyopākaraṇe maṇḍalādihome viniyuktā ||
tvāṃ hyagne sadamitsaṃmanyavo devāso devamaratiṃ nyerira iti kratvā nyerira |
amartyaṃ yajata martyeṣvā devamādaivaṃ janat pracetasaṃ viśvamādaivaṃ janat pracetamam ||
1 ||
tvāṃ | hi | agne | sadam | it | sa'manyavaḥ | devāsaḥ | devam | aratim | ni'erira | iti | kratvā | ni'erira | amartyam | yajata | martyeṣu | ā | devam | ā'daivam | janat | pra'cetasam | viśvam | ā'daivam | janat | pra'cetasam || 1 ||
vāmadevaḥ stauti |
he vagne vsamanvayaḥ |
manyuḥ spardhā |
tayā saha vartamānāḥ |
svasamānaiḥ spardhamānā ityarthaḥ |
vdevāsaḥ devaṃnaśīlā indrādayo devāḥ vdevaṃ dyotamānam varatiṃ śīghraṃ gantāraṃ vtvāṃ vmadamin sadaiva vnyerira vhi |
spardhānimitte yuddhe prerayanti khalu |
itiśabdo hetvarthaḥ |
yasmāt devāstvāṃ yuddhārthaṃ nitarāṃ prerayanti tasmādyajamānāḥ vkratvā stutilakṣaṇena karmaṇā vnyerira |
yajña- karmaṇi devān haviḥ prāpayituṃ devānāhvānaṃ ca tvāṃ nitarāṃ prerayanti khalu |
vyajata yajanīya he agne vamartyaṃ maraṇadharmarahitam vā sarvataḥ vdevaṃ dyotamānaṃ vpracetasam |
prakṛṣṭaṃ karmaviṣayaṃ ceto jñānaṃ yasya tādṛśaṃ tvāṃ vmartyeṣu yāgaṃ kurvāṇeṣu manuṣyeṣu vādaivam āgantāraṃ vjanat devā haviḥśrapa- ṇārtham3 ajanayan |
tathā vpracetasaṃ karmābhijñam vādaivaṃ tattadyajñeṣvāgantāraṃ tvāṃ vviśva vjanat yuga- pajjñānādeśavanniṣu yajñeṣu yaugapadyena havirānayanārthaṃ vyāsaṃ devā akurvan ||
nyerira |
‘īra gatau’ |

saheti yogavibhāgāt samāsaḥ |
hiyogādanighātaḥ |
kratvā |
‘jasādiṣu chandasi vāvacanam’ iti nādeśābhāvaḥ |
yaṇādeśaḥ ||
1. ta-vāmadeve caturthe maṇḍale; sa-atha caturthe vāmadevamaṇḍale | 2. ta-dvāsaptyakṣarā | 3. la- dvirānayanārtham | 4. ga-na-karmaṇo'bhijñam |
sa ātaraṃ varuṇamagra ā vavṛtsva devā~ acchā sumatī yajñavanasaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ yajñavanasam |
ṛtāvānamādityaṃ carṣaṇīdhṛtaṃ rājānaṃ carṣaṇīdhṛtam ||
2 ||
saḥ | ātaram | varuṇam | agne | ā | vavṛtsva | devān | accha | su'matī | yajña'vanasam | jyeṣṭham | yajña'vanasam | ṛta'vānam | ādityam | carṣaṇi'dhṛtam | rājānam | carṣaṇi'dhṛtam || 2 ||
he agne saḥ tādṛśavaṃ ātaram ekasthānavāsena ātaraṃ varuṇam |
vṛṇātyudakamiti varuṇaḥ |
taṃ devaṃ yajñavanasaṃ yajñarjanayajñairhavirbhiḥ saṃbhajanīyaṃ yajñavanasaṃ ca yajñasya saṃbhaktāraṃ jyeṣṭham atiśayena praśasyam ṛtāvānam apāṃ svāmitayodakavantaṃ satyavantaṃ vā ādityam aditaḥ putraṃ carṣaṇīdhṛtaṃ manuṣyāṇāmudakapradānena dhārakaṃ sumatī śobhanabuddhyā hetunā carṣaṇīdhṛtaṃ carṣaṇibhivirddharddhṛtaṃ rājānaṃ rājantam etādṛśaṃ varuṇa devān devanaśīlān stotṛn accha abhi ā vavṛtsva āvartaya abhisukhīkuru ||

pravagyai'bhiṣave uttarasmin paṭale 'sakhe sakhāyam' ityeṣā |
sūtritaṃ ca--'sakhe sakhāyamabhyā vavṛtsvordhva ū ṣu ṇa ūtaye' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 7 ) iti ||
sakhe sakhāyamabhyā vavṛtsvāśuṃ na cakraṃ rathyaiva raṃhāsmabhyaṃ dasma raṃhā |
agne mṛḻīkaṃ varuṇa sacā vidò marutsu viśvabhānuṣu |

3 ||
sakhe | sakhāyam | abhi | ā | vavṛtsva | āśum | na | cakram | rathyā'iva | raṃhā | asmabhyam | dasma | raṃhā | agne | mṛḻīkam | varuṇa | sacā | vidaḥ | marut'su | viśva'bhānuṣu | tokārya | tuje | śuśucānam | śam | kṛdhi | asmabhyam | dasma | śam | kṛdhi || 3 ||
1. ca-ekena sthānavāsena; ta-na-ekasthānāvāsena |

4. ṇa-sumatī sumatyā |

ṛgveda:
ᵛsakhe sanniśruta ᵛdasma darśanīya he agne ᵛtvaṃ tava ᵛsakhāyaṃ varuṇam ᵛaramayyam aramartham ᵛbhā ᵛvavṛtvastra abhimukhāṃkuru |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵛraṃhā raṃhaṇe gamane sādhubhūtau ᵛratheva rathe yojitau aśvau ᵛyathā ᵛāśuṃ ᵛśīghragantu ᵛcakraṃ lakṣyadeśaṃ prati abhimukhīkurutastadvat |
he ᵛagne ᵛsacā tava sahāye ᵛvaraṇe ᵛmṛḻīkaṃ sukhakaraṃ haviḥ ᵛvidaḥ labdhavānasi |
tathā ᵛviśvabhānuṣu sarvato vyāptatejaskeṣu ᵛmahrāsu sukhakaraṃ laghvavānasi |
āgnivāraṇāgnimāratayoḥ karmaṇorihavānasītyarthaḥ |
ᵛśuśucāna dīpyamāna he agne ᵛtokāya |
tujyate pīḍyate'nena ᵛmātā garbhavāseneti tokaṃ putraḥ |
tasmai ᵛtuje |
gacchatyanenānuṇyaṃ piteti tuka pautraḥ |
tasmai ᵛśaṃ sukhaṃ ᵛkṛdhi kuru |
ᵛdasma darśanīya he agne ᵛasmabhyaṃ ᵛśaṃ ᵛkṛdhi sukhaṃ kuru |
ratheva |
rathasyemāvityarthe ‘ rathādyat ’ iti yat |
‘ supāṃ suluk ’ iti supo ḍādeśaḥ |
raṃhā |
raṃhaśabdāt ‘ tatra sādhuḥ ’ iti yatpratyayaḥ |
‘ yato'nāvaḥ ’ ityādyudāttatvam ||
‘ tvaṃ no agne ’ ityetadārabhyedaṃ sūktamuttarāṇi sūktāni ca prātaranuvāke āgneye ṛtau traiṣṭubhe chandasyābhinaśastre ca viniyuktāni |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ tvaṃ no agne varuṇasya vidvānityetatprabhṛtīni catvāri ’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
avabhṛtheṣṭyāṃ sviṣṭakṛdarthe'gnīvaruṇau yaṣṭavyau |
tatra ‘ tvaṃ no agne ’ iti dve yājyānuvākye |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ agnīvaruṇau sviṣṭakṛdarthe tvaṃ no agne varuṇasya vidvāniti dve ’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 13) iti |
asnāto yathāgnihotraṃ juhuyāt tadā ᵛāgnivāraṇīṣṭiḥ |
tasyāmeta eva yājyānuvākye |
‘ tadāhurya āhitāgniḥ ’ ityupakramyāmnātaṃ—‘ tasya yājyānuvākye tvaṃ no agne varuṇasya vidvānstvaṃ no agne'vamo bhavatī ’ (ai. ā. 7. 9) iti ||
tvaṃ noṃ agne varuṇasya vidvānde॒vasya̭ he॒ḻo'va̭ yāsisīṣṭhāḥ |
yaji̭ṣṭho va॒hni̭tama॒ḥ śośṷcāno॒ viśvā॒ dveṣā̭ṃsi॒ pra mṷmugdhya॒smat ||
4 ||
tvam | naḥ | agne | varuṇasya | vidvān | de॒vasya̭ | he॒ḻaḥ | ava̭ | yāsisīṣṭhāḥ | yaji̭ṣṭhaḥ | va॒hni̭tamaḥ | śośṷcānaḥ | viśvā̭ | dveṣā̭ṃsi | pra | mu॒mu॒gdhi | a॒smat || 4 ||
he ᵛagne ᵛvidvān sarvapuruṣārthasādhanopāyajñaḥ ᵛtvaṃ ᵛdevasya dyotamānasya ᵛvaruṇasya ᵛheḻaḥ krodhān ᵛnaḥ asmatsambandhino varuṇakartṛkān pāśagrahaṇān ᵛadha ᵛyāsisīṣṭhāḥ apanaya |
kiṃca ᵛyajiṣṭhaḥ yaṣṭatamaḥ ᵛvahvitamaḥ havipāmatiśayena voḍhā ᵛśośucānaḥ atiśayena dīpyamānastvaṃ ᵛviśvā sarvāṇi ᵛdveṣāṃsi pāpāni dveṣṭṛṇi rajāṃsi vā ᵛasmat asmattaḥ ᵛpra ᵛmumugdhi prakarṣeṇa mocaya ||

5 ||

ṛ. 2-63
vsaḥ agniḥ vprathamaḥ āhavanīyādirūpatayā śreṣṭhaḥ san vpaśyāsu yajamānānāṃ gṛheṣu vjāyata āhutyadhikaraṇatayā saṃpadyate |
tathā vmahaḥ mahataḥ vasya vrajasaḥ antarikṣasya vbudhne mūle pṛthivyāṃ tatrāpi vyonau uttaravedyādisthāne saṃpadyate |
sā'gniḥ vapāt pādavarjitaḥ vaśīrṣā śirovarjitaḥ |
tadevāha |
vantā ādyante pādaśirasī vgṛhamānaḥ gṛhamānaḥ |
avijñeyapādaśirā ityarthaḥ |
vvṛṣabhasya varṣaṇasamarthasya meghasya vnīle nilaye nabhasi vāyoyuvānaḥ dhūmākāreṇātmānamāyojayan vartate |
yadvā |
yaḥ prathamo vaidyuto'gniḥ paśyāsu nadīṣu vbudhne'ntarikṣe maho mahato rajasa udakramya tejaso vā yonau sthāne ca carati |
apādaśīrṣā pādaśirovarjitaḥ antā ātmanaḥ paryantapradeśān svatejasā gṛhamāna ācchādayan punaśca āyoyuvānaḥ tāni tejāṃsi svātmani miśrīkurvan vṛṣabhasya meghasya2 nīle sthāne nabhasi carati so'gniradhunā karmayogatayā ajāyata |
apāt |
‘pādasya lopo'hastvādibhyaḥ’ iti akāralopaḥ samāsāntaḥ |
gṛhamānaḥ |
‘gṛha saṃvaraṇe’ |
śabvikaraṇau3 |
śānaci rūpam |
śānaco lasārvadhātukasvare kṛte pratyayasvaraḥ |
āyoyuvānaḥ |
‘yu miśraṇe’ |
yaṅluki śānaci rūpam |
abhyastasvareṇādyudāttaḥ |
pra śardha ārta prathamaṃ vipanyā ṛtasya yonā vṛṣabhasya nīḻe |
spārho yuvā vapuṣyo vibhāvā sapta priyāso'janayanta vṛṣṇe |
12 |
pra | śardhaḥ | ārta | prathamam | vipanyā | ṛtasya | yonā | vṛṣabhasya | nīḻe | spārhaḥ | yuvā | vapuṣyaḥ | vibhāvā | sapta | priyāsaḥ | ajanayanta | vṛṣṇe || 12 ||

ugra tejasvin he agne devānām aṅgirasāṃ saṃbandhva yajñanimaṃ yaṃ gosaṃdham anti parvatasya samīpe ā avyata ā samantādindro'paśyata |

‘ ā vo rājānam ’ iti ṣoḍaśarcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |

1 ||
ā | vaḥ | rājānam | adhvarasya | rudram | hotāram | satya'yajam | rodasyoḥ | agnim | purā | tanayitnaḥ | acittāt | hiraṇya'rūpam | avase | kṛṇudhvam || 1 ||
he ṛtvigyajamānāḥ ᵥadhvarasya yajñasya ᵥrājānam adhipatiṃ ᵥhotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāraṃ ᵥrudraṃ rorayamāṇaṃ dravantaṃ śatrūn rodayantaṃ vā |
yadvā |
‘ eṣā vā agnestanūryadrudraḥ ’ iti rudrātmakaṃ ᵥrodasyoḥ |
1. kha-ca-ṇa-na- ' yajñasya ' nāsti |
dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ vsatyayajaṃ satyayajñasya dātāram | yadvā | satyayajaṃ satyena haviṣā devān yajantam | yadvā | rodasyordhyāpya vartamānam vhiraṇyarūpaṃ suvarṇaprabham evaṃvidham vagniṃ vvaḥ yuṣmākam vavase rakṣaṇāya vtanayitnoḥ | tanayitnurśaniḥ sa hyakasmikaḥ | tatsadṛśāt vacittāt | na vidyate cittaṃ yasmin tadacittam | cittopalakṣitasarvendriyopasaṃhāro maraṇamiti yāvat | tasmānmaraṇāt vpurā prāgeva vā vkṛṇudhvam | yūyaṃ samantādvirbharasiṃ bhajadhvam || rudram | rodayateḥ ‘roderṇilugvaca’ (u. sū. 2. 179) iti rapratyayaḥ | tanayitnoḥ | taniḥ śabdārthaḥ | curādidantaḥ | ‘stani- śudhipigamimanbhyo ṇeristuc’ (u. sū.3. 309) iti istucpratyayaḥ | | citsvareṇāntodāttaḥ | supā sahaikādeśe ekādeśasvaraḥ | kṛṇudhvam | ‘kṛvi hiṃsākaraṇayoḥ’ | loṭi rūpam ||
ayaṃ yoniśkṛmā yaṃ vayaṃ teṃ jāyeva patya uśatī suvāsāḥ |
arvācīnaḥ parivīto ni ṣīdemā ' te svapāka pratīcīḥ |
2 |
ayam | yoniḥ | | cakṛm | yam | vayam | te | jāyā'iva | patye | uśatī | suvāsāḥ | | arvācīnaḥ | parivītaḥ | | ni | sīda | imāḥ | oṃ iti | te | su'apāka | pratīcīḥ || 2 ||
he agne vte vsvadarthaṃ vyam uttaravedilakṣaṇaṃ pradeśaṃ vvayaṃ vcakṛma kurmaḥ | vayaṃ vyoniḥ tava sthānaṃ bhavati | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | vjāyeva | yathā vpatya vuśatī patiṃ kāmayamānā vsuvāsāḥ śobhanavastropetā jāyā svātmasamīpe tasya sthānaṃ karoti tadvat | ha vsvapāka svapaska sukarmajñe tvaṃ vparivītaḥ yaṣṭavyadevaistejobhirvā parivītaḥ | varvācīnaḥ asmadabhimukhaḥ san vni vṣīda uttaravedyāmupaviśa | tatra sthitasya vte tava vimāḥ jvālāḥ | stutayaḥ vvā vpratīcīḥ tvadabhimukhāḥ pravartante | vu vpūraṇaḥ | vcakṛma | karoterliṭ rūpam | vākyabhedājñighātābhāvaḥ | arvācīnaḥ | ‘vibhāṣājñeradik’ iti svaḥ | parivītaḥ | ‘vyeñ saṃvaraṇe’ | karmaṇi niṣṭhā | yajāditvāt saṃprasāraṇam | ‘gatiranantaraḥ’ iti gateḥ svaraḥ ||
āśuṇuvate arhapitāya manmaṃ nṛcakṣase sumlīkāya vedhaḥ |
devāya śastimumṛtāya śaṃsa grāveva sotā madhudhuyamīḻe |

ā'śṛṇuvate | arhapitāya | manmaṃ | nu'cakṣase | su'mlīkāya | vedhaḥ | | devāya | śastim | amṛtāya | śaṃsa | grāvā'iva | sotā | madhu'sut | yam | īḻe || 3 ||
vvedhaḥ stotrāṇāṃ kartarhe stotaḥ vāśṛṇavate stotrākarṇaparāya varhapitāya asāyāpramattāya vnṛcakṣase nṛṇāṃ draṣṭe vsumlīkāya susukhāya vamṛtāya maraṇadharmarahitāya vdevāya taramā agnaye vmanma stotraṃ vśasti śastaṃ ca vśaṃsa |

^pūryya pūrvakālīnaṃ pūrakaṃ vā ^yat maṇḍalaṃ ^usiyā kṣīrādyutsā- viṇī ^gauḥ agnihotrādyartha ^saccata sevate |
^ṛtasya udakaraya ^pade sthāne'ntarikṣe ^adhi adhikam |
yadvā adhīta sasagyardhānuvādaḥ |
^dvīyānaṃ dyotamānaṃ ^guhā guhāyāṃ ^raghuṣyat śīghraṃ syandamānaṃ ^raghuyat śīghraṃ gacchat sūryyamaṇḍalaṃ ^viveda vaiśvānarameveti jñātavānasmi ||
ardha yutānaḥ pitroḥ sacāsāmanuta guhyaṃ cāru pṛbhaiḥ |
mātuṣpade parame anti ṣadrorvṛṣṇaḥ śociṣaḥ prayatnasya jihvā ||
10 ||
ardha | yutānaḥ | pitroḥ | sacā | āsā | amanuta | guhyam | cāru | pṛbhaiḥ | mātuḥ | pade | parame | anti | sat | goḥ | vṛṣṇaḥ | śociṣaḥ | pra'yatasya | jihvā || 10 ||
^adha atha ^yutānaḥ dīpyamānaḥ ^pitroḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ ^sacā saha madhye vyāptaḥ san ^pṛbhaiḥ goḥ saṃbandha ^cāru ramaṇīyaṃ ^guhyam udhasi nigūḍhaṃ payaḥ ^āsā svakīyenāsyena ^amanuta pābhāya abudhyata |
uktamevārthaṃ vivṛṇoti |
^mātuḥ kṣīrādinirmātryāḥ ^goḥ ^parame ^pade utkṛṣṭe sthāne udholakṣaṇe ^anti ^sat samīpe vidyamānaṃ kṣīraṃ ^vṛṣṇaḥ phalānāṃ varṣituḥ ^śociṣaḥ dīptasya ^prayatasya āvahanīyādirūpeṇa niyatasya vaiśvānarasya ^jihvā pātumicchatīti śeṣaḥ ||
2 ||
ṛtaṃ voce narmasā pṛcchyamānastavāśasā jātavedo yadīdam |
tvamasya kṣayasi yaddu viśvai divi yadu draviṇaṃ yatpṛthivyām ||
11 ||
ṛtam | voce | narmasā | pṛcchyamānaḥ | tava | ā'śasā | jāta'vedaḥ | yadi | idam | tvam | asya | kṣayasi | yat | du | viśvai | divi | yat | ū~ iti | draviṇam | yat | pṛthivyām || 11 ||
ahaṃ yajamānaḥ ^namasā namaskāreṇa saha ^pṛcchyamānaḥ anyairanuyujyamānaḥ san ^ṛtaṃ ^voce satyaṃ bravīmi |
kiṃ tadityucyate |
he ^jātavedaḥ jātānāṃ veditarjātaprajña vā agne ^tavāśasā svastutyā sādhanena ^yadīdaṃ dhanaṃ labdhamasmābhiḥ syāt ^asya dhanasya ^tvaṃ ^kṣayasi tvameveśvaro bhavasi |
tasya^ dhanasya yajñādidvārā abhyarthavāditi bhāvaḥ |
atyalpamidamuktam |
^yaddu ^viśvaṃ yatkhalu kṛtsnaṃ dhanamasti tasyāpīśiṣe |
viśvamiti sāmānyena yaduktaṃ tadeva viśadayati |
^divi dyuloke ^yadu ^draviṇaṃ yadeva dhanaṃ ^yat ca ^pṛthivyāṃ dhanaṃ tasya sarvasya tvamīśvaro bhavasi |
‘yajñārthaṃ dravyamutpannam’ iti smaraṇāt |
agnivyatirekeṇa yajñāsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ ||
1. ta-agnihotrārthā |
2. ta-vārṭā |
3. ta-asya |
kiṃ naṷ a॒sya dravi̭ṇa॒ṃ kadra॒tnaṃ vi naṷ voco jātavedaścikitvān |
guhā̭dhvanaḥ para॒maṃ yatpa̭ro a॒sya reku॒ pada॒ṃ na ni॒dānā̭ a॒ganma ||
12 ||
kim | naḥ | a॒sya | dravi̭ṇam | kat | ha | ra॒tnam | vi | naḥ | vocaḥ | jāta̭'vedaḥ | ci॒ki॒tvān | guhā̭ | a॒dhvanaḥ | pa॒ra॒mam | yat | naḥ | a॒sya | rekṷ | pa॒dam | na | ni॒dānā̭ | a॒ganma || 12 ||
pūrvamantreṇa yasya̭ dhanasya̭ a॒sminsvāmi॒tvamṷktam asya dhanasya̭ kiṃ draviṇaṃ kiṃ sādhanabhūtaṃ naḥ asmabhya̭ṃ dātavyaṃ dhanamasti |
kadra ratnaṃ kiṃ ca ramaṇīyaṃ hitakaraṃ dhanamasti |

yadvā |
kiṃ dātavyaṃ draviṇamasti |
asya draviṇarāśeḥ kadranamiti vā yojyam |

vayaṃ reku riktaṃ padaṃ gantavyaṃ sthānaṃ gṛhādikaṃ nidānā anyairnindyamānāḥ ||
karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ ||
na aganma na prāpumaḥ ||
kā ma॒ryādā̭ va॒yunā̭ kadra॒ vāma̭ccha॒ gamḙma ra॒ghavo॒ na vā॒jam |
kadā̭ naṷ de॒vīrmṛ̭tasya patna॒ḥ sūro̭ va॒rṇana॒ tata̭nu॒ṣārḥ ||
13 ||
kā | ma॒ryādā̭ | va॒yunā̭ | kat | ha | vā॒mam | a॒ccha | gamḙma | ra॒ghvaḥ | na | vā॒jam | kadā̭ | naḥ | de॒vīḥ | a॒mṛta̭sya | patnī̭ḥ | sūro̭ | va॒rṇana | tata̭nu॒ṣārḥ | u॒ṣasa̭ḥ || 13 ||
kā maryādā kā prītyādivyavasthā kā ca vayunā prajñānaṃ padārthaviṣayaṃ kadra kiṃ ca vāmaṃ vananīyaṃ padārthajātam |
etatsarvam accha gamema abhigacchema |

raghvo na vājam |
śīghragāmino'śvādayaḥ saṃgrāmamiva |

kadā kasmin kāle naḥ asmākaṃ vyavahārāya devīḥ devyo dyotamānāḥ amṛtasya amaradharmasyādityasya patnīḥ patnyaḥ pālayiṣyaḥ sūraḥ prasaviṣyaḥ uṣāsaḥ uṣasaḥ varṇana prakāśena tatanu vistārayeyuḥ |
evaṃ sūryasambandhana uṣasaḥ stutyā sarvātmano vaiśvānarasya stutiravagantavyā ||

dvirbhāvahalādiśeṣau ||

a॒dhā tḙ a॒gne kimi̭ha va॒danta̭nāyu॒dhāsa̭ ā॒satā̭ sa॒cantām ||
14 ||
a॒nirḙṇa | va॒rcasā̭ | phu॒lvena̭ | pra॒tītyḙna | ku॒dhunā̭ | a॒ta॒pāsa̭ḥ | a॒dha | tḙ | a॒gne | kim | i॒ha | va॒danti̭ | a॒nāyṷdhāsaḥ | a॒satā̭ | sa॒cantām || 14 ||

^anireṣa |
irā annaṃ tadrahitena |
^phalavena tucchena ^pratīyena pratigantavyena |
ārohaṇetyarthaḥ |
^kṛdhunā hasvena |
kṛdhivati hasvanāma ' kṛdhuko vamrakaḥ ' ( ni. 2. 2. 6 ) iti tannāmasūttvāt |
evaṃvidhena ^vacasā ^anupāsaḥ anusā janāḥ ^adha adhunā ^iha asmin loke he ^agne ^te tvāṃ ^kiṃ ^vadanti |

havirādisādhanarahitāste ^asatā duḥkhena ^saccantāṃ saṃgacchantām ||

^samidhānasya samidhyamānasya^ |
karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ |
^vṛṣṇaḥ varṣituḥ phalasya ^vasoḥ^ vāsayituḥ ^asya agneḥ ^anīkaṃ tejaḥsaṃghaḥ ^dame yāgagṛhe ^ā ^ruroca sarvato dīpyate |
kimartham |
^śriye śreyase yajamānānām |
so'gniḥ ^ruśat |
varṇanāmratat |

kimiva |
^rāyā aśvādidhanena ^kṣitirna rājādiva |

3 ||
‘ ūrdhva ū pu ṇaḥ ’ ityekādaśacaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
‘ ūrdhva ū pvekā- daśa ’ ityanukāntam |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayorāgneye kratau traiṣṭubhe chandamīdam |
sūtritaṃ ca--‘ ūrdhva ū pu ṇaḥ sasasya yahiyuteti pañca ’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti ||
ūrdhva ū ṣu ṇo adhvarasya hotarge tiṣṭha devatātā yajīyān |
tvaṃ hi viśvamabhyasi manma pra vedhasaścittarasi manīṣām ||
1 ||
ūrdhvaḥ | ū~ iti | su | naḥ | adhvarasya | hotaḥ | agne | tiṣṭha | deva'tātā | yajīyān | tvam | hi | viśvam | abhi | asi | manma | pra | vedhasaḥ | cit | tirasi | manīṣām || 1 ||
he ^adhvarasya ^hotaḥ yāgasaṃbandhināṃ devānāmāhvātaḥ |
yadvā |
hota rityatra prakṛtyaṃśo'nuvādaḥ |
he homaniṣpādaka ^agne ^yajīyān yaṣṭatamastvaṃ ^naḥ asmākam ^ūrdhva ^ū ^ṣu ^tiṣṭha unnata eva tiṣṭha |

^devatātā devatātāau |

cit pūjāyām^ |

1. kha-na-bha-ukthena |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- ‘ samidhyamānasya ’ nāsti |

4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ṛtvigbhyajamānaiḥ |
5. ca-pha-yajñe manma mananīyatvaṃ vedhaso brahmaṇo viśvamabhi ami hi khalu |
devanāyāḥ |
ta-na-bha-vistāryante |
amūroṃ hotā nyasādi viśvarbhirmandro vidatheṣu pracetāḥ | ūrdhvaṃ bhānuṃ savitevāśrinetaiva dhūmaṃ stabhāyaduṣa dyām || 2 || amūraḥ | hotā | ni | asādi | viśva | agniḥ | mandraḥ | vidatheṣu | pra'cetāḥ | ūrdhvam | bhānum | savitā'iva | aśret | metā'iva | dhūmam | stabhāyat | upa | dyām || 2 ||
^amūraḥ amūḍhaḥ |
pragalbha ityarthaḥ |
^hotā homaniṣpādakaḥ ^mandraḥ madanīyaḥ ^mādayitā vā ^pracetāḥ prakṛṣṭajñānaḥ ^agniḥ devaḥ ^viśva ṛtvigpāsu ^prajāsu madhye ^vidatheṣu yāgeṣu nimittabhūteṣu ^nyasādi nitarāṃ sthāpitaḥ |
atha tathābhūto'gniḥ ^bhānuṃ dīptiṃ ^saviteva uditaḥ sūrya iva ^ūrdhvam ^aśret ūrdhvamukhamāśrayati |
kiṃca ^meteva syūṇeva |
sā yathā svādhiṣṭhitaṃ ^vaṃśādikaṃ stabhnāti tadvadayamagniḥ ^dhūmaṃ svosthitaṃ ^dyām ^upa dyulokasyopari ^stabhāyat stabhnāti ||
yatā sujurṇī rātanīṃ ghṛtācīṃ pradakṣiṇedevatātimurāṇaḥ | udu svarurvajā nākraḥ paśvo anakti sudhitaḥ sumekaḥ || 3 || yatā | su'jurṇiḥ | rātanīṃ | ghṛtācīṃ | pra'dakṣiṇita | deva'tātim | urāṇaḥ | ut | ū~ iti | svaruḥ | nava'jāḥ | na | akraḥ | paśvaḥ | anakti | sudhitaḥ | su'mekaḥ || 3 ||
^yatā saṃyatā ^sujurṇiḥ śobhanajavā suṣṭhu jīrṇā purāṇī vā ^ghṛtācī |
ghṛtamañcatīti ghṛtācī juhūḥ |

havirlakṣaṇadhanavatī |

stīrṇe barhiṣi samidhāne agnā ūrdhvo adhvaryurjujuṣāṇo asthāt | paryubhiḥ paśupā na hotā triviṣṭyaiti pradivaṃ urāṇaḥ || 4 || stīrṇe | barhiṣi | sam'idhāne | agnau | ūrdhvaḥ | adhvaryuḥ | jujuṣāṇaḥ | asthāt | pari | agniḥ | paśu'pāḥ | na | hotā | tri'viṣṭyai | eti | pra'divaṃ | urāṇaḥ || 4 ||

6. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-bha-la-abhavat |
vbarhiṣi vedyāṃ vstīrṇe āstṛte sati vagnau āhavanīyādike vsamidhāne samyagdhidhyamāne ca mati vadhvaryuḥ adhvarasya netā vjujuṣāṇaḥ devān prīṇayan vūrdhvaḥ vasthāt tiṣṭhati |

purāṇāmaitat |

trirhi paryagniḥ kriyate ||

uktamevārthaṃ spaṣṭayati |

he vsvanīka śobhanajvāla vagne vghorasya bhītijanakasyāpi vsataḥ vviṣuṇasya viśvak sarvato vyāsasya vte tava vcāruḥ ramaṇīyā vbhadrā stutyā kalyāṇī vā mūrtiḥ vsaṃhak saṃdṛṣṭiḥ |
samyak dṛṣṭyā bhavatītyarthaḥ |

na kurvanti |

1 ||
ayam | iha | prathamaḥ | dhāyi | dhātṛbhiḥ | hotā | yajiṣṭhaḥ | adhvareṣu | īḍyaḥ | yam | ardhavānaḥ | bhṛgavaḥ | virurucuḥ | citram | vimbam | viśeṣviśe || 1 ||
dhātubhiḥ yajñārthaṃ karma kurvadbhiradhvaryubhiḥ iha asmin yajñe hotā devānāmāhvātā yajiṣṭhaḥ atiśayena yaṣṭā adhvareṣu yāgeṣu īḍyaḥ ṛtvigbhiḥ stūyamānaḥ prathamaḥ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ mukhyaḥ ayam āhavanīyādisthāneṣu pratyakṣopalabhyamāno'gniḥ dhāyi adhāyi nihitaḥ ||

1. kha-ga-ca-na-bha-la-sa-yajamānaṃ dūtam |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-sa-agne pāvaka dūtaṃ va iti sūkte iti; na-pra yadvastriṣṭubhaṃ dūtaṃ va ityādimārutamiti |

vdame yajñagṛhe vṛtāyate ṛtaṃ yajñamicchate yajamānāya vpriyāṇi vcit abhīṣṭānyapi vvasu vasūni paścādīnair dhanāni vdāti dadāti prayacchati ||

'iṅ gatau' |

vpuruṣaspṛhaḥ: ṛtvigādibhirabhilapyamānāḥ vrāyaḥ: paścādidhanāni vasme asmāsu yajamāneṣu vdvivedine pratidinaṃ vsaṃ vcarantu pracarantyo bhavantu ||

‘agne mṛḻa’ ityaṣṭakaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ gāyatrīcchandaskamagnidevatākam |

agne mṛḻa mahā~ asi ya īmā de॒vyuṃ ja̭nam | iyḙtha ba॒rhiḥsada̭m || 1 || agne | mṛḻa | ma॒hān | a॒si | yaḥ | ī॒mā | de॒vyum | ja॒nam | iyḙtha | ba॒rhiḥ | sada̭m || 1 ||
he vagne vmṛḻa asmān sukhaya |

sa mānṷṣīṣu॒ dukṣṷ pra॒vīrma̭rtyaḥ | dū॒to vi॒śveṣā̭ṃ bhuvat || 2 || saḥ | mānṷṣīṣu | du॒ḥdabha̭ḥ | vi॒kṣu | pra॒vīḥ | ma॒rtyaḥ | dū॒taḥ | vi॒śvḙṣām | bhuvat || 2 ||
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-pha-bha-kṣiprāyeva; na-kṣiprameva |
2. sa-gāyatramāmeyam |

sūktaviniyogo laikṣikaḥ ! |

tathā ca sūtram |

ᵛdūtabha: durdabho rākṣasādinā durdaihasa: ᵛmānuṣīṣu manusambandhinīṣu ᵛviśva prajāsu ᵛprāvī: prakarṣeṇa gantā ᵛamartya: amaradharmā ᵛsa: agni: ᵛviśveṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ ᵛdūta: ᵛbhuvat bhavattu ||
sa sa॒tra pari̭ ṇīyate hotā̭ ma॒ndro di॒viṣṭi̭ṣu | u॒ta potā̭ ni ṣīdati || 3 || sa: | sa॒tra | pari̭ | nīyate | hotā̭ | ma॒ndra: | di॒viṣṭi̭ṣu | u॒ta | potā̭ | ni | sīdati || 3 || ᵛsa: agni: ᵛsatra yajñagṛhaṃ ᵛpari ṇīyate ṛtvigbhi: parito nīyate | nīyamāna: san ᵛdivi-ṣṭiṣu yāgeṣu ᵛmandra: madanīya: stutya: so'gni: ᵛhotā ca bhavati | ᵛuta api ca ᵛpotā san ᵛni ᵛṣīdati yajñagṛhe upaviśati ||
u॒ta grā a॒gnira̭dhva॒ra u॒to gṛ॒hapa̭ti॒rdamḙ | u॒ta bra॒hmā ni ṣīdati || 4 || u॒ta | grā: | a॒gni: | a॒dhva॒re | u॒to iti̭ | gṛ॒hapa̭ti: | damḙ | u॒ta | bra॒hmā | ni | sīdati || 4 || ᵛuta api ca ᵛagni: ᵛadhvare yāge ᵛgrā: devapatnīryajati | yadvā bhavatītyartha: | yadvā | grā gacchan adhvaryurbhavati | ᵛuto api ca so'gni: ᵛdame yajñagṛhe ᵛgṛhapati: yajamānaśca bhavati | ᵛuta api ca ᵛbrahmā brāhmaṇācchaṃsī san ᵛni ᵛṣīdati upaviśati ||

ᵛupavaktā adhvaryuprabhṛtīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāmanujñārthaṃ ‘māṃ praṇaya’ ityādirūpasya vākyasya vaktā man brahmā cāsi |

' agne tamadya ' ityetad daśamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣam āgneyam |
tathā cānukramaṇikā— ' agne tamadya padapāṅkaṃ ' pañcamī mahāpadapaṅktiranyoṣṇi k caturthī ṣaṭpanthānyā vā saptamyāḥ pañcakau mukhyau tṛtīyaḥ saptako navakaścāṣṭamyāḥ pañcakaḥ pādaścatuṣkaḥ saptakaścaiṣambhraśca ' iti |
idaṃ kṛtsnamapi sūktaṃ padapaṅkticchandaskaṃ ' pañcakāścatvāraḥ ṣaṭkaścaikāścaturthaścatuṣko vā padapaṅktiḥ ' ityukta- lakṣaṇopetatvāt |
pañcamī ' tava svādiṣṭhā ' ityeṣā mahāpadapaṅktiḥ |

anyā ' śivā naḥ satyā ' ityeṣā''dyuṣṇicchandaskā |

daśame'hani idaṃ sūktamājyaśastram |

1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-pa-la-sa-yajamānasādhanam | 2. ta-la-hirādeśaḥ | 3. va-prakṛteḥ śrubhāvaśca | 4. na-la-sa-' te ' nāsti | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ta-na-bha-duhisaḥ | 6. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-la-sa-' āgneyaṃ ' nāsti | 7. ta-padapaṃkti | 8. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa-ityuktalakṣaṇatvāt |
agnḙ ta॒madyā̭śvaṃ na stomai̭: kratuṃ na bha॒draṃ hṛ̭di॒spṛśa̭m | ṛ॒dhyāmā̭ ta॒ ohai̭: ||1|| agnḙ | ta॒m | adya̭ | a॒śvam | na | stomai̭: | kratṷm | na | bha॒dram | hṛ̭di॒spṛśa̭m | ṛ॒dhyāma̭ | te | ohai̭: || 1 ||
ha vagne vadya asminnahani vayamṛtvigādayaḥ vohaiḥ indrādiprāpakaiḥ vstomaiḥ stotrasamūhaiḥ vtaṃ vprasiddhaṃ vte vtvām vṛdhyāma samarthayāmaḥ |

adhā̭ hya॒gne kra॒torbha॒drasya॒ dakṣa̭sya sādho̭: | ra॒thīṛ॒tasya̭ bṛ॒hato̭ ba॒bhūtha̭ || 2 || adha | hi | agnḙ | kra॒toḥ | bha॒drasya̭ | dakṣa̭sya | sādho̭: | ra॒thīḥ | ṛ॒tasya̭ | bṛ॒hata̭ḥ | ba॒bhūtha̭ ||2||

‘pratyamṛṣasaḥ’ iti pañca ca caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevayārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyaṃ māntravarṇikadaivataṃ vā |

viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||

ā nāsatyorugāyā rathenemaṃ yajñamupa no yātamuccha ||
1 ||
prati | agniḥ | uṣasaḥ | jātavedāḥ | aruvyat | devaḥ | rocamānāḥ | mahaḥ'bhiḥ | ā | nāsatyā | uru'gāyā | rathena | imam | yajñam | upa | naḥ | yātam | accha || 1 ||
ᵥdevaḥ dyotamānaḥ ᵥjātavedāḥ jātadhanaḥ ᵥagniḥ ᵥmahobhiḥ tejobhiḥ ᵥrocamānāḥ dīpyamānāḥ ᵥuṣasaḥ ᵥprati upaso lakṣīkṛtya ᵥasyat prabuddho bhavati |
ᵥurugāyā prabhūtagamanau he ᵥnāsatyā aśvinau ᵥrathena ᵥnaḥ asmadīyam ᵥimaṃ ᵥyajñam ᵥaccha abhimukham ᵥupa ᵥyātam upagacchatam ||
1. ṇa-tantuṃ rajjum | 2. gha-ca-la-arvācīnam | 3. kha-ga-gha-ta-pa-bha-la-sa-atruvan; ca- ṇa-ja-pha-akṛṇvan | 4. ba-pha-ūrdhvamukhaḥ | 5. ca-pha-skandhaḥ | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-ṛtena |
ūrdhvaṃ ketuṃ savitā devo aśrejjyotirviśvasmai bhuvanāya kṛṇvan |
ā prā dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣaṃ vi sūryo raśmibhirjekitānaḥ ||
2 ||
ūrdhvam | ketum | savitā | devaḥ | aśret | jyotiḥ | viśvasmai | bhuvanāya | kṛṇvan | ā | prāḥ | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | antarikṣam | vi | sūryaḥ | raśmibhiḥ | jekitānaḥ || 2 ||
viśvasmai sarvasmai bhuvanāya lokāya jyotiḥ tejaḥ kṛṇvan kurvan savitā prerakaḥ devaḥ ūrdhvam unmukhaṃ ketuṃ prakāśakaṃ bhānum aśret āśrayati |
vi cekitānaḥ sarvaṃ viśeṣeṇa paśyan sūryaḥ raśmibhiḥ svakīyaiḥ kiraṇaiḥ dyāvāpṛthivī dyulokabhūlokau antarikṣaṃ ca ā prāḥ samantādapūrayat ||
āvahantyaruṇījyotiṣāgmahī citrā raśmibhirjekitānā |
prabodhayantī suvitārya devyuṣā īyate suyujā rathena ||
3 ||
āvahantī | aruṇī | jyotiṣā | ā | agāt | mahī | citrā | raśmibhiḥ | jekitānā | prabodhayantī | suvitārya | devī | uṣāḥ | īyate | suyujā | rathena || 3 ||
āvahantī dhanāni dhārayantī aruṇī aruṇavarṇāḥ ||
prathamaikavacane dvitīyābahuvacanaṃ chāndasam ||
jyotiṣā tejasā yuktā mahī mahatī raśmibhiḥ kiraṇaiḥ citrā darśanīyā jekitānā jānatī uṣāḥ āgāt āgacchat |
kiṃca prabodhayantī susān prabuddhān kurvatī devī dyotamānā uṣāḥ suyujā śobhanayukena rathena suvitāya sukhaprāptaye īyate gacchati ||
ā vāṃ vahiṣṭhā iha te vahantu rathā aśvāsa upaso vyuṣṭau |
ime hi vāṃ madhupeyāya somā asmin‌yajñe vṛṣaṇā mādayethām ||
4 ||
ā | vām | vahiṣṭhāḥ | iha | te | vahantu | rathāḥ | aśvāsaḥ | upasaḥ | vyuṣṭau | ime | hi | vām | madhupeyāya | somāḥ | asmin | yajñe | vṛṣaṇā | mādayethām || 4 ||
he aśvinau iha yajñe vahiṣṭhāḥ voḍhatamāḥ te prasiddhāḥ rathāḥ raṃhaṇā gantāraḥ aśvāsaḥ aśvāḥ vāṃ yuvām upasaḥ upodevatāyāḥ vyuṣṭau vyuṣṭanakāle vā vahantu samantāt nayantu |
hi yasmāt kāraṇāt he vṛṣaṇā vṛṣaṇau kāmāનાં vardhitārau he aśvinau ime somāḥ vāṃ yuvāṃ madhupeyāya madhoḥ somasya pānāya asmin‌yajñe mādayethāṃ harṣayethām ||
1. kha-ga-ca-na-pha-ma-sa-sanmukhaṃ |
2. la-bhūlokau ca |
3. ṇa-vyuṣṭikāle |

yajamānā: ^arvantaṃ ^na śīghragāminamaśvamiva ^sānusiṃ saṃbhajanīyaṃ ^diva: ^śiśuṃ ^na dyulokasya putrabhūtaṃ sūryamiva ^aruṣam ārocamānaṃ ^tam agniṃ ^divedive pratidinaṃ ^mṛjyante bhṛśaṃ paricaraṇaṃ kurvanti ||

1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-°ayīyaja: yajñe | 2. pa-dambhaka: | 3. la-bhanena | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bhajanīyam | 5. kha-ga-ca-kurviran |
ᵛsāhadevyaḥ sahadevanāmno rājñaḥ putraḥ ᵛkumāraḥ somakābhidho rājā ᵛharibhyām aśvābhyām aśvaṃ dātuṃ ᵛmā māṃ ᵛyat yadā ᵛbodhat bodhayāmāsa imau tavāśvāviti |
tadā aham ᵛaccha abhimukhyena kumāreṇa ᵛhūtaḥ? san ᵛna ᵛudaraṃ tāvathāvalabdhvā na nirgatavānasmi ||
uta tyā ya̭jatā harī̭ kumā॒rātsā̭hade॒vyāt |
pra̭yatā sa॒dha ā̭ de॒de ||
8 ||
uta | tyā | ya̭jatā | harī̭ iti | kumā॒rāt | sā̭hade॒vyāt | pra̭yatā | sa॒dhaḥ | ā | de॒de ||8||
ᵛuta api ca ᵛsāhadevyāt sahadevasya putrāt ᵛkumārāt somakākhyāt2 rājñaḥ ᵛyajatā pūjanīyau3 ᵛprayatā prayatau ᵛthyā ryau tau prasiddhau ᵛharī aśvau ᵛsadhaḥ yasmin dine tenāhūtastasminneva dine ᵛā ᵛdede ādastavānasmi |
mantradraṣṭā ṛṣiḥ 'bodhat' ityagdayena kumāramabhepsayābhyupagamya aśvau etasmāt4 labdhvā prīto'bhavadityarthaḥ ||
eṣa vā̭ṃ de॒vāva̭śvinā kumā॒raḥ sā̭hade॒vyaḥ |
dī॒rghāyṷrastu soma̭kaḥ ||
9 ||
eṣaḥ | vām | de॒vau | a॒śvinā̭ | kumā॒raḥ | sā̭hade॒vyaḥ | dī॒rgha'ā̭yuḥ | a॒stu | soma̭kaḥ ||9||
ᵛdevau dyotamānau he ᵛaśvinā aśvinau ᵛvāṃ yuvāṃ tarpakaḥ ᵛsāhadevyaḥ sahadevasya putraḥ ᵛkumāraḥ ᵛsomakaḥ somakābhidhānaḥ ᵛeṣaḥ rājā ᵛdīrghāpurastu śatāyuṣko bhavatu |
'etamu haiva procatuḥ parvatanādau somakāya' ityādinā 'sarve haiva mahārājā āsuḥ' (ai.brā. 7.34) ityanena brāhmaṇena somakaraya rājatvaṃ spaṣṭīkṛtam ||
taṃ yu॒vaṃ dḙvāvaśvinā kumā॒raṃ sā̭hade॒vyam |
dī॒rghāyṷṣaṃ kṛ॒ṇota॒na ||
10 ||

he ᵛdevāvaśvinā dyotamānāśvinau ᵛyuvaṃ yuvāṃ ᵛtaṃ ᵛsāhadevyaṃ sahadevasya putraṃ ᵛkumāraṃ somakākhyaṃ rājānaṃ ᵛdīrghāyuṣaṃ śatāyuṣkaṃ ᵛkṛṇotana kurutam |

'ā satyo yātu' ityekaviṃśatyṛcaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
āgneyamanukramaṇikā- 'ā satyaḥ saikendram' iti |

ṛgveda: [ a. 3. a. 5. 1. 17 ]
ā sṛtyo yātu maghavaॉ ṛjīṣī dravantvasya hara̭ya upa naḥ |
tasmā idandhaḥ suṣamā sudakṣamihābhipitvaṃ karate gṛṇānaḥ ||
1 ||
ā | satyaḥ | yātu | magha'vān | ṛjīṣī | dravantu | asya | hara̭yaḥ | upa | naḥ | tasmai | it | andhaḥ | suṣam | sudakṣam | iha | abhi'pitvam | karate | gṛṇānaḥ || 1 ||
1 ṛjīṣī |
ṛjīpaśabdena niṣpiṣṭo vigatasāraḥ somo'bhidhīyate |
tadvān satyaḥ satyavān maghavān dhanavānindraḥ naḥ asmākam upa samīpe ā yātu āgacchatu |
kiṃca asya indrasya harayaḥ aśvāḥ no'smākam upa samīpe dravantu āgacchantu |
vayaṃ yajamānāḥ iha asmin yajñe tasmai indrāya sudakṣaṃ śobhanabalam |
sāropetamityarthaḥ |
it idaṃ pratyakṣeṇopalabhyamānam andhaḥ annarūpaṃ somaṃ suṣuma abhiṣutaṃ kuryāma |
gṛṇānaḥ asmābhiḥ stūyamāna indraḥ abhipitvam asmadabhiprāpti karate karotu ||
avaṃ sya śurādhva̭no nānte'sminbhoṃ adya sarvane mandadhyai |
śaṃsātyukthamuśaneva vedhāścikituṣe asuryāya manma ||
2 ||
avaṃ | sya | śūra | adhvanaḥ | na | ante | asmin | naḥ | adha | sarvane | mandadhyai | śaṃsāti | uktham | uśanā'iva | vedhāḥ | cikituṣe | asuryāya | manma || 2 ||
ha śūra śatrūṇām abhibhāvasamarthendra atha idānīm asmin savane mādhyaṃdinākhye mandadhyai tvāṃ mādayituṃ naḥ asmān ava sya vimuñca |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
adhvanaḥ gantavyasya mārgasya ante samīpe na2 aśrānmanuṣyā yathā2 muñcanti tadvat |
uśaneva kāvya iva vedhāḥ śaṃsitā yajamāno ha indra cikituṣe sarva jānate asuryāya asurāṇāṃ hiṃsakāya tubhya manma mananīyam ukthaṃ śastrarūpaṃ stotraṃ śaṃsāti śaṃsanaṃ karoti ||
kuvirna niṇyaṃ vidathāni sādhanvaṣā yatsekaṃ vipipānā arcāt |
diva itthā jījanatsapta kārūnahā ciccakurvyunā gṛṇantaḥ ||
3 ||
kuviḥ | na | niṇyam | vidathāni | sādhan | vṛṣā | yat | sekam | vi'pipānaḥ | arcāt | divaḥ | itthā | jījanat | sapta | kārūn | ahā | cit | cakruḥ | vayunā | gṛṇantaḥ ||3||
kaviḥ medhāvī niṇyaṃ na |
niṇyamisyantarhitanāmaitat |
antarhitaṃ gūḍhamarthamiva vidathāni veda- nīyāni kāryajātāni sādhan sādhayan vṛṣā kāmānāṃ varṣitendraḥ yat yadā sekaṃ sevanīyaṃ somaṃ
1. ṇa-aṃbhabhavatasamarthendra |
2. kha-ga-ṇa-na-bha-manuṣyā yathā aśvān; gha-manuṣyā yathā aśvān; ca-manuṣyā ayathā aśvān; ta-la-aśvān manuṣyā yathā aśvān; na-aśvān manuṣyā yathā |
3. ṇa- karmajātāni |
vipipānaḥ ayathaṃ piban vaarthāt viśeṣeṇārcita |
somapāne śraddhāṃ karotītyarthaḥ |
tadānīṃ vadivaḥ amuṣmāt yulokāt vaitthā satyameva vasasa sasasaṃkhyākān vakārūn raśmīn vajījanata arjājanata |
janayati |
vagṛṇantaḥ stūyamānā raśmayaḥ vavayunā vayunāni manuṣyāṇāṃ jñānāni vaahvā vacita vadivasenāpi vacakruḥ kṛtavantaḥ ||
svaryededaṃ sudṛśīkamarkairmahi jyotīṃ rurucurdva vastauḥ |
andhā tamā~si dudhitā vicakṣe nṛbhyaścakāra nṛtamo abhiṣṭau ||
4 ||
svaḥ | yat | vedaṃ | sudṛśīkam | arkaiḥ | mahi | jyotiḥ | rurucuḥ | yat | ha | vastauḥ | andhā | tamā~si | dudhitā | vi'cakṣe | nṛbhyaḥ | cakāra | nṛ'tamaḥ | abhiṣṭau || 4 ||
yadva yadā khalu vamahi mahat prabhūtaṃ vajyotiḥ tejorūpaṃ vayat vasvaḥ yo yulokaḥ vaarkaiḥ raśmibhiḥ vasudṛśīkaṃ suṣṭhu darśanīyaṃ vaveda avedi jñāyate tadānīṃ devāstasmin svarge vavastauḥ nivāsāthaṃ varurucuḥ dīptiyuktā babhūvuḥ |
vanṛtamaḥ netṛtamaḥ sūryaḥ |
vaabhiṣṭau abhyāgamane vanṛbhyaḥ nṛṇāṃ vavicakṣe viśeṣeṇa darśanāya vaandhā andhakārarūpāṇi nividāni vatamā~si vadudhitā dudhitāni nāśitāni vacakāra kṛtavān ||
vavakṣa indro amitamṛjīpyubhe ā paprau rodasī mahitvā |
ataścidasya mahimā vi reci abhi yaḥ viśvā bhuvanā babhūva ||
5 ||
vavakṣe | indraḥ | amitam | ṛjīṣī | ubhe iti | ā | paprau | rodasī iti | mahi'tvā | ataḥ | cit | asya | mahimā | vi | reci | abhi | yaḥ | viśvā | bhuvanā | babhūva || 5 ||
vaṛjīrpā |
ṛjīpaśabdena niṣpiṣṭo vigatasāraḥ somo'bhidhīyate |
tadvān vaindraḥ parameśvarayuktaḥ vaamitam iyattārāhitaṃ mahimānaṃ vavavakṣe uvāha |
kiṃca vamahitvā mahattvena svena mahimnā vaubhe varodasī ubhe dyāvāpṛthivyau vaā vapaprau samantādapūrayat |
vayaḥ indraḥ vaviśvā vabhuvanā sarvāṇi bhuvanāni vaabhi vababhūva tiraścakāra |
vaataścit ebhyo'pi viśvebhyo bhuvanebhyaḥ vaasya indrasya vamahimā vavi vareci atirirece |
adhiko babhūvetyarthaḥ ||
17 ||
viśvāni śakro naryāṇi vidvānapo rireca sakhibhirnikāmaiḥ |
aśmānaṃ cid bibhidurvarcobhirgomantamuśijo vi vavruḥ ||
6 ||
viśvāni | śakraḥ | naryāṇi | vidvān | apaḥ | rireca | sakhibhiḥ | nikāmaiḥ | aśmānam | cit | bibhiduḥ | varcaḥ'bhiḥ | gomantam | uśijaḥ | vi | vavruḥ | vavruḥ iti | || 6 ||
1. kha-ga-na-ṇa-na-bha-sa-lokāt |
viśvāni samastāni naryāṇi manuṣyāṇāṃ hitāni vṛtrādīni kāryāṇi vidvān jānan śakraḥ samartha indraḥ nikāmaiḥ nikāmayamānebhyaḥ sakhibhiḥ mitrabhūtabhyo marudbhiḥ ||
dhyāyayena caturthyarthe tṛtīyā ||
apaḥ vṛṣṭilakṣaṇānyudakāni rireca riktīkāra |
indraḥ sasyādiphalasiddhayarthaṃ meghasthānyudakāni marudbhyo dattavānityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
nikāmairnikāmayamānaiḥ sakhibhirmarudbhiḥ sahitaḥ san apo nabhasaḥ sakāśāt |
āpa ityantarikṣanāmaiṭatat |
atra vibhaktavyatyayaḥ |
rireca vyatiriktaḥ2 babhūva |
ye marutaḥ vacobhiḥ vāgrūpairvanibhiḥ ajmānaṃ cit parvatamatapi meghaṃ vā |
asmeti meghanāmaiṭatat |
bibhiduḥ abhaitsuḥ |
uśijaḥ kāmayamānāste marutaḥ gomantaṃ gobhirupetaṃ vrajam aṅgirasāṃ saṃbandha gosthānaṃ vi vamuḥ ācchādayāmāsuḥ ||
apo vṛtraṃ vavrivāṃsaṃ parāṃhnāvartta vajre pṛthivī sacetāḥ |
prāṇīsi samudriāṇyainoḥ patirbhavacchavasā śūra dhṛṣṇo ||
7 ||
apaḥ | vṛtram | vavrivāṃsam | parā | ahan | pra | āvat | te | vajram | pṛthivī | sa'caitāḥ | pra | arṇaṃsi | samudriyāṇi | ainoḥ | patiḥ | bhavan | śavasā | śūra | dhṛṣṇo iti || 7 ||
he indra prāvat rākṣasādibhyaḥ prakaryeṇa pālayitu te tvadīyaṃ vajram apaḥ vṛṣṭi- lakṣaṇānyudakāni vavrivāṃsaṃ āvarakaṃ vṛtraṃ meghaṃ parāhan prakaryeṇa prerayati sma |
pṛthivī bhūmiḥ sacetāḥ cetanāvatī |
tvayā saṃgatā babhūva |
dhṛṣṇo dharṣaṇaśīla he śūra vikrāntendra tvaṃ śavasā balena patirbhavan lokānāṃ pālakaḥ san samudriyāṇi samudrasambandhīni arṇaṃsi nabhasi sthitāni udakāni pra ainoḥ prerakaḥ abhūḥ ||
apo yadadriṃ puruhūta dardvirāvirbhavatsaramā purvyaṃ te |
sa nau netā vājamā darṣi bhūri gotrā rujannaṅgirobhirguṇānaḥ ||
8 ||
apaḥ | yat | adrim | puru'hūta | dardvaḥ | āviḥ | bhuvat | saramā | purvyam | te | saḥ | naḥ | netā | vājam | ā | darṣi | bhūrim | gotrā | rujan | aṅgiraḥ'bhiḥ | guṇānaḥ ||8||
he puruhūta bahubhirāhūtendra yat yadā apaḥ prati vṛṣṭilakṣaṇānyudakāni lakṣīkṛtya adriṃ meghaṃ dardvaḥ vidāritavānasi |
saramā devaśunī purvya purā te tubhyaṃ paṇibhiḥ apahṛtaṃ godhanam āvirbhavat prakāśayāmāsa |
aṅgirobhiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ guṇānaḥ stūyamānaḥ san gotrā gotrāṇyatrāṇi rujan naḥ asmān bhūri prabhūtaṃ vājam annaṃ netā prāpayitā saḥ tvam ā darṣi ādaraṃ kṛtavānasi ||
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-bha-kāmayamānebhyaḥ | 2. ca-pha-riktaḥ | 3. kha-ga-gha-pa-pha-bha-sa-tvadīyaṃ ca | 4. kha-ga-ca-āvārakam | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ta-na-bha-sa-babhūva | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-sa-apahṛtaṃ dhanam; na-sa-āhṛtam |
acchā kṛviṃ nṛmaṇo gā abhiṣṭau svarśatā maghavannādhamānam |
ūtibhistamiṣaṇo yuṣmahūtau ni māyāvānabrahmā dasyurarta ||
9 ||
acchā | kṛvim | nu'manaḥ | gāḥ | abhiṣṭau | svaḥ'sātā | maghavan | nārdhamānam | ūti'bhiḥ | tam | iṣaṇaḥ | yuṣma'hūtau | ni | māyā'vān | abrahmā | dasyuḥ | ārta || 9 ||
he maghavan dhanavannindra nṛmaṇaḥ nṛbhirmanuṣyairmanyate iti nṛmaṇaḥ |
tvaṃ kaviṃ medhāvinaṃ kutsam acchā gāḥ abhyāgāḥ |
kiṃca svarśatā |
svadhanam |
tasya sātau dāne nimitte abhiṣṭau kaverabhigame sati nādhamānaṃ yuddhārthaṃ tvāṃ yācamānaṃ kutsam ūtibhiḥ śatrukṛtopadravaparihārarūpai rakṣaṇaiḥ iṣaṇaḥ preritavānasi |
māyāvān kapaṭavān abrahmā |
ṛtvigbhiḥ kriyamāṇaṃ sarvaṃ karmajātamajñatārūpeṇa jānan ṛtvigviśeṣo brahmaśabdenoca
yāsi kutsena sarathamavasyustodo vātasya haryorīśāna: |
ṛjvā vājaṃ na gadhyaṃ yuyūṣankuviryadahanapāryāya bhūṣāt ||
11 ||
yāsi | kutsena | sa'ratham | avasyuḥ | todaḥ | vātasya | haryoḥ | īśānaḥ | ṛjvā | vājam | na | gadhyam | yuyūṣan | kaviḥ | yat | ahan | pāryāya | bhūṣāt || 11 ||
he indra vavasyuḥ kutsasya rakṣaṇamicchan vchandasi vyatyayaḥ parecchāyāmapi dṛśyate |
vtodaḥ todakaḥ śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsakaḥ vvātasya vāyusadṛśayorvegavatoḥ vharyoḥ aśvayoḥ vīśānaḥ īśvarastvaṃ vkutsena kutsākhyena rājarṣiṇā vsarathaṃ samānarathaṃ vyāsi gacchasi |
vgadhyaṃ grāhyam |
vgadhyaṃ gṛhyate |
vṛjvā vājaṃ na gadhyaṃ yuyūṣannapi nigamo bhavati (niru. 5. 15) iti niruktam |
vvājaṃ vna annamiva vṛjvā ṛjugāminau aśvau vyuyūṣan svakīye rathe yojayan vkaviḥ prājñaḥ kutsaḥ vyat yasmin vahan ahani divase vpāryāya pāraṇāya vāpajjistaraṇāya vbhūṣāt prabhavate |
tasminneva vdine kutsena marathaṃ yāmīti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ |
kutsāya śuṣṇamaśuṣṇaṃ ni barhī: prapitve ahaḥ kuryavaṃ sahasrā |
sadyo dasyūna mṛṇa kutsena pra sūraścakaṃ bṛhatābhīkai ||
12 ||
kutsāya | śuṣṇam | aśuṣṇam | ni | barhīḥ | pra'pitve | ahaḥ | kuryavam | sahasrā | sadyaḥ | dasyūna | pra | mṛṇa | kutsena | pra | sūraḥ | cakram | bṛhatā | abhīkai || 12 ||
‘śuṣṇaṃ piprum’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 103. 8) ityasyāmṛci pratipāditaḥ arthaḥ ‘kutsāya’ ityanena ṛgadvayena spaṣṭīkriyate |
he indra tvaṃ vkutsāya kutsārtham vaśuṣṇam |
śuṣṇamiti sukhanāmetat |
tadhi- tam |
atra dīrghābhāvaḥ |
vśuṣṇaṃ śuṣṇanāmānamasuraṃ vni vbarhīḥ nyabarhīḥ avadhīḥ |
tathā vahaḥ divamasya vprapitvaṃ prakrame pūrvāhe vkuyavaṃ kuyavanāmānamasuraṃ ca hatavānasi |
tathā sa tvaṃ vsahasrā sahasreṇa bahubhiḥ parijanaiḥ saha vsadyaḥ yasmin kāle kuyavamavadhīstasmin kāla eva vkutsena |
kutsa iti vajranāmetat |
kutsa veva vkutsasya vajram |
tena vdasyūna śatrūna vpra vmṛṇa jahi ||
bhūtārthe vloṭ ||
hatavānasi |
tathā vsūraḥ prerakasya sūryasya vcakram āyudham vabhīke samīpe saṃgrāme vā vpra vbṛhatāna prakarṣeṇa chinnavānasi ||
1. ta- ‘śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsakaḥ’ nāsti | 2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-sa- ‘samānaratha’ nāsti | 3. na-preraṇāya | 4. ta-na-sa-asminneva | 5. kha-ga-ṇa-apyarthaḥ | 6. ta- ‘kutsa eva’ nāsti | 7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-la-sa-bhūte'rthe | 8. ta-sa-sthitavānasi; na-rakṣitavānasi |
tvaṃ pipruṃ mṛgayaṃ śuśuvāṃsamṛjirdhane vaidathinārya randhīḥ | paścāttakṛṣṇā ni vapaḥ sahasrākaṃ na puro jarimā vi dardaḥ || 13 || tvam | piprum | mṛgayam | śuśu'vāṃsam | ṛjirdhane | vaidathinārya | randhīḥ | paścāśat | kṛṣṇā | ni | vapaḥ | sahasrā | arkam | na | puraḥ | jarimā | vi | dardariti dardaḥ ||13||
he indra tvaṃ pipru piprunāmānamasuraṃ śuśuvāṃsaṃ pravṛddhaṃ mṛgayaṃ mṛgayanāmānamasuraṃ ca hatavānasi kiṃca sa tvaṃ vaidathinārya vidathinaḥ putrāya ṛjiśdhane ṛjiśdhanānne rājñe randhīḥ vaśamanayaḥ |
pañcāśat pañcāśatsaṃkhyākāni sahasrā sahasrāṇi kṛṣṇā kṛṣṇavarṇāni rakṣāṃsi ni vapaḥ nyavapaḥ avadhīḥ |
tathā sa tvaṃ jarimā jarā arkaṃ na vayoviśeṣaṃ rūpamiva puraḥ śambarasya nagarāṇi vi dardaḥ vidāritavānasi ||
sūra upāke tanvaṃ dadhāno vi yatte cetyamṛtasya varpaḥ | mṛgo na hastī tarviṣīmuṣāṇaḥ siṃho na bhīma āyudhāni bibhrat || 14 || sūraḥ | upāke | tanvam | dadhānaḥ | vi | yat | te | ceti | amṛtasya | varpaḥ | mṛgaḥ | na | hastī | tarviṣīm | uṣāṇaḥ | siṃhaḥ | na | bhīmaḥ | āyudhāni | bibhrat ||14||
he indra sūraḥ prerakasya sūryasya upāke samīpe tanvaṃ tvadīyaṃ śarīraṃ yat yadā dadhānaḥ dhārako'bhavaḥ tadā amṛtasya maraṇarahitasya te tvadīyaṃ varpaḥ rūpaṃ vi ceti vyaceti |
he indra jñātṛbhirviśeṣeṇa ajñātaḥ |
sūryasya samīpe sarvāṇi rūpāṇi malinībhavanti na tathendrasya rūpaṃ kiṃtu sūryasya samīpe indrasya rūpaṃ viśeṣeṇa bhāsamānaṃ babhūvetyarthaḥ |
api ca he indra hastī mṛgaḥ na gajaviśeṣo mṛga iva tarviṣīṃ pareṣāṃ balam uṣāṇaḥ dahan āyudhāni vajrādīni śastrāṇi bibhrat bibhrāṇastvaṃ siṃho na siṃha iva bhīmaḥ bhayaṃkaro'bhūḥ ||
indraṃ kāmā vasayanto agamantsarvamīḻhe na sarvane cakānāḥ | śravasyavaḥ śaśamānāsa ukthairoko na raṇvā sudaśīṃva puṣṭiḥ || 15 || indram | kāmāḥ | vasu'yantaḥ | agaman | svaḥ'mīḻhe | na | sarvane | cakānāḥ | śravasyavaḥ | śaśamānāsaḥ | ukthaiḥ | okaḥ | na | raṇvā | sudaśīṃ'iva | puṣṭiḥ ||15||

^ya: indra: ^nayāṃ nayāṃṇi manuṣyahitāni ^purūṇi bahūni ^tā tāni prasiddhāni karmāṇi ^cakāra akarot |

ha ᵥindra ᵥnu kṣipraṃ ᵥstutaḥ pūrvaiṛṣibhiḥ saṃstutaḥ ᵥnu kṣipraṃ ᵥgṛṇānaḥ asmābhiḥ stūyamānaśca ᵥtvam ᵥiṣam annaṃ ᵥjaritre stotre mahyaṃ ᵥnadyo ᵥna agnirnadīriva ᵥpīpeḥ pyāyaya pravṛddhaṃ kuru |
he ᵥharivaḥ harivan harisaṃjñakāśvopetendra ᵥte tubhyaṃ ᵥnavyaṃ navataraṃ ᵥbrahma stotram ᵥakāri asmābhiḥ kriyate |
ᵥrathyaḥ rathavanto vayaṃ ᵥdhiyā prajñārūpayā stutyā ᵥsadāsāḥ tvāṃ sarvadā bhajamānāstvadartha havīrūpasya annasya 2 dātāro vā ᵥsyāma bhūyāsma ||

‘ tvaṃ mahā~ indra tubhyam ’ ityekaviṃśatyṛcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣam 2 |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—‘ tvaṃ mahā~ asikanyāmekapadā ’ iti |
‘ asikanyāṃ yajamāno na hotā ’ ityekapadā virāḍ śiṣṭā viṃśatiḥ triṣṭubhaḥ |
indro devatā |
samūḻahe daśarātre'ṣṭame'hani niṣkevalye idaṃ sūktaṃ viniyuktam 4 |
tathā ca sūtritaṃ— ‘ tvaṃ mahā~ indra tubhyamiti niṣkevalyam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 7 ) iti |
indrasya vṛtraghnaḥ ‘ tvaṃ mahā~ ’ iti paśupuroḍāśasyānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ tvaṃ mahā~ indra tubhyaṃ ha kṣāḥ satrāṇam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 3. 8 ) iti ||
tvaṃ mahā~ indra tubhyaṃ ha kṣā anu kṣatraṃ maṃhanā manyat dyauḥ |

1 ||

he ᵥindra ᵥsabādhaḥ bādhāsahitāḥ ᵥvayaṃ hi vayaṃ khalu ᵥte tvām ᵥā ᵥcakrama āhvayāmaḥ |
kiṃ kurvantaḥ |
ᵥābhiḥ pratyakṣeṇopalabhyamānaiḥ ᵥśamībhiḥ stutirūpaiḥ karmabhiḥ ᵥmahayantaḥ tvāṃ ᵥpūjayantaḥ ||
indrasya vṛtraghnaḥ puroḍāśasya 'stuta indraḥ' iti yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'stuta indro madhavā yad vṛtraivā vasva indraḥ satyaḥ samrāṭ' ( ābha. śrau. 3. 8 ) iti ||
stuta indro madhavā yad vṛtrā bhūrīṇyekau apratīni hanti |
asya priyo jaritā yasya śarmakirdevā vāryante na martāḥ ||
19 ||
stutaḥ | indraḥ | madha'vā | yat | ha | vṛtrā | bhūrīṇi | ekaḥ | apratīni | hanti |
asya | priyaḥ | jaritā | yasya | śarman | nakiḥ | devāḥ | vāryante | na | martāḥ || 19 ||
ᵥyad yadā khalu ᵥmadhavā dhanavān ᵥindraḥ ᵥstutaḥ asmābhiḥ stuto bhavati tadendraḥ ᵥekaḥ asahāya eva ᵥbhūrīṇi bahūni ᵥapratīni apratigamanāni |
abhigamanayuktānītyarthaḥ |
ᵥvṛtrā vṛtrāṇi śatrūn ᵥhanti hinasti |
ᵥyasya indrasya ᵥśarman śarmaṇi āśraye vartamānaṃ stotāraṃ ᵥdevāḥ ᵥnakiḥ ᵥvārayante nivāraṇaṃ na kurvanti |
ᵥmartāḥ ca ᵥna nivārayante |
ᵥasya indrasya ᵥjaritā stotā ᵥpriyaḥ bhavati ||
ābhimārutaśastre 'evā na indraḥ' iti paridhānīyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'evā na indro madhavā virapśīti paridadhyāt' ( ābha. śrau. 5. 20 ) iti ||
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-la-sa- 'svargādiśokaṃ' nāsti |
2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-dehi |
3. kha-ga gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha- 'he indra' nāsti |
4. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-sa- 'tvāṃ' nāsti |
5. kha-ga-ca- ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-sa-nivārayante nivāraṇaṃ ( kha-ga-ca-pha-nivāraṇaṃ, bha-sa-nirdhāraṇaṃ ) na kurvanti kra. 2-73
evā na indro maghavā virapśī karatatsatyā carṣaṇīdhṛtanarvā | tvaṃ rājā januṣāṃ dhehyasme adhi śravo māhine yarjire || 20 || eva | naḥ | indraḥ | maghavā | virapśī | karata | satyā | carṣaṇīdhṛta | anarvā | tvam | rājā | januṣāṃ | dhehi | asme iti | adhi | śravaḥ | māhinem | yat | jaritre || 20 ||
virapśī bahuvidhaśabdavān carṣaṇīdhṛta sarvāsāṃ prajānāṃ dhārayitā anarvā anapyutaḥ |
kenāpi pratikūlena na prāpta ityarthaḥ |
maghavā dhanavān indraḥ evā evamasmābhiḥ stutaḥ san naḥ asmākaṃ satyā satyarūpānyabhimatāni phalāni karata karotu |
he indra januṣāṃ janmavatāṃ sarveṣāṃ rājā prabhuḥ tvaṃ māhine mahimnopetaṃ yat śravaḥ yaśaḥ jaritre stotre dyaṃ tadyaśaḥ asme asmāsu adhi adhikatvena dhehi |
evā na indro maghavā virapśītyuttamayā paridhānīyam vā indro maghavā virapśī (ai. brā. 3. 38) ityādi brāhmaṇam ||
nū ṣuta indra nū gṛṇāna iṣaṃ jaritre nadyo na pīpeḥ | akāri te harivo brahma navyaṃ dhiyā syāma rathyaḥ sadāsāḥ || 21 || nu | sutaḥ | indra | nu | gṛṇānaḥ | iṣam | jaritre | nadyaḥ | na | pīperiti pīpeḥ | akāri | te | harivaḥ | brahma | navyam | dhiyā | syāma | rathyaḥ | sadāsāḥ || 21 ||
nū ṣuta indrati vyākhyātā ||
24 ||
'ayaṃ panthāḥ' iti trayodaśarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ traiṣṭubham |
atrānukramaṇikā—'ayaṃ panthāḥ sahonā saṃvāda indrāditivāmadevānām' iti |
'yasya vākyaṃ sa ṛṣiḥ yā tenocyate sā devatā' iti paribhāṣitatvāt ṛṣidevate tatra tatra jñātavye |
asmin sūkte indrāditivāmadevasaṃvādarūpiṇī kācit ākhyāyikā ślokarūpeṇa sūcyate— garbhastho jñānasaṃpannā vāmadevo mahāmuniḥ |
matiṃ cakre na jāyeya yonideśāttu mātṛtaḥ ||
1 ||
kiṃtu pārśvāditaśceti jñātvā nu jananī vidam |
dadhyau śāntyai śacīṃ devīmaditiṃ svindramātaram ||
2 ||
aditiścvandrasahitā garbhiṇīmabhyagādane |
aditīndrāvāmadevāḥ saṃvādamatha cakre ||
3 ||
saṃvāda iti sūtreṇa kathā saipātra sūcyate |
nokto vaktṛviśeṣo'tra hyupadeśeṣvanuktiḥ ||
4 ||
arthatastvavagantavyo vaktṛbheda iti sthitiḥ |
indra'ditirvipraśvāsminnmathaḥ sūkte samūcire ||
5 ||
garbhaṃ śayānaṃ suciraṃ māturbhādanirgatam |
vāmadevaṃ pratibrūta ādyarcā śatakratuḥ ||
6 ||
dvitīyādibhi- rardharcairvipratrāha pañcabhiḥ |
na hī nvasyeti sapta syurdharcā aditarvacaḥ ||
7 ||
mamañcana tvā yuvatirityṛcaḥ pañca vai muneḥ |
daurbhāgyaśāntimatrāha vāmadevastathānvayā ||
8 ||
evamarthādakṛbheda iti bahudhaśāsanam |
atho vivekaspaṣṭatvādupadeśeṣu neritaḥ ||
9 ||
ṛṣidevatāsiddhayarthaṃ vivekādartha īritaḥ' (anu. bhā. 4. 18) iti ||
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha- 'prabhuḥ' nāsti | 2. la-paridhyādiyam | 3. ta-vyākhyāteyam; sa-prāgvyākhyānā | 4. ca-ṇa-saṃvādarūpā |
ayaṃ panthā anuvittaḥ purāṇo yato devā udajāyanta viśve | ataścidā janiṣīṣṭa pravṛddho mā mātaraṃmamuyā pattave kaḥ || 1 || ayam | panthāḥ | anu'vittaḥ | purāṇaḥ | yataḥ | devāḥ | ut'ajāyanta | viśve | ataḥ | cit | ā | janiṣīṣṭa | pra'vṛddhaḥ | mā | mātaram | amuyā | pattave | kariti kaḥ ||1||
indravākyam |
purāṇaḥ anādiḥ yaṃ pratyakṣopalabhyamānaḥ panthāḥ yonirgamanarūpo mārgaḥ anu ānupūrvyena vittaḥ sarvairjāyamānairlabdhaḥ |
yataḥ yasmādyonimārgāt viśve sarve devāḥ uda- jāyanta utkarṣeṇotpannā bhavanti |
tasmādyonimārgātmanuṣyaḥ utpanna bhavantīti kimu vaktavyam |
ataścit asmādyonimārgādeva pravṛddhaḥ garbhe prakarṣeṇa vṛddhiṃgato vāmadevaḥ ā janiṣīṣṭa ā samantāt jāyatām |
amuyā amūṃ mātaraṃ pattave patanāya |
maraṇāyetyarthaḥ |
mā kaḥ mā karotu ||
nāhamato niryā durgahaitacchiraśtā pārśvābhirgamāṇi | bahūni me akṛtā katvāni yudhyai tvena saṃ tvena pṛcchai || 2 || na | aham | ataḥ | niḥ | aya | duḥ'gahā | etat | tiraśvatā | pārśvāt | niḥ | gamāni | bahūni | me | akṛtā | katvāni | yudhyai | tvena | sam | tvena | pṛcchai || 2 ||
vāmadeva evamuktavantamindraṃ pratyāha |
aham ataḥ yonimārgāt na niryā na niryāṇi |
na nirgacchāni |
etat yonirgamanarūpaṃ vartma durgahā durgamam |
duḥkhena grāhyam |
na prāpyaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
kiṃtu tiraśvatā tiraśmīnāt pārśvāt nirgamāni nirgacchāni |
yonideśādanirgato'haṃ pārśve bhittvā nirgacchānītyarthaḥ |
anyairakṛtamidameva na kevalaṃ mayā kriyate kiṃvanvayaiḥ akṛtāni bahūni karmāṇi me karvāṇi kartavyāni santi |
tvena ekena sapatnena vivadamānena saha yudhyai yuddhaṃ karavāṇi |
tvena ekena yudhusunā saṃ pṛcchai samyak pṛcchāni ||
parāyatīṃ mātaranmanvaṣṭa na nānu gānyanu nū gamāni | tvaṣṭurgṛhe apibatsomamindraḥ śatadhanvyai cumbauḥ sutasya || 3 || parā'yatīm | mātaram | anu | acche | na | na | anu | gāni | anu | nu | gamāni | tvaṣṭuḥ | gṛhe | apibat | somam | indraḥ | śata'dhanvyam | cumbauḥ | sutasya || 3 ||
1. na-la-sa- indravākyaṃ nāsti | 2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-ta-na-bha-sa-bhavanti | 3. la-kim | 4. ta-durgaham | 5. na-durbhahaṇīyam | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-pārśve | 7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-akṛtā sapatnenākṛtāni | 8. ta-pṛcchāmi |
mayi garbhasthite' satīndro madīyāṃ ^mātaraṃ ^parāyatīṃ paretāṃ mriyamāṇām ^anvacaṣṭa anvamabīt |
evamapi idānīṃ garbhe sthito'haṃ purāṇaṃ panthānaṃ ^na ^nānu ^gāni nānugacchānīti na |
kiṃtu ^nu kṣipram ^anu ^gamāni anugacchānyeva |
vāmadevaḥ svakīyamakṛtyakāritvaṃ parihatyendrasyākṛtyakāritvam uttarā- rdhena^ pratipādayati |
^indraḥ ^camvoḥ somābhipavaphalakayoḥ ^sutasya somaṃ sutavato'bhiṣutavataḥ ^tvaṣṭuḥ saṃbadhini ^gṛhe anupahūtaḥ san ^śatadhanyaṃ bahudhanena krītaṃ ^somam ^apibat balātkāreṇa somasya pānaṃ kṛtavān |
ayamarthamaittirīyaiḥ : 'tvaṣṭā hataputro vīndraṃ somamāharat' (tai. saṃ. 2. 4. 12. 1) ityanuvāke mahatā prabandhena prapañcitaḥ ||
kiṃ sa ṛdhakṛṇvadyaṃ sahasraṃ māso jabhāra śaradaśca pūrvīḥ |
nahī nvasya pratimānamastyantarjāteṣu ye janitvāḥ ||
4 ||
kim | saḥ | ṛdhak | kṛṇvat | yam | sahasram | māsaḥ | jabhāra | śaradaḥ | ca | pūrvīḥ | nahi | nu | asya | prati'mānam | asti | antaḥ | jāteṣu | uta | ye | jani'tvāḥ || 4 ||
aditiḥ ^yam indraṃ ^sahasraṃ bahūn ^māsaḥ māsān ^pūrvīḥ bahvīḥ ^śaradaśca saṃvatsarānva ^jabhāra babhāra ||
bibharteridaṃ rūpam ||
garbhe sthita indro bahupu saṃvatsarevaditiṃ kleśitavānityarthaḥ |
evaṃbhūtaḥ ^saḥ indraḥ ^kiṃ yat^ kimapi |
sarvamapītyarthaḥ |
^ṛdhak viraddhaṃ karma ^kṛṇvat akarot |
indrasya ākṣepam^ asahamanāditirindramātāpi enaṃ prati brūte |

^nu^ iti pādapūraṇārthaḥ^ |
^uta api ca ^ye devādayaḥ ^janitvāḥ janitavyā janayiṣyamāṇāsteṣu madhye'syendrasya pratimānaṃ nāsti ||
avaghamiṃva manyamānā guhākirindraṃ mātā vīryeṇā nyuṣṭam |
athodasthātsvayamakaṃ vasān ā rodasī apuṇājjāyamānaḥ ||
5 ||
avagham'iva | manyamānā | guhā | akaḥ | indram | mātā | vīryeṇa | ni'ṛṣṭam | atha | ut | asthāt | svayam | arkam | vasānaḥ | ā | rodasī | iti | apuṇāt | jāyamānaḥ || 5 ||
^guhā guhāyāṃ gahanarūpe sūtikāgṛhe jātam ^indram ^avaghamiva garbhava ^manyamānā jānatī ^mātā indrajanayaditiḥ ^vīryeṇa sāmarthyena ^nyuṣṭaṃ nitarāṃ prāptam ^akaḥ akarot |
^atha anantaraṃ
1. ta-garbhasthe | 2. ta-uttarārdhena | 3. kha-ga-ca-na-bha-sa-kiyat | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-kṣepaṇam | 5. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-ma-la-sa-'devādiva' nāsti | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-'upamānaṃ' nāsti | 7. kha-ga- ca-na-bha-sa-hi | 8. ta-la-pūraṇārthaḥ | 9. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-ta-na-pa-pha-bha-la-sa-'garbhava' nāsti |

^alalābharvantī: alalayetyevarūpaṃ śabdaṃ kurvatya: ^ṛtāvarīriva udakavatya eva ^etā: nadya: ^saṃkrośamānā: indramahattvapratipādakoktena harṣeṇa bahuvidhaṃ śabdayamānā: satya: ^arṣanti gacchanti |
evaṃbhūtā nadya: ^idaṃ ^kiṃ ^bhananti śrotragrāhyaṃ śabdayamānaṃ kiṃ vadantīmamarthaṃ he ṛṣe tvam ^etā: nadya: ^vi ^pṛccha viśeṣeṇa pṛṣṭavān bhava |
evaṃ saṃgṛccha |
vicārite sati etacchabdayamānamindramāhā- tmyasyaiva vacanaṃ bhavatītyartha: |

^nivida: |

indro'sṛṣṭā āpastamya pāpaṃ jaguritihyartha: |
1. ta-la-sa- ‘eva ’ nāsti | 2. ta-tiṣṭhati | 3. ṇa-pūrāmāsa iti | 4. ta-udakāvārakam | 5. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-ma-sa- ‘indraṃ ’ nāsti | 6. ta-ya: indra: | 7. ṇa-samutsṛṣṭavān |

8 ||
marma̭t | cana | tvā | yuvatiḥ | parā'āsa̭ | marma̭t | cana | tvā | kuṣavā̭ | ja॒gāra̭ | marma̭t | cit | āpaḥ | śiśa̭ve | ma॒mṛḍyuḥ | marma̭t | cit | indraḥ | sahasā | ut | atiṣṭhat || 8 ||
sūktadoṣeṇa ṛpirindraṃ stauti |
he indra marmacana mācanasyeva |
pramataivetyarthaḥ |
yuvatiḥ tvadīyā mātāditiḥ tvā tvāṃ parāsa parākṣipa |
kiṃca kuṣavānākṣī kācit rākṣasī sā marmacana pramasyaiva tvā tvāṃ jagāra girati sma |
he indra marmacit pramātasya eva āpaḥ śiśave jātāya tubhyaṃ mamṛḍyuḥ sukhayāṃcakruḥ |
marmacit māghasyeva indraḥ sahasā svena vīryeṇa udatiṣṭhat sūtikāgṛhāt rākṣasīṃ bādhamānaḥ san utkarṣeṇa tasthau ||
marma॒cana tai maghava॒nyaṃso nivivi॒dhvā~ apa̭ ha॒nū ja̭ghāna |
adhā̭ nivi̭ddha utta̭ro babhūvāñchiro̭ dā॒sasya̭ saṃ piṇagva॒dhena̭ ||
9 ||
marma̭t | cana | te | magha'van | vi'aṃsaḥ | ni'vivi॒dhvān | apa | ha॒nū iti̭ | ja॒ghāna | adha | ni'viddhaḥ | ut'taraḥ | babhūvān | śiraḥ | dā॒sasya̭ | sam | piṇak | va॒dhena̭ || 9 ||
he maghavan dhanavannindra marmacana pramātasyaiva vyaṃso nāma rākṣasaḥ nivividhvān pravidhan te hanū tvadīye hanū mukhapāśrve apa jaghāna apahatavān |
adha athānantaraṃ he indra nividdhaḥ rākṣasena tāḍitaḥ tvam uttaraḥ vyaṃsādadhikabalaḥ babhūvān bhūta: san dāsasya kṣīṇasya vyaṃsasya śiraḥ vadhena vajreṇa saṃ piṇak piṣṭavānasi ||
gṛṣṭiḥ saṃsṷva sthaviraṃ tavā॒gāma̭nādhṛṣyaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ tu॒mamindra̭m |
arī̭ḻahaṃ vṛ॒tsa cura̭thāya mā॒tā sva॒yaṃ gātuṃ tu॒nva i̭cchamānam ||
10 ||
gṛṣṭiḥ | susṷva | sthaviram | tavā॒gām | anādhṛṣyam | vṛṣabham | tu॒mam | indra̭m | arī̭ḻaham | vṛ॒tsam | cura̭thāya | mā॒tā | sva॒yam | gātum | tu॒nvai | i̭cchamānam || 10 ||
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- ' kācit ' nāsti |

4. kha-ga-ca-pha-nividdhaḥ svecchayā he indra; gha-ṇa-nividdhaḥ svecchayā; pa-bha-he indra nividdhaḥ svecchayā; sa-he indra |
5. ṇa-tvaṃ he indra |

kīdṛśamindram |

he indra tvaṃ carṣaṇīnāṃ prajānāṃ rāyaḥ dhanasya kṣayasya gṛhasya ca īkṣe īkṣase |

9 ||
kayā | tat | śṛṇve | śacyā | śaciṣṭhaḥ | yayāṃ | kṛṇoti | muhu | kā | cit | ṛṣvaḥ | puru | dādhuṣe | viḍcayiṣṭhaḥ | aṃhaḥ | arthā | dadhāti | draviṇam | jaritre || 9 ||

sa indraḥ dādhuṣe havirdattavate yajamānāya puru bhūyiṣṭham aṃhaḥ pāpaṃ vicayiṣṭhaḥ atiśayena nāśakau bhavati |
atha api ca jaritre stotre draviṇaṃ paścādidhanaṃ dadhāti dadāti ||
mā nau madhīrā bharā daddhi tannaḥ pra dādhuṣe dātave bhūri yattai |
navyai deṣṇe śaste asinta ukthe pra bravāma vayamindra stuvantaḥ ||
10 ||
mā | naḥ | madhīḥ | ā | bhara | daddhi | tat | naḥ | pra | dādhuṣe | dātave | bhūri | yat | te | navyai | deṣṇe | śaste | asmin | te | ukthe | pra | bravāma | vayam | indra | stuvantaḥ || 10 ||
he indra tvaṃ naḥ asmān mā madhīḥ mā hiṃsīḥ |
kiṃtu tvam ā bhara poṣakau bhava |

pra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
naḥ asmabhyaṃ tat dhanam āhṛtya daddhi dehi |

1. ta-dhanasya īkṣe | 2. kha-pa-pha-bha-' ca ' nāsti | 3. kha-pa-pha-bha-' kayā ' nāsti | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-yaḥ ṛṣvaḥ | 5. ta-na-la-sa-' mahānindraḥ ' nāsti | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-yad bhūri bahupi dhanam |
na॒ ṣṭuta॒ indra̭ nū gṝ॒ṇāna॒ iṣa̭ṃ ja॒ri॒tre॒ na॒dyo॒ na pī̭peḥ | a॒kāri̭ te॒ hari̭vo॒ brahma̭ na॒vya॒ṃ dhiyā॒ syāma̭ ra॒thya̭ḥ sa॒dāsā̭ḥ || 11 || nu | stutaḥ | indra | nu | gṝṇānaḥ | iṣam | jaritre | naryaḥ | na | pīperiti pīpeḥ | akāri | te | harivaḥ | brahma | navyam | dhiyā | syāma | rathyaḥ | sadāsāḥ || 11 || pūrvaṃ vyākhyātā || || 4 ||
‘ ā yā॒tvindraḥ ’ ityekādaśarcamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ ā yātu ’ ityanukrāntam |
pṛṣṭhābhiplavapaḍahayoḥ prathame'hanyetadvijñānīyām |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ ā yātvindro'vasa iti marutvatīyam ’ ( ādha. śrau. 7. 5 ) iti ||
ā yā॒tvindro'va̭su॒ upa̭ na॒ iha̭ stu॒taḥ sa॒dha
ā yātvindro diva ā pṛthivyā makṣu samudrāduta vā purīṣāt | svarṇarādavase no marutvānparāvatau vā sadanādṛtasya || 3 || ā | yātu | indraḥ | divaḥ | ā | pṛthivyāḥ | makṣu | samudrāt | uta | vā | purīṣāt | svaḥ'narāt | avase | naḥ | marutvān | parāvataḥ | vā | sadanāt | ṛtasya || 3 ||
^marutvān marudbhiḥ sahitaḥ ^indraḥ ^naḥ asmākam ^avase rakṣaṇāya ^divaḥ svargalokāt ^ā ^yātu āgacchatu |
^pṛthivyāḥ bhūlokācca ^makṣu śīghram ^ā yātu |
^uta ^vā athavā ^samudrāt antarikṣalokādvāyātu |
samudra dṛśyantarikṣanāma 'sagaraḥ samudraḥ adhvaram' (ni. 1. 3. 15) iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt |
athavā ^purīṣāt udakādāyātu |
purīṣamityudakanāma 'madhu purīṣaṃ pippalam' (ni. 1. 12. 12) iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt |
^svarṇarāt svarādityayo naro netā yasya tasmāllokādvā āyātu |
^parāvato ^vā dūrādvā āyātu |
^ṛtasya udakasya ^sadanāt sthānāt meghalokādvā āyātu ||
sthūrasya rāyo bṛhato ya īśe tamu stvāma vidatheṣbindram | yo vāyunā jayati gormatīṣu pra dhṛṣṇuyā nayati vasyo accha || 4 || sthūrasya | rāyaḥ | bṛhataḥ | yaḥ | īśe | tam | ū~ iti | stvām | vidatheṣu | indram | yaḥ | vāyunā | jayati | go'matīṣu | pra | dhṛṣṇuyā | nayati | vasyaḥ | accha || 4 ||
^yaḥ indraḥ ^sthūrasya sthūlasya ^bṛhataḥ mahataḥ ^rāyaḥ dhanasya ^īśe īṣṭe ^vidatheṣu yajñeṣu |
vidatha iti yajñanāmaiṭatat |
^tam ^indram ^u ^tamindrameva vayaṃ stotāraḥ ^stvvāma |

^dhṛṣṇuyā dhṛṣṇuḥ pragalbho ya indraḥ stotṛn ^vasyaḥ vasīyaḥ śreṣṭhaṃ paścādidhanam ^accha abhi ^pra ^nayati prāpayati |
tamevendraṃ stavāmeti parvaṇa sambandhaḥ ||
upa yo namo narmasi stabhāyanniryati vāceṃ janayantyajñai | ṛñjānaḥ puruvāraṃ ukthairendraṃ kṛṇvīta sadaneṣu hotā || 5 || upa | yaḥ | namaḥ | narmasi | stabhāyan | iryati | vācam | janayan | yajñai | ṛñjānaḥ | puru'vāraḥ | ukthaiḥ | ā | indram | kṛṇvīta | sadaneṣu | hotā || 5 ||

2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-sa- 'tamindram' nāsti |

'ṛatiḥ prasādhanakarmā' iti yāskaḥ |

nama ityannanāmainatat |

6 ||

^dhiṣaṇyantaḥ dhiṣaṇāmindraviṣayāṃ stutimicchantaḥ ^auśijasya uśijaḥ kāmayamānā ṛtvijaḥ |

pṛṣṭhābhilavaṣaḍahayoḥ prathame'hani mādhyandinasavane maitrāvaruṇaśastre dvitīyamidaṃ saṃpātasūktam |

1 ||
yat | naḥ | indraḥ | jujuṣe | yat | ca | vaṣṭi | tat | naḥ | mahān | karati | śuṣmī | ā | cit | brahma | stomam | maghavā | somam | ukthā | yaḥ | aśmānam | śavasā | bibhrat | eti || 1 ||
^indraḥ ^naḥ asmadīyaṃ ^yat havilakṣaṇānnādikaṃ ^jujuṣe sevate ^yacca havirādikaṃ ca ^vaṣṭi kāmayate ^mahān prabhūtaḥ ^śuṣmī balavānindraḥ |
^ciditi cārthe |
^brahma ^cit havilakṣaṇapuroḍāśādi- kamaṃ ca ^stomaṃ stotrasamūhaṃ ca ^somam abhiṣutaṃ somaṃ ca ^ukthā ukthāni śastrāṇi yeyevaṃbhūtaṃ ^tat brahmāditvaṣṭayam ^ā ^karati ākarotu svīkarotu |

2 ||
vṛṣā | vṛṣandhim | catuḥ'aśrim | asyan | ugraḥ | bāhu'bhyām | nṛ'tamaḥ | śacī'vān | śriye | paruṣṇīm | uṣamāṇaḥ | ūrṇām | yasyāḥ | parvāṇi | saruyāya | viṇye || 2 ||
^vṛṣā kāmānāṃ varṣitā ^vṛṣandhiṃ meghabhedanadvāreṇa varṣaṃ kurvantaṃ ^caturaśri catasṛbhiḥ aśmibhiḥ dhārābhirupetaṃ vajraṃ ^bāhubhyāṃ hastābhyām ^asyan śatruṣu prakṣiptan ^ugraḥ udvarṇabalaḥ ^nṛtamaḥ netṛtamaḥ ^śacīvān karmavānindraḥ ^ūrṇām ācchādikāṃ ^paruṣṇīṃ parvatavatīṃ nadīṃ ^śriye āśrayaṇāryam ^uṣamāṇaḥ sevamānaḥ^1 bhavati |
^yasyāḥ nadyāḥ ^parvāṇi bhinnān deśān ^saruyāya sakhikarmaṇe ^viṇye saṃvṛtavān||
yo devo devatamo jāyamāno maho vājairmahadbhiśca śubhraiḥ |

3 ||
yaḥ | devaḥ | deva'tamaḥ | jāyamānaḥ | mahaḥ | vājaiḥ | mahat'bhiḥ | ca | śubhraiḥ | dadhānaḥ | vajram | bāhvoḥ | uśantam | dhāma | mena | rejayat | pra | bhūma || 3 ||

˅viśva
he ᵛindra tvaṃ ᵛyat yadā ᵛbadhumānāḥ ᵛyatreṇa badhyamānāḥ ᵛsīmanu etāḥ ᵛnadīrhisya ᵛdīrghām adhyadhikāṃ ᵛprasitiṃ bandhanam ᵛanu ᵛsyandayadhyai yathākāmaṃ syandituṃ ᵛpra ᵛmucaḥ prakarṣeṇāmocayaḥ |
he ᵛharivaḥ harivan indra ᵛannāha atraiva mocanavelāyāṃ ᵛtā ᵛu tāḥ prasiddhā eva ᵛdevīḥ devyo dyotamānāḥ ᵛsvasāraḥ nadhaḥ |
atra svasaśabdo nadīvācī |
ᵛavobhiḥ tvatkṛtai rakṣaṇaiḥ ᵛte tvāṃ ᵛstvanta stāvayanti |
nadīpūraṇādibhiḥ karmabhiḥ janā indraṃ stuvantītyarthaḥ ||
pipīle aṃśurmadyo na sindhurā tvā śamīṃ śaśamānasya śaktiḥ |
asmadryakṣaśuśucānasya yamyā āśune raśmi tubhyojasaṃ goḥ ||
8 ||
pipīle | aṃśuḥ | madyaḥ | na | sindhuḥ | ā | tvā | śamīṃ | śaśamānasya | śaktiḥ | asmadryak | śuśucānasya | yamyāḥ | āśuḥ | na | raśmim | tuvi'ojasam | goḥ || 8 ||
he indra ᵛmaghaḥ mādayitā ᵛaṃśuḥ somaḥ ᵛpipīle apīḍhyata |
abhyabhūyata ᵛityarthaḥ |
neti saṃpraśraye |
ᵛna idānīṃ ᵛsindhuḥ syandamānaḥ somaḥ ᵛtvā tvām ᵛā ᵛyamyāḥ āyacchatu |
ᵛśuśucānasya dīpyamānasya ᵛśaśamānasya stuvataḥ saṃbandhī ᵛasmadryak asmadabhimukhaṃ ᵛśamī śamanaṃ ᵛśaktiḥ stutikarma ca āyacchatu |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵛāśuḥ śīghragāmī yantā ᵛgoḥ ganturaśvasya ᵛtubhyojasaṃ bahubalam |
dṛḍhamityarthaḥ |
ᵛraśmi ᵛna aśvabandhanarajjumiva tadvat ||
asme varṣiṣṭhā kṛṇuhi jyeṣṭhā nṛmṇāni satrā sahure sahauṃsi |
asmabhyaṃ vṛtrā suhanāni randhi jahi vardharvanuṣo martyasya ||
9 ||
asme iti | varṣiṣṭhā | kṛṇuhi | jyeṣṭhā | nṛmṇāni | satrā | sahure | sahauṃsi | asmabhyam | vṛtrā | su'hanāni | randhi | jahi | vardhaḥ | vanuṣaḥ | martyasya || 9 ||
he ᵛsahure sahanaśīlendra tvaṃ ᵛsatrā sarvadā ᵛsahauṃsi śatrūnbhibhāvukāni ᵛvarṣiṣṭhā varṣiṣṭhāni pravṛddhāni ᵛjyeṣṭhā jyeṣṭhāni praśastāni ᵛnṛmṇāni balāni ᵛasme asmabhyaṃ ᵛkṛṇuhi kuru |
he indra tvam ᵛasmabhyaṃ ᵛsuhanāni śobhanavaghāni ᵛvṛtrā vṛtrāṇi śatrūn ᵛrandhi randhaya vaśaṃ naya |
kiṃca ᵛvanuṣaḥ hiṃsakasya ᵛmartyasya maraṇadharmaṇaḥ ᵛśatroḥ saṃbandhi ᵛvadhaḥ hananasādhanamāyudhaṃ ᵛjahi nāśaya ||
asmākamitsu śṛṇuhi tvamindrāsmabhyaṃ citrāṃ upa māhi vājān |
asmabhyaṃ viśvā iṣaṇaḥ puraṃdhīrasmākaṃ su maghavanbodhi godāḥ ||
10 ||
asmākam | it | su | śṛṇuhi | tvam | indra | asmabhyam | citrān | upa | māhi | vājān | asmabhyam | viśvāḥ | iṣaṇaḥ | puram'dhīḥ | asmākam | su | maghavan | bodhi | go'dāḥ || 10 ||
1. pa-pha-bādhyamānāḥ | 2. na-la-sa-enāḥ | 3. na-abhyasūyata | 4. ta- maraṇaśīlasya |
he indra tvam asmākamita asmadīyāḥ eva stutīḥ su suṣṭhu śṛṇuhi |
tathā he indra tvaṃ citrān cayanīyāni vājān annāni asmabhyam upa māhi dehi |
kiṃca asmabhyaṃ viśvāḥ sarvāḥ puraṃdhīḥ buddhīḥ dṛṣaṇaḥ preraya |
api ca he maghavan dhanavacchindra tvaṃ asmākaṃ godāḥ gavāṃ dātā su suṣṭhu bodhi bhava ||
nū ṣuta indra nū gṛṇāna iṣe jaritre nayo' na pīpeḥ |
akāri te harivo brahma navyaṃ dhiyā syāma rathyaḥ sadāsāḥ ||
11 ||
nu | stutaḥ | indra | nu | gṛṇānaḥ | iṣam | jaritre | nadyaḥ | na | pīperiti pīpeḥ | akāri | te | hari'vaḥ | brahma | navyam | dhiyā | syāma | rathyaḥ | sadāsāḥ || 11 ||
pūrve vyākhyātā ||
8 ||
‘kathā mahām’ ityekādaśacaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ṛtasya hi’ ihāthāstintra ṛtadevatākā vā |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘kathopanyāstintra ṛtadevyo vā’ iti |
ābhiplavike'hani daśarātre prathame'hani mādhyandinasavane maitrāvaruṇaśastre'hīnasūktasya sthānaṃ idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ saṃpātasūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘kathā mahāmindraḥ pūrbhidha eka it’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 5) iti |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye etat |
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake śaunakena sūtritaṃ ca—‘kathā mahāmavṛdhatsya hoturiti saṃpāta:’ (ai. ā. 5. 2. 2) iti ||
kathā mahāmavṛdhatsya hoturyajñaṃ juṣāṇo abhi somamūrdhaḥ |
piban‌nuśāno juṣamāṇo andho vavakṣa ṛṣvaḥ śucate dhanāya ||
1 ||
kathā | mahām | avṛdhat | kasya | hoturaḥ | yajñam | juṣāṇaḥ | abhi | somam | ūrdhaḥ | piban | uśānaḥ | juṣamāṇaḥ | andhaḥ | vavakṣe | ṛṣvaḥ | śucate | dhanāya || 1 ||
mahā mahāntaṃ guṇaiḥ pravṛddhamindraṃ kathā kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa avṛdhat vardhayet |
asmatpreritā stutiriti śeṣaḥ |
tasya mahimno'timahattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
sa evendraḥ hotuḥ homaniṣpādakasya kasya yajamānasya yajñaṃ yāgaṃ juṣāṇaḥ prīyamāṇaḥ san abhi gacchediti śeṣaḥ |
upasargaśruterhacitakriyādhyāhāraḥ |
kiṃca ūdhaḥ uddhatamatipravṛddhaṃ somam andhaḥ somalakṣaṇamannaṃ piban āsvādayan uśānaḥ tadevānnaṃ kāmayamānaḥ juṣamāṇaḥ sevamānaśca ṛṣvaḥ mahānindraḥ śucate pradīptāya dhanāya hiraṇyādilakṣaṇāya |
uktalakṣaṇaṃ dhanaṃ kasmai yajamānāya dātuṃ vavakṣe vahati |
pītamevānnaṃ jaṭhare dhārayati |
anena vākyadvayena indrasyācintyacaritatvaṃ khyāpitaṃ bhavati ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-asmadīyāmeva stutim | 2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-bahīḥ | 3. na-sa-‘tvaṃ’ nāsti | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-avadharyat | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa-hiraṇyārtha lakṣaṇāya, ta-hiraṇyārthalakṣaṇāya | ṛ. 2-76
ko asya vīraḥ sadhamādamāpa samānaṃśa sumatibhiḥ ko asya |
kasya citraṃ cikite kadutī vṛdhe bhuvacchaśamānasya yajñauḥ ||
2 ||
kaḥ | asya | vīraḥ | sadha'mādam | āpa | sam | ānaṃśa | sumati'bhiḥ | kaḥ | asya | kat | asya | citram | cikite | kat | ūtī | vṛdhe | bhuvat | śaśamānasya | yajñauḥ || 2 ||
^asya indrasya saṃbandhin ^sadhamādam |
saha mādhantīti sadhamādaḥ saṃgrāmaḥ |
taṃ saha somapānaṃ vā ^vīraḥ stotrāṇāmamitrāṇāṃ vā vīrayitā ^kaḥ pumān ^āpa prāpnoti |
^kaḥ ca ^asya indrasya ^sumatibhiḥ śobhanābhiranugrahabuddhibhiḥ ^samānaṃśa saṃgacchate |
^kat kadā vā ^asya indrasya ^citraṃ cāyanīyaṃ dhanaṃ ^cikite prajñāyate |
tatpradānasamayaṃ na ko'pi jānātītyarthaḥ |
^kat kadā vā ^śaśa- mānasya stuvataḥ ^yajñauḥ yajurajamānasya ^vṛdhe vardhanāya ^ūtī ūrayā rakṣayā ^bhuvat bhavet ||
kuthā śṛṇoti hūyamānamindraḥ kuthā śṛṇvarvasāmasya veda |
kā asya pūrvīrūpamātayo ha kuthainamāhuḥ parpuriṃ jaritre ||
3 ||
kuthā | śṛṇoti | hūyamānam | indraḥ | kuthā | śṛṇvan | avasām | asya | veda | kāḥ | asya | pūrvīḥ | upa'mātayaḥ | ha | kuthā | enam | āhuḥ | parpurim | jaritre || 3 ||
^indraḥ parameśvarayuktaḥ ^hūyamānam āhvayantaṃ stotāraṃ ^kathā kathaṃ ^śṛṇoti |
stotrāṇi ^śṛṇvan ^asya stotuḥ saṃbandhīni ^avasāṃ rakṣaṇāni ^kathā kathaṃ ca ^veda vetti |
^pūrvīḥ purā- tanāni ^asya indrasya saṃbandhīni ^upamātayaḥ dānāni ^kāḥ ^ha kānicit santīti śeṣaḥ |
tāni dānāni ^jaritre stotuḥ saṃbandhīnāṃ kāmānāṃ ^parpuriṃ pūrayitāram ^enam indraṃ ^kathā kathaṃ ca ^āhuḥ vadanti ||
kuthā sabādhaḥ śaśamāno asya naśadābhi draviṇaṃ dīdhyānaḥ |
devo bhuvabhedā ma ṛtānāṃ namau jagmuvā~ abhi yajñajoṣat ||
4 ||
kuthā | sa'bādhaḥ | śaśamānaḥ | asya | naśat | abhi | draviṇam | dīdhyānaḥ | devaḥ | bhuvat | na'vedāḥ | me | ṛtānām | namaḥ | jagmuvān | abhi | yat | juṣat || 4 ||
^sabādhaḥ sapatnānāṃ pīḍāsahitaḥ ^śaśamānaḥ indraviṣayāṃ stutiṃ kurvan ^dīdhyānaḥ karmabhiḥ dīpyamāno yajamānaḥ ^asya indrasya saṃbandhi ^draviṇaṃ paścādidhanaṃ ^kathā katham ^abhi ^naśat

abhiprāmroni |

ye stotāra: asmin indre suyuja
ayamindraḥ ᵛduhaṃ drohaṃ kurvantīṃ ᵛdhvarasaṃ hiṃsikām ᵛanindrāṃ indramajānantīṃ rākṣasīṃ ᵛjighāṃsan hantumicchan ᵛtigmā tigmāni pūrvameva tīkṣṇāni ᵛanīkā anīkāni āyudhāni ᵛtujase tadvadhāya ᵛtetike atyarthaṃ tīkṣṇīkaroti |
ᵛyatra yāsūpāḥ su tadupalakṣitevahaḥ su ᵛṛṇā ᵛcit ṛṇāmyapi ᵛnaḥ asmān bādhanta iti śeṣaḥ |
tāḥ ᵛupasaḥ ca ᵛugraḥ udgūrṇabalaḥ ᵛṛṇayāḥ ṛṇasya hantendraḥ |
yātiḥ vadhakarmā |

30 ||
‘ kā sustutiḥ ’ ityekādaśacaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣamaindram |
daśamyanuṣṭup śiṣṭātriṣṭubhaḥ |
tathā cānukramaṇikā—‘ kā sustutirānyā'nuṣṭup ’ iti |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
kā sustutiḥ śarvasaḥ sunumindramarvācīnaṃ rādhasa ā vavartat |
dadirhi vīro gṛṇate vasūni sa gopatirniṣidhāṃ no janāsaḥ ||
1 ||
kā | su'stutiḥ | śarvasaḥ | sunum | indram | arvācīnam | rādhase | ā | vavartat | dadiḥ | hi | vīraḥ | gṛṇate | vasūni | saḥ | go'patiḥ | niḥ'sidhām | naḥ | janāsaḥ || 1 ||
vkā kīdṛśī vsustutiḥ vśarvasaḥ sūnuṃ balasya putram |
atibalinamityarthaḥ |
varvācīnam asmadabhimukham vindraṃ parameśvarayuktaṃ vrādhase dhanāya asmabhyaṃ dhanaṃ dātum vā vvavartat āvartayet |
he vjanāsaḥ janā yajamānāḥ vvīraḥ vikrāntaḥ vgopatiḥ paścādidhanasya pālakaḥ vsaḥ indraḥ vniṣidhāṃ niṣedhaṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ sambandhīni vvasūni dhanāni vgṛṇate gṛṇataḥ stotṛbhyaḥ vnaḥ asmabhyaṃ vdadirhi dātā khalu bhavati ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-devasya |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-devāya |
sa vṛtrahatye havyaḥ sa īḍyaḥ sa suṣṭuta indraḥ satyarādhāḥ | sa yāman maghavā martyāya brahmaṇyate sunvaye varivo dhāt || 2 || saḥ | vṛtra'hatye | havyaḥ | saḥ | īḍyaḥ | saḥ | su'stutaḥ | indraḥ | satya'rādhāḥ | saḥ | yāman | ā | magha'vā | martyāya | brahmaṇyate | sunvaye | varivaḥ | dhāt || 2 ||
saḥ indraḥ vṛtrahatye vṛtrāṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ hanye hanane nimittabhūte sati yāman yuddhe havyaḥ āhvātavyo bhavati |
saḥ indraḥ īḍyaḥ stutyo bhavati |
saḥ indraḥ suṣṭutaḥ san satyarādhāḥ yajamānebhyo dātuṃ satyadhano bhavati |
maghavā dhanavān saḥ indraḥ brahmaṇyate stotramicchate sunvaye somaṃ sunvate martyāya manuṣyāya yajamānāya varivaḥ dhanam |
variva iti dhananāmailatat |
ā dhāt ā samantāt dadāti ||
taminnaro vi hayante samīke ririkāṃsastanvaḥ kṛṇvata trām | mithyo yasyāgamabhayāso agmabharastokasya tanayasya sātau || 3 || tam | it | naraḥ | vi | hayante | sam'īke | ririkāṃsaḥ | tanvaḥ | kṛṇvata | trām | mithaḥ | yat | tyāgam | ubhayāsaḥ | agman | naraḥ | tokasya | tanayasya | sātau || 3 ||
naraḥ netāro manuṣyāḥ samīke yuddhe tamit tamevendraṃ vi hayante vividhamāhvānaṃ kurvate |
tanvaḥ svakīyāni śarīrāṇi ririkāṃsaḥ tapasā recayanto yajamānāstamevendraṃ trāṃ trātāraṃ kṛṇvata kṛṇvate kurvate |
yat yadā mithaḥ parasparaṃ saṃgatāḥ ubhayāsaḥ ubhaye yajamānāḥ stotāraśca tyāgaṃ tyāgakartāraṃ dātāramindram agman upagacchanti tadā naraḥ ubhayavidhā manuṣyāḥ tokasya putrasya tanayasya pautrasya ca sātau lābhe nimittabhūte sati tamevendraṃ trātāraṃ kurvantīti saṃbandhaḥ ||
kratuyanti kṣitayo yoga ugrāśuṣāṇāso mithyo arṇasātau | saṃ yadviśo'rvavṛtranta yuṣmā ādimema indrayante abhīke || 4 || kratu'yanti | kṣitayaḥ | yoge | ugra | āśu'ṣāṇasaḥ | mithaḥ | arṇa'sātau | sam | yat | viśaḥ | avavṛtranta | yuṣmā | āt | it | nemaṃ | indra'yante | abhīke || 4 ||
he ugra utkaṭabalendra āśuṣāṇāsaḥ vyāpnuvantaḥ kṣitayaḥ manuṣyāḥ |

kaścit ᵥādit anantara
ᵛatha asmin divase ᵛyaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵛindrāya paramarthayuktāya devāya ᵛsomaṃ¹ sollakṣaṇamannaṃ ᵛsunavat sunuyāt abhipavaṃ kuryāt |
tathā yaḥ ᵛpaktīḥ paktavyāṃśca puroḍāśādīn ᵛpacāt pacet pacanaṃ kuryāt |
ᵛuta api ca yaḥ ᵛdhānāḥ bharjanayogyān yavān |
atra bhṛṣṭayavavācī dhānāśabdastathāyaṃ yavamātraṃ lakṣayati |
ᵛbhṛjāti bhṛjeta bharjanaṃ kuryāt |
ᵛindraḥ ᵛmanāyoḥ stutimicchato yajamānasya ᵛucathāni stotrāṇi ᵛprati ᵛharyan pratikāmayamānaḥ san ᵛtasmin yajamāne ᵛvṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣaṇa- samarthaṃ ᵛśuṣmaṃ balaṃ ᵛdadhat dhārayati ||
yadā saṃmarya vyacedaghāvā dīrgha yadājimabhyaruryadaryaḥ | arcikadṛdharṣaṇaṃ patnyacchā duroṇa ā niśitaṃ somasudbhiḥ || 8 || yadā | sa'maryam | vi | acait | ṛghāvā | dīrgham | yat | ājim | abhi | ardhyat | aryaḥ | arcikadat | vṛṣaṇam | patnīṃ | accha | duroṇe | ā | niśitam | somasudbhiḥ || 8 ||
ᵛyadā ᵛṛghāvā² śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsaka indraḥ ᵛsamarthaṃ saha martyaṃ śatruṃ ᵛrayaceta vyajñāsīt viśeṣeṇa jānīyāt |
ᵛyat yadā ᵛaryaḥ svāmīndraḥ ᵛdīrgham atyadhikam ᵛāji saṃgrāmam ᵛadhyarayata abhipaśyeta tadā tasya³ indrasya ᵛpatnī ᵛduroṇe yajñagṛhe ᵛsomasudbhiḥ somaṃ suvadbhirhavirbhiḥ ᵛniśitaṃ tīkṣṇīkṛtam |
somapānenotsāhavantamityarthaḥ |
ᵛvṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāramindram ᵛaccha abhi ā ᵛacikadat ākrandati āhūyate ||
bhūryasā vṛtramacaratkanīyo'vikrīto akāniṣaṃ punaryan | sa bhūryasā kanīyo nārirecīdīnā dakṣā vi duhanti pra vāṇam || 9 || bhūryasā | vṛtram | acarat | kanīyaḥ | ⁴avikrītaḥ | ⁵akāniṣam | punaḥ | yan | saḥ | bhūryasā | kanīyaḥ | na | ⁶arirecīt | dīnāḥ | ⁷dakṣāḥ | vi | duhanti | pra | vāṇam || 9 ||
atra ṛgdaye saṃpradāyavidbhiḥ pūrvācāryaiḥ kecit ślokāḥ paṭhyante | te eva⁸ likhyante | ‘ahaṃ yaḥ parigṛhṇāti mūlyaṃ paṇyena bhūyasā | sa kretāraṃ punargacchanna vikrītastvayaṃ⁹ mayā’ || 1 || iti bhuvaṃ kāmayate punarmūlyasya pūraṇam | sa⁶ vikretā⁶ punarmūlyaṃ bhūyasā na praparyet || 2 || hīnaṃ na labhate varaṃ⁵ yadā vikrīta- vānpurā | yathāsamayameva syādyonaṃ punaranyathā || 3 || ayaṃ vikraya eveti samayaścet kṛto⁶ bhavet | atha mūlyārthametasyādvicāryaiva⁷ tu nirṇayaḥ⁹ || 4 || ityevaṃ samayo'kāri tadā mūlyaṃ praparyet¹⁰ | tasmādādau mayā kāryaḥ samayo'treti¹¹ cintayan¹¹ || 5 || vāmadevo vaśīkṛtya śakaṃ stotreṇa bhūyasā | vikrīṇan

6 ||
ataśca dṛṣṭvā ekārtho bhūyasā vastamityayam' |
ayamarthaḥ pratipādyate |
kaścit bhūyasā bhuñjā paṇyena dravyeṇa kanīyaḥ alpataraṃ vastaṃ vasu mūlyaṃ dhanam acarat prāpnoti |
punaryan kretāraṃ punargacchan sa vikretāyamartho mayā avikrītaḥ iti bruvan akānipaṃ mūlyapūrti kāmayate ||
vyatyayena uttamapuruṣaḥ ||
saḥ vikretā bhūyasā dhanena kanīyaḥ alpataraṃ mūlyaṃ nārirecīta ketuḥ sakāśācca riktīkaroti |
na labhata ityarthaḥ |
dīnāḥ asamarthāḥ dakṣāḥ samarthāśca vāṇaṃ vacanaṃ vi pra duhanti yathoktaṃ krayakāle tathaiva labhante ||
ka imaṃ daśābhirmamendraṃ krīṇāti dhenubhiḥ | yadā vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanadathainaṃ me punardedat || 10 || kaḥ | imam | daśābhiḥ | marma | indram | krīṇāti | dhenubhiḥ | yadā | vṛtrāṇi | jaṅghanat | atha | enam | me | punaḥ | dadat || 10 ||
mama madīyam imaṃ svabhūtam indraṃ dhenubhiḥ prāṇayitrībhiḥ daśābhiḥ daśasaṃkhyākābhiḥ stutibhiḥ kaḥ krīṇāti krayaṃ karoti |
tadānīṃ he kretāro yuṣmākaṃ madhye evamapi samayaḥ kriyate |
yadā ayamindraḥ vṛtrāṇi tvadīyān śatrūn jaṅghanat hanyāt atha anantarameva enam indraṃ me mahyaṃ punardadat punardadyāt ||
nū ṣuta indra nū gṛṇāna iṣe jaritre nadyo na pīpeḥ | akāri te harivo brahma navyaṃ dhiyā syāma rathyaḥ sadāsāḥ || 11 || nu | stutaḥ | indra | nu | gṛṇānaḥ | iṣam | jaritre | nadyaḥ | na | pīperiti pīpeḥ | akāri | te | harivaḥ | brahma | navyam | dhiyā | syāma | rathyaḥ | sadāsāḥ || 11 ||
pūrva vyākhyātā ||
12 ||
‘ko atha’ ityṛcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘ko athāṣṭau’ iti |
daśarātre caturthe'hani mādhyaṃdine savane stomavṛddhau maitrāvaruṇaśastre ārambhaṇīyāyā upariṣṭādidaṃ sūktamāpanīyaṃ ca |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘stome vardhamāne ko atha nyaḥ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7, 12 ) iti ||
ko adya naryo devakāma uśannindrasya sakhyaṃ jujoṣa | ko vā mahe'rvase pāryāya samiddhe agnau sutasoma īṭṭe || 1 || kaḥ | adya | naryaḥ | deva'kāmaḥ | uśan | indrasya | sakhyam | jujoṣa | kaḥ | vā | mahe | arbase | pāryāya | samiddhe | agnau | sutasomaḥ | īṭṭe || 1 ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-bha-vastamīritam; la-vastamityacam |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ta-na-pa-pha-bha-sa- ‘stutibhiḥ’ nāsti |
ṛ. 2-77
ᵛatra asmin divase ᵛnaryaḥ ᵛmanuṣyahitaḥ ᵛdevakāmaḥ ᵛdevānicchan ᵛuśan kāmayamānaḥ ᵛkaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵛindrasya ᵛmatsyaṃ sakhitvaṃ ᵛjujoṣa sevate |
kiṃca ᵛsutasomaḥ abhiṣutasomaḥ ᵛko ᵛvā yajamānaḥ ᵛagnau ᵛsamiddhe prajvalite sati ᵛmahaṃ mahate pravandāya ᵛpārvāya pāramahate ᵛavase tarpaṇāyā ᵛīṣṭe indraṃ stauti ||
ko nānāma varcasā somyārya manāyurvā bhavati vaste ustrāḥ | ka indrasya yujyaṃ kaḥ sakhitvaṃ ko bhrātraṃ vaṣṭi kavaye ka ūtī || 2 || kaḥ | nanāma | varcasā | somyārya | manāyuḥ | vā | bhavati | vastai | ustrāḥ | kaḥ | indrasya | yujyam | kaḥ | sakhitvam | kaḥ | bhrātram | vaṣṭi | kavaye | kaḥ | ūtī || 2 ||
ᵛkaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵛvacasā vāgrupayā stutyā ᵛsomyāya somāhyendrāya ᵛnanāma namati prahvī- bhavati |
kaḥ ᵛvā indrāya ᵛmanāyuḥ ᵛbhavati stutikāmo'smi |
kaḥ ᵛustrāḥ indreṇa daptā gāḥ ᵛvaste ācchādayati |
dhārayatītyarthaḥ |
ᵛkaḥ ᵛindrasya ᵛyujyaṃ sāhāyyaṃ ᵛvaṣṭi icchati |
kaḥ ᵛsakhitvaṃ ᵛko ᵛbhrātraṃ bhrātṛbhāvaṃ vaṣṭi icchati |
ᵛkavaye ⁴ krāntadarśine indrāya ᵛūtī ūtaye tarpaṇāyā ᵛkaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ ||
ko devānāṃrvau adyā vṛṇīte ka ādityā~ aditiṃ jyotirīṭṭe | kasyāśvināvindrau agniḥ sutasyāṃśoḥ pibanti manasāvivenam || 3 || kaḥ | devānām | avaḥ | aṣa | vṛṇīte | kaḥ | ādityān | aditim | jyotiḥ | īṭṭe | kasya | aśvinau | indraḥ | agniḥ | sutasya | aṃśoḥ | pibanti | manasā | avivenam || 3 ||
ᵛatha asmin dine ᵛkaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵛdevānāṃ dyotamānānāmindrādīnām ᵛavaḥ rakṣaṇaṃ ᵛvṛṇīte prārthayate |
ᵛka ᵛādityān devān ᵛaditiṃ devamātaraṃ ca ᵛjyotiḥ udakaṃ ca ᵛīṭṭe yācate |
ᵛaśvinau adhidevau ᵛindraḥ ca ᵛagniḥ ca ᵛmanasā stutyā prītāḥ santaḥ ᵛavivenam avigatakāmam |
yathākāmamityarthaḥ |
ᵛkasya yajamānasya sambandhine ᵛsutasya abhiṣutasya ᵛaṃśoḥ somasya |
soma- mityarthaḥ |
ᵛpibanti pānaṃ kurvanti ||
1. kha-pa-pha-bha-kraḥ naryaḥ | 2. la-manuṣyaḥ nabhyo hitaḥ | 3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ta-na-pa-pha-bha-sa- arpaṇāya | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-kaḥ sakhitvaṃ kaḥ bhrātraṃ bhrātṛttvaṃ ca dhārayatītyarthaḥ | ka indrasya yujyaṃ sāhāyyaṃ vaṣṭi icchati | kaḥ kavaye | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa- devān nānbhi | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha- ca ' nāsti | 7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-devāṃ | 8. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-somam |
tasmā~ abhirbharataḥ śarmaṃ yaṃsujjyokapyātsūryamuccarantam | ya indrāya sunavāmetyāha nare naryāya nṛtamāya nṛṇām || 4 || tasmai | agniḥ | bhārataḥ | śarma | yaṃsat | jyok | paśyāt | sūryam | ut'carantam | yaḥ | indrāya | sunavāma | iti | āha | nare | naryāya | nṛtamāya | nṛṇām || 4 ||
vbhārata: haviṣo bhartāgniḥ |
'prāṇo bhūtvā prajā dhārayan bhārataḥ' iti vājasaneyakam |
vagni: vtasmai yajamānāya vśarma sukhaṃ vyaṃsat yacchet |
kiṃcāyamagniḥ vjyok cirakālam vuccarantam udyantaṃ vsūrya vpaśyāt paśyet |
vyaḥ yajamānaḥ vindrāya vsunavāmetyāha indrārtha somābhiṣavaṃ kuryāmiti bravīti |
kīdṛśāyendrāya |
vnare netre vnaryāya narahitāya vnṛṇāṃ narāṇāṃ netṛṇāṃ madhye vnṛtamāya netṛtamāya ||
na taṃ jinanti bahavo na dabhrā urbasmā aditiḥ śarma yaṃsat | priyaḥ sukṛtpriya indrai manāyuḥ priyaḥ suprāvīḥ priyo asya somī || 5 || na | tam | jinanti | bahavaḥ | na | dabhrāḥ | uru | asmai | aditiḥ | śarma | yaṃsat | priyaḥ | su'kṛt | priyaḥ | indrai | manāyuḥ | priyaḥ | su'prāvīḥ | priyaḥ | asya | somī || 5 ||
vdabhrāḥ alpāḥ vbahavaḥ vna vbahavo'pi śatrujanāḥ vtaṃ yajamānaṃ vna vjinanti na hiṃsantu |
ya indrāya sunavāmetyāha tamiti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
vaditiḥ indramātā vasmai yajamānāya vuru vistīrṇaṃ vśarma sukhaṃ vyaṃsat yacchatu |
kiṃca vindre indrāya vsukṛt śobhanayāgādīnāṃ kartā yajamānaḥ vpriyaḥ bhavati |
vmanāyuḥ indraviṣayastatikāmaścāsyendrasya vpriyaḥ bhavati |
vsuprāvīḥ suṣṭhu prāvitā suṣṭhu pragacchabupagacchan vā yajamāno'syendrasya vpriyaḥ bhavati |
vsomī somavān yajamānaḥ vasya indrasya vpriyaḥ bhavati ||
13 ||
suprāvyaḥ prāśuṣāḻeṣa vīraḥ survaiḥ paktiṃ kṛṇute kevaḻendraḥ | nāsuberāpina sakhā na jāmirduṣprāṇyo'vahantedavācaḥ || 6 || su'pra'avyaḥ | prāśuṣāṭ | eṣaḥ | vīraḥ | su'vaiḥ | paktim | kṛṇute | kevalā | indraḥ | na | asu'veḥ | āpiḥ | na | sakhā | na | jāmiḥ | duḥ'pra'avyaḥ | ava'han | it | avācaḥ || 6 ||

ᵥprāśuṣāt śatrūṇāṃ prakarṣeṇa śīghramibhavitā ᵥvīraḥ vikrāntaḥ ᵥeṣaḥ ᵥindraḥ ᵥsuprādhyaḥ suhu upagacchato havirbhiḥ suṣṭhu pratarpayiturvā ᵥsutveḥ sunvato yajamānasya saṃbandhinoṃ ᵥkevalā kevalamasādhāraṇāṃ ᵥpaktiṃ ᵥkaṇute ātmanaḥ kurute |
kiṃcendraḥ ᵥasukheḥ asuvato'yajamānasya ᵥāpiḥ vyāptaḥ ᵥna bhavati |
indro'yajamānasya ᵥsakhā ᵥna bhavati |
ᵥjāmiḥ bandhuśca ᵥna bhavati |
api ca ᵥduṣprāpyaḥ durupagamanasya ᵥavācaḥ stutirahitasya puruṣasya ᵥavahantat hiṃsitaiva bhavati ||
na revatā paṇinā sukhyāmindro'sunvatā sutapāḥ saṃ gṛṇīte |
āsya vedaḥ khidati hanti nugraṃ vi sunvaye pakteye kevalo bhūt ||
7 ||
na | revatā | paṇinā | sakhyam | indraḥ | asunvatā | sutapāḥ | sam | gṛṇīte | ā | asya | vedaḥ | khidati | hanti | nugram | vi | sunvaye | pakteye | kevalaḥ | bhūt || 7 ||
ᵥsutapāḥ abhiṣutaṃ somaṃ piban ᵥindraḥ ᵥrevatā dhanavatā ᵥpaṇinā vaṇijā lubdhena ᵥasunvatā somābhipavamakurvatā ayajamānena saha ᵥsakhyaṃ sakhitvaṃ ᵥna ᵥsaṃ ᵥgṛṇīte na saṃstauti |
nānumanyata ityarthaḥ |
kiṃca ᵥasya ayajamānasya ᵥnagaṃ nirarthakaṃ ᵥvedaḥ dhanaṃ jñānaṃ vā ᵥā ᵥkhidati uddharati |
ᵥhanti hinasti ca |
api ca ᵥsunvaye somaṃ sunvate ᵥpakteye havīṃṣi pacate yajamānāya ᵥkevalaḥ asādhāraṇaḥ san ᵥvi ᵥbhūt vibhavati ||
indraṃ pare'vare madhyamāsa indraṃ yānto'vasitāsa indram |
indraṃ kṣiyanta uta yudhyamānā indraṃ naro vājayantau havante ||
8 ||

ᵥpare utkṛṣṭāḥ ᵥavare nikṛṣṭāśca janāḥ ᵥindraṃ ᵥhavante āhvayanti |
ᵥmadhyamāsaḥ madhyamāśca ᵥindram eva havante |
ᵥyāntaḥ kāryasiddhayarthaṃ gacchanto janāḥ ᵥindramevāhvayanti |
ᵥavasitāsaḥ nivi- ṣṭāśca ᵥindram evāhvayanti |
ᵥkṣiyantaḥ gṛhe nivasanto janāḥ ᵥindram eva havante |

14 ||
‘ahaṃ manuḥ’ iti saptarcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣam |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—‘ahaṃ manuḥ saptādhābhistasṛbhirindramivātmānamṛṣistaṣṭāvendro vātmānaṃ parā navāśau vā śyenastutiḥ’ iti |
1. la-sakhā sahāyaśca | 2. ca-‘janāḥ’ nāsti | 3. ca-pha-indrameva | 4. kha-ga-ṇa-na-bha-janāḥ jayārtham | 5. ca-udyogino janāḥ jayārthaṃ ca |
atra 'ahaṃ manuḥ' ityādyabhistṛbhirātmānamindrarūpeṇa vāmadevaḥ stutavān |
yadvā |
indra eva ātmānaṃ stutavān |
ato vāmadevavākyapakṣe vāmadeva ṛṣirendro devatā |
indravākyapakṣe'śvindra ṛṣiḥ paramātmā devatā |
triṣṭup chandaḥ |
avaśiṣṭānāma vāmadeva ṛṣiḥ |
avaśiṣṭābhistaruttasūktena ca suparṇatmanā brahma stūyate |
atastāsāṃ parabrahma devatā |
iyenanāmyyekāhe mādhyandinasavane marutvatīyaśastre idaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—'iyenājirābhyāmibhiranyajetāhaṃ manurbheme nu san' (ābha. śrau. 9. 7) iti ||
ahaṃ manurabhavaṃ sūryaścāhaṃ kakṣīvā~ ṛṣirasmi vipraḥ |
ahaṃ kutsamārcuneyaṃ nyuñje'haṃ kaviruśanā paśyatā mā ||
1 ||
aham | manuḥ | abhavam | sūryaḥ | ca | aham | kakṣīvān | ṛṣiḥ | asmi | vipraḥ | aham |
adāt |
kiṃca ᵥahaṃ ᵥvāvaśānāḥ śabdāyamānāḥ ᵥapaḥ udakāni ᵥanayaṃ sarvamapi pradeśam agamayam |
ᵥdevāsaḥ vahnyādayo devāḥ ᵥmama ᵥketaṃ madīyaṃ saṃkalpam ᵥanu ᵥbhāyan anuyanti ||
ahaṃ puro mandasāno vyairaṃ nava sākaṃ na̭vatīḥ śambarasya |
śatatamaṃ veśyaṃ sarvatātā divodāsamatathigvaṃ yadāvam ||
3 ||
aham | puraḥ | mandasānaḥ | vi | airam | nava | sākam | navatīḥ | śambarasya | śata'tamam | veśyam | sarva'tātā | divaḥ'dāsam | atithi'gvam | yat | āvam || 3 ||
ᵥahaṃ vāmadeva indro vā ᵥmandasānaḥ somena mādyan ᵥnava navasaṃkhyākāḥ ᵥnavatīḥ navati- saṃkhyākāśca ᵥśambarasya etannāmakasyāsurasya saṃbandhīni ᵥpuraḥ purāṇi ᵥsākaṃ yugapadeva ᵥvyairam adhvaṃsayam |
ᵥśatatamaṃ śatasaṃkhyāpūraṇaṃ puraṃ ᵥveśyaṃ divodāsanāmne praveśārhamakaravam |
ᵥsarvatātā sarvatātau yajñe ᵥatithigvam atithīnāmabhigantāraṃ ᵥdivodāmaṃ divodāsanāmakaṃ rājarṣi ᵥyata yadā ᵥāvam apālayam |
tadā puro ᵥvyairamiti ᵥpūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
pra su ṣa vibhyo maruto viraṃstu pra śyenaḥ śyenebhya āśuptvā |

pra | su | saḥ | vi'bhyaḥ | marutaḥ | vi | astu | pra | śyenaḥ | śyenebhyaḥ | āśu'ptvā | acakrayā | yat | svadhayā | su'parṇaḥ | havyam | bharat | manave | deva'juṣṭam || 4 ||
athaitadādibhiḥ śyenaḥ stūyate |

kiṃca ᵥśyenebhyaḥ śaṃsanīya- gamanebhyaḥ svasamānajātibhyaḥ pakṣibhyaḥ sakāśāt ᵥāśuptvā āśupatataḥ śīghragāmī ᵥśyenaḥ prakṛṣṭo bhavatu |

‘ garbhaṃ nu ’ iti pañcacaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham |

‘tvā yujā ’ iti pañcakaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham indrāsomadevatākam |

‘ā naḥ stutaḥ ’ iti pañcarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |

viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
ā naḥ stuta upa vājairbhūtī indraṃ yāhi harirbhirmandasānaḥ | tiraścidarthaḥ sarvanā puruṇyāṅguṣebhirguṇānaḥ || 1 || ā | naḥ | stutaḥ | upa | vājaibhiḥ | ūtī | indra | yāhi | harirbhiḥ | mandasānaḥ | tiraḥ | cit | arthaḥ | sarvanā | puruṇi | āṅguṣebhiḥ | guṇānaḥ || 1 ||
he ᵥindra ᵥstutaḥ tvaṃ ᵥvājaibhiḥ vājaiḥ saha ᵥtiraścita prāsāni |

kīdṛśaḥ |
ᵥmanda- sānaḥ modamānaḥ ᵥarthaḥ svāmī ᵥāṅguṣebhiḥ stotraiḥ ᵥguṇānaḥ stūyamānaḥ ᵥsatyarādhāḥ satyadhanaḥ ||
ā hi ṣmā yāti naryaiścikitvān hūyamānaḥ sotṛbhirupa yajñam | svaśvo yo abhīrurmanyamānaḥ suṣvāṇebhirmadati saṃ ha vīraiḥ || 2 || ā | hi | sma | yāti | naryaiḥ | ᵥcikitvān | hūyamānaḥ | sotṛbhiḥ | upa | yajñam | su'aśvaḥ | yaḥ | abhīruḥ | manyamānaḥ | suṣvānebhiḥ | madati | sam | ha | vīraiḥ || 2 ||
ᵥnaryaḥ nṛtyo hitaḥ ᵥcikitvān sarvaṃ jānan ᵥsotṛbhiḥ somamabhiṣavadbhirṛtvigbhiḥ ᵥhūyamānaḥ āhūyamāna indro'smadīyaṃ ᵥyajñam ᵥupa prati ᵥā ᵥyāti āgacchatu |
ᵥhi ᵥsma iti pūraṇī |

sa indra āgacchatviti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
