7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-bhavatām; na-teṣām |
8. ta-la-sukarmā śobhanakarmā |
devānāṃ sarveṣāṃ sambandhī 'bhavat |
tathā 'indrasya sambandhī 'ṛbhukṣāḥ 'ṛbhuyeṣṭhaḥ |
'viśvā madhyamaḥ 'varuṇasya saṃvandhyabhavat |
'hi 'pūraṇaḥ ? ||
ye harīṃ medhayokthā mada̭nta indrāya cakruḥ suyujā ye aśvā̭ |
te rāyaspoṣaṃ draviṇānyasme dhatta ṛbhavaḥ kṣemayanto na mitram ||
10 ||
ye | harī iti | medhayā | ukthā | mada̭ntaḥ | indrāya | cakruḥ | suyujā̭ | ye | aśvā̭ | te | rāyaḥ | poṣa̭m | draviṇāni | asme iti | dhatta | ṛbhavaḥ | kṣema̭yantaḥ | na | mitram || 10 ||
'ye 'ṛbhavaḥ 'harī 'aśvau 'medhayā 'prajñayā 'ukthā 'uvarthaḥ 'stutibhiḥ 'madantaḥ 'harṣayantaḥ 'cakruḥ |
'ye 'ca 'tau 'suyujā 'suyujau 'suyojanāvaśvā 'indrāya 'indrārtha 'cakruḥ 'akurvan |
'he 'ṛbhavaḥ 'te 'yūyaṃ 'rāyaspoṣaṃ 'dhanapuṣṭiṃ 'draviṇāni 'gatrādidhanāni 'ca 'sukhāni 'vā 'amme 'asmāsu 'dhatta 'dhārayata |
kimiva |
'kṣemayantaḥ 'kṣemamicchantaḥ 'mitraṃ 'na 'mitramiva |
'atra 'yathāyeka 'eva 'ṛbhunāmā 'tatsambandhāta 'itare'pyabhava 'ucyante 'prāṇa'nyāyena ||
idāhvaḥ pītimuta vo madaṃ dhuna ṛte śrāntasya maruyārya devāḥ |
te nunamasme ṛbhavo vasvani tṛtīye asminsarvane dadhāta ||
11 ||
idā | ahaḥ | pītim | uta | vaḥ | madam | dhuḥ | na | ṛte | śrāntasya | sudhyārya | devāḥ | te | nunam | asme iti | ṛbhavaḥ | vasvani | tṛtīye | asmin | sarvane | dadhāta || 11 ||
'idā 'idānīṃ 'camādirnimāṇānantaram 'ahaḥ 'sutyāhasambandhinamṛtīye 'savane 'pīti 'somapānam 'uta 'api 'ca 'madaṃ 'tajjanitaṃ 'vaḥ 'yuṣmākaṃ 'dhuḥ 'dadhurdevāḥ |
'śrāntasya 'śrāntapoṣayuktāna 'ṛte 'satyāya 'saccitvāya 'na 'bhavanti 'devāḥ |
'yata 'ete 'śrāntā 'ato 'dadhuḥ 'ityarthaḥ |
'he 'ṛbhavaḥ 'te 'mahanto 'yūyaṃ 'nunaṃ 'niścayama 'asme 'asmāsu 'asmin 'tṛtīye 'savane 'vasvani 'dhanāni 'dadhāta 'dhārayata ||
‘ ṛbhuvibhvā ’ ityekādaśarcaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevamārṣaṃ ṛbhubhamārbhavam |
‘ ṛbhuvibhvā ’ ityanukramaṇikā |
vyūḍhe daśarātre pañcame'hani vaiśvadevaśastre ābhavaniṣiddhānītyidam |
‘ ṛbhuvibhvā stupe janam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 8 ) iti sūtritatvāt ||
1. kha-ga-ta-na-la-sa- ‘ hi pūraṇaḥ ’ nāsti |
2. kha-ga-gha-ṇa-ta-na-pa-pha-bha-sa- ‘ prajñayā ’ nāsti |
3. kha-ga-ṇa-na-bha-ma-suyojamānāśvāḥ; gha-ca-pa-pha-ta-suyojamānāśvāḥ |
4 kha-ga-gha-ṇa-pa-pha-bha- sa-śrāntāya śramayuktāya, na-śrāntāya tapojuktāya |
ṛbhurvibhvā vāja indro no acchemaṃ yajñaṃ ratnadheyopa yāta | idā hi vo dhiṣaṇā devyādhārtpītiṃ saṃ madā agmatā vaḥ || 1 || ṛbhuḥ | tri'bhvā | vājaḥ | indraḥ | naḥ | acche | imam | yajñam | ratna'dhayā | upa | yāta | idā | hi | vaḥ | dhiṣaṇā | devī | ahārm | ardhāt | pītim | sam | madāḥ | agmata | vaḥ ||1||
ṛbhurvibhvā vāja indraḥ caite devāḥ naḥ asmadīyama imaṃ yajñam acche abhimukhaṃ ratnadheyā asmabhyaṃ ratnadhānāya upa yāta upagacchata |
nanu indraḥ somasvāmitvāt āgacchatu kathameteṣāṃ yajñaprāptiriti cet |
ucyate |
idā hi idānīṃ khalu vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ dhiṣaṇā vāk devī prajāpateḥ saṃbandhinya ahārm somābhipavamabandhinyāṃ pāti somapānam adhāta |
ya prajāpatira- brahmaṇvitāraṃ tatra vā ime'ntevāmāstvamevabhiḥ saṃpraśva ' (e. vā. 3.30) iti brāhmaṇam |
ya
he ṛbhavaḥ vaḥ yuṣmadartham ayaṃ yajñaḥ akāri |
yaṃ yajñaṃ manuṣat manuṣyavat pradivaḥ prakarṣeṇa dyotamānāḥ santaḥ ā dadhidhve dhārayata jaṭhare |
tadarthaṃ vaḥ yuṣmān accha abhimukhyena jujupānāsaḥ sevamānāḥ somāḥ pra asthuḥ pratighnante |
uta api ca dhārayitvā ca ha vājāḥ ṛbhavaḥ viśve yūyam agriyā agrārhā agratvasaṃpādino vā abhūta bhavatha||
abhūdu vo vidhate ratnadheyamidā naro dāśuṣe martyāya |
pibata vājā ṛbhavo dade vo mahiṃ tṛtīyaṃ sarvarnaṃ madāya ||
4 ||
abhūt | ū~ iti | vaḥ | vidhate | ratna'dheyam | idā | naraḥ | dāśuṣe | martyāya | pibata | vājāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | dade | vaḥ | mahi | tṛtīyam | sarvarnam | madāya || 4 ||
he naraḥ netāra ṛbhavaḥ vaḥ yuṣmākamanugrahāt idā idānīṃ tṛtīye savane ratnadheyaṃ dātavyaṃ ranaṃ vidhate | vidhatiḥ paricaraṇakarmā | stutyā paricarate dāśuṣe havirdattavate martyāya yajamānāya mahyam abhūta bhavatu | tadarthaṃ he vājāḥ ṛbhavaḥ | etadvayaṃ vivacanāmasyāpyupalakṣaṇam | itarā- pekṣayā pratyekaṃ bahuvacanam | yūyaṃ pibata | tadarthaṃ vaḥ dade | kim | mahi mahaprabhṛtaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanam | tatsambandhinaṃ somamityarthaḥ | kimartham | madāya harṣāya tṛptaye vā ||
ā vāṃjā yātāpaṃ na ṛbhukṣā maho naro draviṇasā gṛṇānāḥ |
ā vaḥ pītayo'bhipitve ahrāmimā astai navasveiva gamn ||
5 ||
ā | vājāḥ | yāta | upa | naḥ | ṛbhukṣāḥ | mahaḥ | naraḥ | draviṇasaḥ | gṛṇānāḥ | ā | vaḥ | pītayaḥ | abhi'pitve | ahrām | imāḥ | astam | nava'sve'iva | gamn || 5 ||
he vājāḥ ṛbhukṣāḥ naraḥ netāro yūyaṃ naḥ asmān upa ā yāta | kiṃ kurvantaḥ | mahaḥ mahataḥ draviṇasaḥ dhanasya || karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhyau || mahat draviṇaṃ gṛṇānāḥ stuvantaḥ | kiṃca ahrām abhipitve abhipatane samāsau | tṛtīye savane ityarthaḥ | vaḥ yuṣmān imāḥ pītayaḥ pānāni ā gamn āgacchanti | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | astam gṛhanāmaṃtat | gṛhaṃ navasvaḥ navaprasavā gāvaḥ iva || || 3 ||
ā napātaḥ śavaso yātanopemaṃ yajñaṃ narmasā hayamānāḥ |
sajoṣasaḥ sūryo yasya ca stha madhvaḥ pāta ratnadhā indravantaḥ ||
6 ||
ā | napātaḥ | śavasaḥ | yātana | upa | imam | yajñam | narmasā | hayamānāḥ | sa'joṣasaḥ | sūryaḥ | yasya | ca | stha | madhvaḥ | pāta | ratna'dhāḥ | indra'vantaḥ || 6 ||
1. kha-ga-gha-ṇa-bha-pradādhidhve; ca-prādadhidhve |
2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-ā asthuḥ; na-sa-asthuḥ |
3. na-la-sa- 'aśmāna' nāsti |
ha 'śavasaḥ 'napātaḥ balasya na pātayitārastasya putrā vā |
balavanta ityarthaḥ |
yūyaṃ 'namasā stotreṇa 'hyamānāḥ ākāritāḥ santaḥ 'hyamaṃ 'yajñam 'upa 'ā 'yātana upāgacchata |
kīdṛśā yūyam |
'sajopasaḥ indreṇa saha prītāḥ |
'murayaḥ medhāvinaḥ |
dvandvasya caiṣāṃ ca kaḥ prasaṅga ityucyate |
'yasya 'ca 'mya |
ceti prasiddhau |
yasyendrasya saṃbandhino yūyaṃ bhavatha taducitāśvanirmāṇāt' ityabhi- prāyaḥ |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāt 'indravantaḥ prītena indreṇa tadvantaḥ 'ratnadhāḥ ramaṇīyadhanānāṃ dātāro yūyaṃ 'madhvaḥ madhuraṃ somaṃ 'piva pivata ||
sajoṣā indra varuṇena somaṃ sajoṣāḥ pāhi girvaṇo murudbhiḥ |
agrepābhirṛtupābhiḥ sajoṣā grāpastanībhī ratnadhābhiḥ sajoṣāḥ ||
7 ||
sa'joṣāḥ | indra | varuṇena | somam | sa'joṣāḥ | pāhi | girvaṇaḥ | murudbhiḥ | agre'pābhiḥ | ṛtu'pābhiḥ | sa'joṣāḥ | grāḥ'patnībhiḥ | ratna'dhābhiḥ | sa'joṣāḥ || 7 ||
ha 'indra 'tvaṃ 'varuṇena rājyabhimānidevena 'sajoṣāḥ samānaprītiḥ san 'somaṃ 'pāhi piva |
ha 'girvaṇaḥ gīrbhirvandanīya saṃbhajanīyendra 'tvaṃ 'marudbhiḥ 'sajoṣāḥ saṃgataḥ san somaṃ 'pāhi piva ||
kiṃca 'agrepābhiḥ prathamapātṛbhiḥ 'ṛtupābhiḥ ṛtuyājadevaiśca 'grāḥ'patnībhiḥ |
strīṇāṃ pālayitryo devyo grāḥ'patnyaḥ |
tābhiḥ |
' 'menā grā iti strīṇām' (niru. 3. 21) iti niruktam |
'ratnadhābhiḥ ramaṇīyadhanadātṛbhirṛbhubhiḥ saha piva |
yadvā |
etana grāḥ'patnībhirtyasya viśeṣaṇam |
tābhirvā 'sajoṣāḥ somaṃ piva ||
sajorpasa ādityairmādayadhvaṃ sajorpasa ṛbhavaḥ parvatebhiḥ |
sajorṣo daivyena savitrā sajorṣaḥ sindhubhī ratnadhobhiḥ ||
8 ||
sa'jorpasaḥ | ādityaiḥ | mādayadhvam | sa'jorpasaḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | parvatebhiḥ | sa'jorṣaḥ | daivyena | savitrā | sa'jorṣaḥ | sindhubhiḥ | ratna'dhobhiḥ || 8 ||
ha 'ṛbhavaḥ 'ādityaiḥ 'sajopasaḥ saṃgatā yūyaṃ 'mādayadhvam |
tathā 'parvatebhiḥ parvadbhiḥ parvaṇyarcyamānadevaviśeṣaiḥ 'sajopasaḥ mādayadhvam |
tathā ca 'daivyena devebhyo hitena 'savitrā 'sajopasaḥ 'ratnadhebhiḥ ratnānāṃ dātṛbhiḥ 'sindhubhiḥ syandanasvabhāvairdevaiśca 'sajopasaḥ' mādayadhvam ||
1. la-sa--taducitāśva nirmāṇāt |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa pha-bha-tena |
3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-ramaṇīyānāṃ dhanānām |
ye aśvino ye pitarā ya ūtī dhenuṃ tatakṣurbhuvo ye aśvāḥ | ye asaṃtrā ya ṛdhugrodasī ye vibhvo naraḥ svapatyāni cakruḥ || 9 || ye | aśvino | ye | pitarā | ye | ūtī | dhenum | tatakṣuḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | ye | aśvāḥ | ye | asaṃtrā | ye | ṛdhak | rodasī iti | ye | vi'bhvaḥ | naraḥ | su'apatyāni | cakruḥ ||9||
'ye ṛbhavaḥ' 'aśvinā aśvinau' 'ūtī ūtyā kriyayā rathanirmāṇarūpayā' aprīṇayan |
'ye ca' 'pitarā pitarau' 'tatakṣuḥ takṣaṇena sampādayan' 'jānāṃ santāvarttī ūyā yuvānau' cakruḥ |
'ye ca' 'dhenuṃ' tatakṣuḥ mṛtāyāḥ punarnavīkaraṇena |
'ye ca' 'aśvā aśvau' tatakṣuḥ |
'niśramaṇe gāmaraṇīta dhītibhiryā jarantā yuvaśā tākṛṇotana' (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 161. 7) ityāyuktam |
'ye ca' 'aṃsatrā aṃsatrāṇi kavacāni devebhyaścakruḥ |
'ye ca' 'rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau' 'ṛdhaka pṛthak' cakruḥ |
evamuktaprakāreṇa 'ye' 'vibhvaḥ' vyāptā ṛbhavaḥ 'naraḥ' 'netāraḥ' 'svapatyāni svapatanasādhanāni tatrāptisādhanāni vā karmāṇi cakruḥ |
'ne'preṣāḥ' ' ityuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ ||
ye gomantaṃ vājavantaṃ suvīraṃ rayiṃ dhattha vasumataṃ purukṣum | te aṃgrepā ṛbhavo mandasānā asme dhatta ye ca rātiṃ gṛṇanti || 10 || ye | go'mantam | vāja'vantam | su'vīram | rayim | dhattha | vasu'mantam | puru'kṣum | te | agre'pāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | mandasānāḥ | asme iti | dhatta | ye | ca | rātim | gṛṇanti || 10 ||
he ṛbhavaḥ 'ye yūyaṃ 'gomantaṃ 'gomahitaṃ 'vājavantaṃ annavantaṃ 'suvīraṃ śobhanaputropetaṃ 'vasumataṃ nivāsayogyagṛhādidhanopetaṃ 'purukṣuṃ bahudhanaṃ 'atha dhārayatha mahyaṃ dātum |
he ṛbhavaḥ 'te yūyaṃ 'agrepāḥ prathamaṃ pānāraḥ 'mandamānāḥ mādyantaḥ santaḥ 'agme asmabhyaṃ 'dhatta datta rayim' |
'ye 'ca 'rātiṃ dānaṃ datta somaṃ vā 'gṛṇanti stuvanti |
'te' dhatta |
yadvā |
ye ca rātiṃ gṛṇanti yajamānāḥ tebhyo'pi dhatta ||
nāpābhūta na vo'tītṛpāmāniḥśastā ṛbhavo yajñe asmin | saminreṇa mardatha saṃ marudbhiḥ saṃ rājabhī ratnadheyāya devāḥ || 11 || na | apāḥ | abhūta | na | vaḥ | atītṛpāma | aniḥśastāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | yajñe | asmin | sam | indreṇa | mardatha | sam | marudbhiḥ | sam | rājabhī | ratna'dheyāya | devāḥ || 11 ||
1. sva-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pha-bha-sa-nirmāṇarūpayā |
2. sva-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-viruddhaṃ kramaṃ |
3. sva- ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-ye ca nare netāraḥ vibhvaḥ ṛbhavaḥ svapatyāni suprajāḥ cakruḥ te ame pāhi (agrepāḥ) ityuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ |
4. sva-ga-ca-ṇa-na bha-sa-gohitam |
5. kha-na-ma-'aśvavantaṃ' nāsti; ga-ca-ṇa- pa-pha-bha-balavantam; ta-la-annavantaṃ balavantam |
6. sva-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-la-datta; ma-dadātu |
7. sva-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-ma-'rayiṃ' nāsti; na-ṛṣim |
8. sva-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-tebhyaḥ |
he ^ṛbhavaḥ yūyaṃ ^nāpābhūta nāpagacchata^ |
aparaktā na bhavata |
vayaṃ ca ^vaḥ yuṣmān ^na ^anītṛpāma atyantaṃ tṛptāna mā karavāma |
ato he ^ṛbhavaḥ ^devāḥ ^aniḥśastāḥ ^aninditāḥ santaḥ ^asmin ^yajñe ^indreṇa ^saṃ ^madatha ^marudbhiḥ ^saṃ ^madatha |
^rājabhiḥ rājamānairanyairdevaiḥ ^saṃ ^madatha tṛptā bhavata |
kimartham |
^ratnadheyāya ramaṇīyāya dhanadānāya |
4 |
‘ihopa yāta’ iti navacaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamārbhavam |
‘ihopa nava’ ityanu- kramaṇikā |
tṛtīyasavane maitrāvaruṇasyedamṛjhīyamānasūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā tvā vahantvasāvi devamihopa yātetyanuvacanam’ (āśva. śrau. 5. 5) iti |
ihopa yāta śavaso napātaḥ saudhanvanā ṛbhavo māpa bhūta |
asminhi vaḥ sarvane ratnadheyaṃ gamantvindramanu vo madāsaḥ |
1 |
iha | upa | yāta | śavasaḥ | napātaḥ | saudhanvanāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | mā | apa | bhūta | asmin | hi | vaḥ | sarvane | ratna'dheyam | gamantu | indram | anu | vaḥ | madāsaḥ || 1 ||
he ^śavaso ^napātaḥ balasya putrāstasya na pātayitāro vā he ^saudhanvanāḥ sudhanvanaḥ putrāḥ he ^ṛbhavaḥ yūyam ^ihopa ^yāta asmin tṛtīyasavane upagacchata |
^māpa ^bhūta apagatā na bhavata |
^asmin ^savane ^ratnadheyaṃ ramaṇīyadhanasya dātāram ^indramanu anusṛtya ^vaḥ yuṣmān ^madāsaḥ madakarāḥ somāḥ ^gamantu gacchantu |
anviti kriyayā vā saṃbadhyate |
indraṃ yuṣmānśvāgacchantu ityarthaḥ |
^hi pūraṇaḥ |
ārgnbhṛṇāmiha ratnadheyabhūtasomasya sudhutasya pītiḥ |
sukṛtyā yatsvapasyāyā~ ca ekaṃ vicakra camasaṃ caturdhā |
2 |
ā | agn | ṛbhuṇām | iha | ratna'dheyam | abhūta | somasya | su'sutasya | pītiḥ | su'kṛtyā | yat | su'apasyāyā | ca | ekam | vi'cakra | camasam | catuḥ'dhā || 2 ||
^ṛbhuṇāṃ yuṣmākam ^iha tṛtīyasavane ^ratnadheyaṃ ratnadānam ^āgan āgacchatu mahyam |
yadvā |
yuṣmākameva somasya ratnamāgan |
yasmāt ^somasya ^suputasya ^pītiḥ pānam ^abhūta prajāpatinā labdhamityarthaḥ |
tadapi kuta iti cet |
ucyate |
^yat yasmāt ^sukṛtyā śobhanahastavyāpāreṇa ^svapasyāyā śobhanarathanirmāṇādikarmecchayā ^ca ^ekaṃ santaṃ ^camasaṃ ^caturdhā ^vicakra kṛtavantaḥ |
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- ‘he ṛbhavaḥ yūyaṃ’ nāsti |
2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-ta-na-bha-la-sa- ‘nāpa- gacchata’ nāsti |
3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha- ‘saṃ madatha’ nāsti |
4. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-la-sa-indram |
5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ta-na-bha-la-sa-ratnamāgamata |
vyakṛṇota camasaṃ caturdhā sakhe vi śikṣatyabravīt |
athaita vājā amṛtasya pantho gaṇaṃ devānāṃmabhavaḥ suhastāḥ ||
3 ||
vi | akṛṇota | camasam | catuḥ'dhā | sakhe | vi | śikṣa | iti | abravīt | artha | eta | vājāḥ | amṛtasya | panthām | gaṇam | devānām | ṛbhavaḥ | su'hastāḥ || 3 ||
he ṛbhavo yūyaṃ camasaṃ caturdhā vyakṛṇota vyakuruta |
kṛtvā ca he sakhe he sakhibhūtāme vi śikṣa anugṛhāṇa somapānam ityabravīta avadata yūyam |
atha mo'priyaṃmānavādīt khalu |
kimiti |
he vājāḥ ṛbhavaḥ suhastāḥ kuśalahastā yūyam amṛtasya amaraṇadharmakasya svargasya panthāṃ panthānam eta gacchata |
kiṃ tadamṛtamiti |
devānām indrādīnāṃ gaṇaṃ saṃghānamaita ||
kiṃmayaḥ sivcamasa eṣa āsa yaṃ kāvyena caturśa vicakra |
athā sunudhvaṃ sarvainaṃ madāya pāta ṛbhavo madhunaḥ somyasya ||
4 ||
kim'mayaḥ | sivat | camasaḥ | eṣaḥ | āsa | yam | kāvyena | caturaḥ | vi'cakra | artha | sunudhvam | sarvainam | madāya | pāta | ṛbhavaḥ | madhunaḥ | somyasya || 4 ||
kiṃmayaḥ sivat kimātmakaḥ | eṣaḥ camasaḥ | āsa | sivat praśne | yaṃ camasamekaṃ mantaṃ kāvyena kavīnāṃ medhāvināṃ saṃbandhinā karmaṇā kauśalena caturaḥ vicakra kṛtavantaḥ | atha adhunā savanam | sūyate iti savanaḥ somaḥ | taṃ madāya harṣāya sunudhvaṃ he ṛtvijaḥ | he ṛbhavaḥ yūyaṃ ca somyasya somasaṃbandhinaṃ madhunaḥ madhurasya pānaṃ pivata ||
śacyākarta pitarā yuvāṃnā śacyākarta camasaṃ devapānaṃ |
śacyā harī dhanutarāvataaindravāhāvṛbhayo vājaratnāḥ ||
5 ||
śacyā | akarta | pitarā | yuvāṃnā | śacyā | akarta | camasam | deva'pānam | śacyā | harī iti | dhanu'tarau | ataṣṭa | indra'vāhau | ṛbhavaḥ | vājaratnāḥ || 5
1. kha-ga-ca-na-pa-pha-ma-he vājāḥ suhastāḥ śobhanahastopetaḥ ṛbhavā vo yūyaṃ camasaṃ caturdhā vyakṛṇota vyakuruta |
kṛtvā ca he sakhe sakhibhūtāme viśikṣate ( na-bha-viśikṣa ) anugraheṇa somapānamiti abravīta ( na-abravīta ) avadata |
atha yūyamamṛtasyāmaraṇadharmakasya svargasya panthā panthānamabhigacchata |
ki tadamṛta- miti devānāmindrādīnāṃ gaṇaṃ saṃghānamaita prāptāḥ ( na- prāptāḥ ' nāsti ) |
2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- ' āsa ' nāsti |
3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- ' madhāvinā ' nāsti |
4. kha-ṇa-ma-madhurasama |
he vājiratnā: ramaṇīyasomānnā he ṛbhava: yūyaṃ śacyā karmaṇā pitarā mātāpitarau yuvānā yuvānau akarta akurata |
tathā śacyā camasaṃ devapānaṃ devapānāīma akarta caturdhā- kurutetyartha: |
śacyā harī aśvau ataṣṭa takṣaṇena saṃpāditavantaḥ |
kīdṛśau harī |
dhanutarau śīghraṃ ganturau indravāho indravoḍhārau ||
5 ||
yo va: sunotyabhipitve ahnāṃ tīvraṃ vājāsa: sarvaṃ madāya |
tasmai rayimṛbhava: sarvavīramā takṣata vṛṣaṇo mandasānā: ||
6 ||
ya: | va: | sunoti | abhi'pitve | ahnām | tīvram | vājāsa: | sarvam | madāya | tasmai | rayim | ṛbhava: | sarva'vīram | ā | takṣata | vṛṣaṇa: | mandasānā: || 6 ||
he ṛbhava: vājāsa: annavantaḥ va: yuṣmabhyaṃ ya: yajamāna: ahnām abhipitve abhipatane |
tṛtīyasavane ityartha: |
tasmin tīvraṃ ramavattaraṃ savanam |
sūyate iti savana: soma: |
taṃ madāya sunoti tasmai sarvavīraṃ bahuputropetaṃ rayiṃ dhanaṃ ha vṛṣaṇa: phalavarṣitāra: mandasānā: modamānā: mantaḥ ā takṣata saṃpādayatetyartha: ||
prāta: sutamapibo haryaśva mādhyaṃdinaṃ sarvaṃ kevalaṃ te |
samubhubhi: pibasva ratnadhebhi: sakhīryā indra cakape sukṛtyā ||
7 ||
prātariti | sutam | apiba: | hari'aśva | mādhyandinam | sarvam | kevalam | te | sam | ṛbhu'bhi: | pibasva | ratna'dhebhi: | sakhīn | yān | indra | cakaṣe | su'kṛtyā ||7||
he haryaśva haritavarṇāśvopetendra prāta: prāta:savane sutam abhiputaṃ somam apiba: |
mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanaṃ kevalaṃ te paraṃ tavaiva nānyeṣām |
savanadvayabhya pṛthagabhidhānāttṛtīyasavanaṃ pariśiṣyate |
tṛtīyasavane ratnadhebhi: ramaṇīyadānai: ṛbhubhi: saṃ pibasva |
he indra yān ṛbhūn sukṛtyā śobhanakarmaṇā sakhīn cakaṣe akaro: ||
ye devāso abhaṃvatā sukṛtyā śyenāivedādhiṃ divi niṣeda |
te ratnaṃ dhāta śavaso napātaḥ saudhanvanā abhaṃvatāmṛtāsa: ||
8 ||
ye | devāsa: | abhaṃvata | su'kṛtyā | śyenā:'iva | it | adhi | divi | ni'seda | te | ratnam | dhāta | śavasa: | napātaḥ | saudhanvanā: | abhaṃvata | amṛtāsa: || 8 ||
1. na-la-sa- 'aśvau' nāsti |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- 'taṃ' nāsti |
he ṛbhavaḥ ! ye yūyaṃ sukṛtyā śobhanakarmaṇā devāḥ abhavata abhūta |
yenāhveta śaṃsanīyagatayo gṛdhraviśeṣā iva divi dyuloke adhi niṣed abhinipaṇṇāḥ he śavasāṃ napātaḥ balavantaḥ te yūyaṃ ratnaṃ dhanaṃ dhāna prayacchata |
he saudhanvanāḥ sudhanvanḥ putrāḥ amṛtāmaḥ amṛtā devāḥ abhavata ||
yattṛtīyaṃ sarvataṃ ratnadheyamarkaṇudhvaṃ svapasyā suhastāḥ |
tadabhavaḥ pariṣiktaṃ va etataṃ madaibhirindriyebhiḥ pibadhvam ||
9 ||
yat | tṛtīyam | sarvatam | ratnadheyam | arkaṇudhvam | suapasyā | suhastāḥ | tat | ṛbhavaḥ | pari'siktam | vaḥ | etat | sam | madaibhiḥ | indriyebhiḥ | pibadhvam ||9||
he suhastāḥ śobhanahastāḥ ṛbhavaḥ ratnadheyaṃ ramaṇīyamāmadānavat yattṛtīyaṃ savanam akuṇudhvaṃ prasādhitavantaḥ |
svapasyā śobhanakarmaṇā tat etat savanaṃ savanagataṃ somadravyaṃ paripiktaṃ pariṣecanavat vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ sambandhabhiḥ madaibhiḥ mādyadbhiḥ indriyebhiḥ indriyaiḥ saṃ pibadhvam |
yadyapi vadanameva pānasādhanaṃ tathāpi cakṣuḥśrotrādīnāmapi darśanaśravaṇādinā tṛptisañjāvāna pātratvamupacaryate ||
6 ||
'anaśvo jātaḥ' iti navacaṃ caturtha sūktamābhavam |
ṛṣivārmādevaḥ |
anayā triṣṭup śiṣṭāṣṭipṛṣṭavanta- paribhāṣayā jagatyaḥ |
'anaśvo'nyā triṣṭup' ityanukramaṇikā |
ābhilavike tṛtīye'hani vaiśvadeva- śastre ābhavanirvidghānīyamidam |
tathā ca sūtritam-'anaśvo jātaḥ pavatau ya iti vaiśvadevam' ( āśva. śrau. 7. 7 ) iti ||
anaśvo jāno anabhīśurukthyo rathastricakraḥ pari vartate rajaḥ |
mahattadvai devasya pravācanaṃ cāmṛtabhavaḥ pṛthivīṃ yacca puṣyatha ||
1 ||
anaśvaḥ | jātaḥ | anabhīśuḥ | ukthyaḥ | rathaḥ | tri'cakraḥ | pari | vartate | rajaḥ | mahat | tat | vaḥ | devasya | pra'vācanam | dhāma | ṛbhavaḥ | pṛthivīm | yat | ca | puṣyatha ||1||
he ṛbhavaḥ aśvinoḥ sambandhī yuṣmaddat rathaḥ anaśvā jātaḥ vāhanānirapekṣaḥ saṃpannaḥ |
tathā anabhīśuḥ pragraharahitaḥ ukthyaḥ stutyaḥ tricakraḥ cakratrayayuktaḥ san rajaḥ antarikṣaṃ pari vartate paribhramati |
mahat prabhūtaṃ tat rathanirmāṇasya karma vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ devatvasya devatvasya pravācanaṃ pravaktṛ praśyāpakam |
yacca yena karmaṇā yāṃ pṛthivīṃ ca puṣyatha puṣṭāṃ kurutha ||
1. na-la-sa- 'sudhanvanḥ putrāḥ' nāsti |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ta-na-ma-somadānavantaṃ tṛtīyasavanam anukuṇudhvam ( ta-akaṇudhvam ) |
3. la-graharahitaḥ |
4. sa-ga-ca-ṇa- 'mana' nāsti |
5. sa-ga-ca- ṇa-dyāvāpṛthivī vā ( na-ca ) |
ṛ. 2-82
rathaṃ ye cakruḥ suvṛttaṃ sucetaso'virharantaṃ manasaspari dhyayā |
tāṃ ū nva̭sya savanasya pītaya ā vo vājā ṛbhavo vedyāmasi ||
2 ||
ratham | ye | cakruḥ | suvṛttam | sucetasaḥ | avirharantam | manasaḥ | pari | dhyayā | tān | ū~ iti | nu | asya | savanasya | pītaye | ā | vaḥ | vājāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | vedyāmasi ||2||
vsucetasaḥ śobhanacittāḥ vye ṛbhavaḥ vsuvṛttaṃ suvartanacakram vavirharantam akuṭilaṃ vrathaṃ vmanasaḥ vdhyayā manasā dhyānenaprayatnena vnu vkṣipraṃ vcakruḥ vtān |
uśadvada evārthe |
tāneva vasya vsavanasya vpītaye etaṃ momaṃ pātuṃ he vvājāḥ he vṛbhavaḥ vvaḥ yuṣmān vā vvedyāmasi āvedayāmaḥ ||
tadvo vājā ṛbhavaḥ supravācanaṃ deveṣu vibhavo abhavanmahitvanam |
jivrī yatsantā pitarā sanājurā punaryuvānā carathāya takṣatha ||
3 ||
tat | vaḥ | vājāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | supravācanam | deveṣu | vi'bhvaḥ | abhavat | mahi'tvanam | jivrī iti | yat | santā | pitarā | sanā'jurā | punaḥ | yuvānā | carathāya | takṣatha || 3 ||
he vvājāḥ vṛbhavaḥ he vvibhavaḥ vibhavaḥ |
itarāpekṣayā parasparaṃ bahuvacanamityuktam |
vvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ vtat vmahitvanaṃ mahattvaṃ māhātmyaṃ vdeveṣu madhye vsupravācanaṃ pravācyam vabhavat |
tadiyuktaṃ yadigrāha |
vjivrī vṛddhau vsantā manto vsanājurā sadā jīrṇau santo vpitarā pitarau vpunaryuvānā niścataraṇau vcarathāya yathecchaṃ saṃcaraṇāya vtakṣatha saṃpāditavantaḥ ||
ekaṃ vi cakra camasaṃ caturvayaṃ niśarmaṇo gāmariṇīta dhītibhiḥ |
athā deveṣvamṛtatvamānaśa śruṣṭī vājā ṛbhavastadva ukthyam ||
4 ||
ekam | vi | cakra | camasam | catuḥ'vayam | ni | carmaṇaḥ | gām | ariṇīta | dhītibhiḥ | atha | deveṣu | amṛta'tvam | ānaśa | śruṣṭī | vājāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | tat | vaḥ | ukthyam || 4 ||
he ṛbhavaḥ vekaṃ vcamasam ekameva mantaṃ pānasādhanaṃ camasaṃ vcaturvayaṃ |
vayāḥ śākhāḥ |
catuḥśākhaṃ caturavayavaṃ vvi vcakra vyakuruta |
tathā vdhītibhiḥ karmabhiḥ vcarmaṇaḥ nūtanāṃ vgāṃ vniḥ variṇīta samastakuruta |
nistyeya samiḥyetasya sthāne |
vatha ataḥ kāraṇāt vdeveṣu madhye vamṛtatva- mānaśa prāputa |
he vvājāḥ he vṛbhavaḥ vtata tādṛśaṃ vtvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ karma |
vśruṣṭī iti kṣipranāmaiṭatat |
kṣipram vukthyaṃ stutyaṃ bhavati ||
1. kha-ga-ṇa-ta-na-bha-suvartanaṃ cakram; sa-suvartamānaṃ cakram |
2. kha-ga-na-ṇa-pa-pha-bha- 'yatheccham' nāsti |
ṛbhuto rayiḥ prathamaśravastamo vājaśratāso yamajījanannaraḥ | vibhvataṣṭe vidatheṣu pravācyo yaṃ devāmo'vathā sa vicarṣaṇiḥ || 5 || ṛbhutaḥ | rayiḥ | prathamaśravaḥ'tamaḥ | vāja'śrutāsaḥ | yam | ajījanan | naraḥ | vibhva'taṣṭaḥ | vidatheṣu | pra'vācyaḥ | yam | devāmaḥ | avatha | saḥ | vi'carṣaṇiḥ || 5 ||
ˇṛbhutaḥ ṛbhūṇāṃ sakāśāta ˇprathamaśravastamaḥ prathamaṃ mukhyamatiśāyitaṃ śravaḥ? atra yaśo vā yasya dhanasya tat ˇrayiḥ dhanam |
asmākaṃ bhavatviti śeṣaḥ |
ˇvājaśrutāmaḥ vājaiḥ agneḥ2 maharai viśyātāḥ5 ˇnaraḥ netāraḥ ṛbhavaḥ ˇyaṃ rayim ˇajījanan utpāditavantaḥ |
ˇvibhvataṣṭaḥ viśvabhirṛbhubhistaṣṭaḥ takṣaṇasaṃpannamaso'śvino ratho vā ˇvidatheṣu yajñeṣu ˇpravācyaḥ prakarṣeṇa stutyaḥ |
he ˇdevāmaḥ dyotamānāḥ yūyaṃ ˇyaṃ rathaṃ camasaṃ vā ˇavatha rakṣatha |
ˇsa ˇvicarṣaṇiḥ vividhaṃ draṣṭā bhavati draṣṭavyo bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
7 ||
ma vājyarvā sa ṛṣīrvacastayā sa śūro amtā pṛtanāsu duṣṭaraḥ | sa rāyaspoṣaṃ sa suvīryaṃ dadhe yaṃ vājo vibhvā ṛbhavo yamāvipuḥ || 6 || saḥ | vājī | arvā | saḥ | ṛṣiḥ | vacastayā | saḥ | śūraḥ | astā | pṛtanāsu | dustaraḥ | saḥ | rāyaḥ | poṣam | saḥ | su'vīryam | dadhe | yam | vājaḥ | vi'bhvā | ṛbhavaḥ | yam | āvipuḥ || 6 ||
ˇsa ˇvājī vajanavān balavān san ˇarvā araṇakuśalo bhavati |
ˇyama ˇṛṣiḥ atīndriyajñānī san ˇvacastayā stutyā yukto bhavati |
ˇsa ˇśūraḥ vikrāntaḥ san ˇastā kṣeptā śatrūṇāṃ bhavati |
svayaṃ ca ˇpṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu ˇduṣṭaraḥ nābhibhāvyo bhavati |
ˇyamaḥ eva ˇrāyaspoṣaṃ dhanapuṣṭiṃ ˇdadhe dhatte |
ˇsa ˇsuvīryaṃ suvīratvaṃ ˇdadhe dhatte |
ˇyaṃ manuṣyaṃ ˇvājo ˇvibhvā ˇṛbhavaḥ ca ˇāvipuḥ arakṣan |
ya evamevaṃ bhavatīti |
anyasya bahuvacanaikadeśakhyānām ṛbhuvyavahāraprācuryābhiprāyaḥ5 ||
śreṣṭhaṃ vaḥ peśo ardhi dhāyi darśataṃ stomau vājā ṛbhavastaṃ jujuṣṭana | dhīrāso hi ṣṭhā kuvaryo vipaścitastanvaṃ enā brahmaṇā vedayāmasi || 7 || śreṣṭham | vaḥ | peśaḥ | ardhi | dhāyi | darśatama | stomaḥ | vājāḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | tam | jujuṣṭana | dhīrāsaḥ | hi | stha | kuvaryaḥ | vipaḥ'citaḥ | tān | vaḥ | enā | brahmaṇā | ā | vedayāmasi ||7||

3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-sadasi khyātaḥ; gha-sa-sadābhikhyātaḥ; ta-sadābhikhyātaḥ |

‘upa no vājā:’ ityaṣṭacaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣamābharvaṃ traiṣṭubham |
pañcamyāścāśrataso'nuṣṭubha: |
tathā cānukrāntam—‘upa no'ṣṭau caturanuṣṭubhantam’ iti |
dyūḍhe daśarātre paṣṭhe'hani vaiśvadevaśastre ādyā: catasra: śasyā: |
‘upa no vājā iti trayodaśārbhavaṃ catasra’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) iti hi sūtritam ||
upa no vājā aśnuvantvṛbhukṣā devā yāta pathibhirdevayānai: |
yathā yajñaṃ manuṣo viśve āsu dadhidhve raṇvā: sudineṣvaham ||
1 ||
upa | na: | vājā: | adhvaram | ṛbhukṣā: | devā: | yāta | pathibhi: | devayānai: | yathā | yajñam | manuṣa: | viśve | āsu | dadhidhve | raṇvā: | sudineṣu | aham || 1 ||
he ‘vājā:’ he ‘ṛbhukṣā:’ ṛbhavo he ‘devā:’ ‘na:’ ‘adhvaraṃ’ yāgam ‘upa’ ‘yāta’ upagacchata |
‘devayānai:’ devairgantavyai: ‘pathibhi:’ mārgai: |
‘yathā’ yena prakāreṇa ‘manuṣa:’ mana: saṃbandhinīṣu ‘āsu’ ‘viśveṣu’ prajāsu yajamāneṣu madhye he ‘raṇvā:’ ramaṇīyā ṛbhava: ‘ahnāṃ’ ‘sudineṣu’ sudinanimitteṣu ‘yajñam’ asmadīyaṃ ‘dadhidhve’ dhārayatha |
yathā yena prakāreṇa yajñamāgamya mudinatvaṃ kurutha tathā yajamāyāteti ||
te vo hṛde manase santu yajñā juṣṭāso adya ghṛtanirṇijo gu: |
pra va: sutāso harayanta pūrṇā: kratve dakṣāya harṣayanta pītā: ||
2 ||
te | va: | hṛde | manase | santu | yajñā: | juṣṭāsa: | adya | ghṛtanirṇija: | gu: | pra | va: | sutāsa: | harayanta | pūrṇā: | kratve | dakṣāya | harṣayanta | pītā: || 2 ||
‘adya’ asmin yāgadine ‘te’ ete ‘yajñā:’ yāgasādhanā: somā: ‘vo’ hṛde yuṣmaddhṛdayāya ‘manase’ tadprītaye ‘santu’ bhavantu |
adyāsmin dine ‘juṣṭāsa:’ paryāptā: somā: ‘ghṛtanirṇija:’ miśraṇa- dravyeṇa dīparūpā: ‘gu:’ gacchantu yuṣmaddhṛdayam |
‘sutāsa:’ abhiputā: somā: camaseṣu ‘pūrṇā:’ ‘va:’ yuṣmadarthaṃ ‘pra’ ‘haryanta’ prahriyante |

he vājāḥ he ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ ṛbhavaḥ naḥ asmākaṃ pathaḥ mārgān yaṣṭave yaṣṭuṃ yajñamārgān vi citana vicetayata |

āśuṃ śīghragāminaṃ tamu tameva dadhikrāṃ devaṃ nu kṣipaṃ stavāma |
uta api ca divaḥ pṛthivyāḥ ca sakāśādasya ghāsaṃ carkarāma vikṣipāma |
ucchantīḥ tamo vivāsayantīḥ uṣaḥ māṃ prati sūdayantu rakṣantu phalāni |
viśvāni sarvāṇi duritāni ani parṣan anipārayantu |
anyadevatākeṣu mantreṣvanyadevatāstutintāsāṃ nipānabhāvācca virudhyate ||
mahaścarmyarvataḥ ṛtuprā dadhikrāvṇaḥ puruvārasya vṛṣṇaḥ |
yaṃ purubhyo dīdivāsaṃ nāgni dadarthurmitrāvaruṇā taturim ||
2 ||
mahaḥ | carkami | arvataḥ | ṛtu'prāḥ | dadhi'krāvṇaḥ | puru'vārasya | vṛṣṇaḥ | yam | puru'bhyaḥ | dīdivāsam | na | agnim | dadarthuḥ | mitrāvaruṇā | taturim || 2 ||
ṛtuprāḥ karmaṇāṃ pūrako'haṃ mahaḥ mahataḥ |
arvataḥ araṇavataḥ |
puruvārasya purubhiḥ bahubhirvarṇīyasya vṛṣṇaḥ varṣakasya dadhikrāvṇaḥ stutiṃ carkami atyarthaṃ karomi |
ha mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ tatugiṃ tārakaṃ yaṃ dīdivāsaṃ nāgni dīpyamānamagnimiva sthitaṃ purubhyaḥ manuṣyebhyastepāmupakārāya dadathuḥ dhārayathaḥ ||
yo aśvasya dadhikrāvṇo akārītsamiddha agra uṣaso vyuṣṭau |
anāgaṃ tamatiḥ kṛṇotu sa mitreṇa varuṇena sajoṣaḥ ||
3 ||
yaḥ | aśvasya | dadhi'krāvṇaḥ | akārīt | sam'iddhe | agrau | uṣasaḥ | vi'uṣṭau | anāgasam | tam | aditiḥ | kṛṇotu | saḥ | mitreṇa | varuṇena | sa'joṣaḥ || 3 ||
yaḥ yajamānaḥ aśvasya abhvarūpasya vyāptasya vā dadhikrāvṇaḥ stutim upaso vyuṣṭau prabhāte sati agnau samiddhe sati akārīt akārṣīt |
mitreṇa varuṇena cāhorātrābhimānidevābhyāṃ

\uta\ \sma\ api ca khalu \dravataḥ\ gacchataḥ \turaṇyataḥ\ tvaramāṇasya \pragṛdhinaḥ\ prakarṣeṇābhikāṅkṣataḥ \asya\ \dadhikrāvṇaḥ\ \devasya\ gati \veḥ\ gantuḥ pakṣiṇaḥ \parṇaṃ\ \na\ gamanaṃ patraṃ vā anusṛtya \yathā\ vānanyanye pakṣiṇastadvat \anu\ \vāti\ sarvo vegavān janaḥ |

antarbhāvitaṇyartho'yam ||

saṃghena gacchantīti śeṣaḥ ||

\uta\ api ca \syaḥ\ saḥ \vājī\ vejanavānaśvarūpo devaḥ \grīvāyāṃ\ garaṇamādhanāyāṃ kaṇṭhe \baddhaḥ\ \apikakṣe\ kakṣapradeśe \āsani\ āsye ca baddhaḥ |

he vāyo svam aśastīḥ abhiśastīḥ niryuvāṇaḥ niḥśeṣaṇa niyojayan niyutvān niyutsaṃjñakāśvavān indrasārathiḥ indrasahāyaḥ sana ā yāhi ||
anuṃ kṛṣṇe vasudhitī yemāte viśvapeśasā | vāyavā candreṇa rathena yāhi sutasya pītaye || 3 || anu | kṛṣṇe iti | vasudhitī iti vasu'dhitī | yemāte iti | viśva'peśasā | vāyo iti | ā | candreṇa | rathena | yāhi | sutasya | pītaye || 3 ||
kṛṣṇe kṛṣṇavarṇe vasudhitī vasūnāṃ dhāryau viśvapeśasā viśvarūpe dyāvāpṛthivyau svām anu yemāte anuga
ha vāyave tvaṃ poṣyāṇāṃ goṣaṇīyānāṃ harīṇāṃ śataṃ yuvasya yojaya rathe |
uta vā api ca te sahasriṇaḥ sahasrasaṃkhyāpūrakā aśvāḥ |
tairyukte rathaḥ pājasā balena ā yātu āgacchatu ||
24 ||
‘idaṃ vāmāsye’ iti ṣaḍarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ gāyatramaindrābārhaspatyam |
atrānukrama- ṇikā—‘idaṃ vāmaॅndrābārhaspatyaṃ gāyatram’ iti |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
idaṃ vāmāsye haviḥ priyāmindrābṛhaspatī | ukthaṃ madaśca śasyate || 1 || idam | vām | āsyai | haviḥ | priyam | indrābṛhaspatī iti | uktham | madaḥ | ca | śasyate || 1 ||
ha indrābṛhaspatī vāmāsye idaṃ priyaṃ haviḥ somarūpam |
prakṣipāmīti śeṣaḥ |
ukthaṃ śastraṃ ca madaḥ madajanakaṃ śasyate ||
ayaṃ vāṃ pari ṣicyate soma indrābṛhaspatī |
cārumadāya pītaye ||
2
asyā viniyoga: pūrvamantreṇa sahokta: |
he indrābṛhaspatī asme asmabhyaṃ rayiṃ dhanaṃ dhattam |
kīdṛśaṃ rayiṃm |
śatagvinaṃ śatasaṃkhyākābhirbhiryutam aśvāvantam aśvavantaṃ sahasriṇaṃ sahasra- saṃkhyāyuktam ||
indrābṛhaspatī vayaṃ sute gīrbhirhavāmahe | asya somasya pītaye || 5 || indrābṛhaspatī iti | vayam | sute | gī:'bhi: | havāmahe | asya | somasya | pītaye || 5 ||
sute some'bhiṣute gīrbhi: stutibhi: | śiṣṭaṃ gatam || sormamindrābṛhaspatī pibaṃtaṃ dāśuṣo gṛhe | mādyethāṃ tadaukasā || 6 || somam | indrābṛhaspatī iti | pibaṃtam | dāśuṣa: | gṛhe | mādyethām | tat'aukasā || 6 ||
he indrābṛhaspatī yuvāṃ dāśuṣa: havirdātaryajamānasya gṛhaṃ tadaukasā |
tadaiva yajamānagṛ- hamoko nivāsasthānaṃ yayostau |
tādṛśau santo somaṃ pibantam |
pītvā ca mādyethām iti |
25|
'yastastambha' ityekādaśacaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham |
daśamī jagatau |
daśamyeka- dadhyāvindrābṛhaspatidevatāke śiṣṭā nava bārhaspatyaḥ |
atrānukramaṇikā—' yastastambhaikādaśa bārhaspatya- manye aindrī copāntyayā jagatau ' |
ābhipulikepukthepu tṛtīyāmavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsina: stomavṛddhāvidaṃ sūktamāvāpārtham |
tathā ca sūtraṃ—' yastastambha yo adribhit ' ( āśva. śrau. 7. 9 ) iti |
ādhe bṛhaspatisave' marutvatīyanikkavatyo: sūktamubīye |
sūtritaṃ ca—' yastastambha dhunetaya iti sūkta- mukhīye ' ( āśva. śrau. 9. 5 ) iti |
yastastambha sahasā vi jamo antānbṛhaspatistriṣadhastho raveṇa |
taṃ pratnāsa ṛṣayo dīdhyānā: puro viprā dadhire mandrajihvam |
1 |
ya: | tastambha | sahasā | vi | jma: | antān | bṛhaspati: | tri'sadhastha: | raveṇa | tam | pratnāsa: | ṛṣaya: | dīdhyānā: | pura: | viprā: | dadhire | mandra'jihvam || 1 ||
ya: bṛhaspati: bṛhato devasya yajñasya vā pālayitā deva: sahasā balena jma: pṛthivyā: antān |
daśa diśa ityartha: |
tān vi tastambha vividhaṃ stambhatavān niyamitavān |
triṣadhastha: triṣu sthāneṣu vartamāna: raveṇa evaṃ tiṣṭhatyanena śabdena taṃ devaṃ pratnāsa: purāṇā: ṛṣaya: atīndriyadraṣṭāra: dīdhyānā: dīpyamānā: viprā: medhāvināṃ yajamānādaya: pura: dadhire purastāt sthāpitavantaḥ stutyādinā |
mandrajihvaṃ mādanajihvaṃ modanajihvam ||
1. kha-ga-ca-na-pa-bha-sa-dhattaṃ dattam |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-he indrābṛhaspatī sute |
3. ta-śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam; pa-vayaṃ vā havāmahe |
asya somasya pītaye |
4. la-indrābṛhaspatī indraśca bṛhaspatiśca |
5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-sa-trām |
6. ta-bārhaspatyāḥ |
7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-la-sa-°savane |
8. ta- bedasya |
9. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-bha-mandrajihvaṃ mandrā jihvā yasyāsau mandrajihva: tam ; ta-mandrajihvaṃ modanajihvam; na-mandrajihvaṃ mādanajihvaṃ tam; pa-mandrajihvaṃ kīdṛśaṃ mandrā jihvā yasyāsau mandrajihva: taṃ mandrajihvam |
dhunetayaḥ supraketaṃ madanto bṛhaspati abhi ye nastatase |
pṛṣantaṃ supramadagdhamuvaṃ bṛhaspate rakṣatādasya yonim ||
2 ||
dhune'tayaḥ | su'praketam | madantaḥ | bṛhaspate | abhi | ye | naḥ | tatase | pṛṣantam | su'pram | adagdham | ūrvam | bṛhaspate | rakṣatāt | asya | yonim || 2 ||
he bṛhaspate dhunetayaḥ |
dhunā śatrūṇāṃ kampayitrītirgatiyaiṣāṃ te |
tādṛśāḥ supraketaṃ śobhanaprajñaṃ tvāṃ madantaḥ mādayantaḥ naḥ asmatsambandhinaḥ ye ṛtvijaḥ abhi tatase ātmānamupakṣipanti stuvanti vā asya rūpāṃ yoniṃ kāraṇaṃ yajñaṃ yajamānaṃ vā māṃ rakṣatāta rakṣa |
yonirviśepyate |
pṛṣantaṃ phalāni siñcantaṃ supraṃ sarpaṇasvabhāvam adagdham ahiṃsitam ūrvam urum ||
'bṛhaspate yā' ityādike dve bārhaspatyaṃ paśau vapāpuroḍāśayoranuvākye |
'bṛhaspate yā paramā parāvaditi dve' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti hi sūtritam ||
bṛhaspate yā paramā paśavadata ā taṃ ṛtaspṛśo ni ṣeduḥ |
tubhyaṃ khātā avatā adridugdhā madhvaḥ śrotantyabhito virapśam ||
3 ||
bṛhaspate | yā | paramā | parā'vata | ataḥ | ā | te | ṛta'spṛśaḥ | ni | seduḥ | tubhyam | khātāḥ | avatāḥ | adri'dugdhāḥ | madhvaḥ | śrotanti | abhitaḥ | virapśam ||3||
he bṛhaspate yā prasiddhā paramā utkṛṣṭā parāvata atyantaṃ dūrabhūtā varmataḥ svargāsyāsthita |
yadvā liṅgavyatyayaḥ |
paramaṃ sthānamasti |
ataḥ asmādeśāna ā ā ityarthārthe |
abhramnāta 'te tava sambandhinaḥ ṛtaspṛśaḥ yajñaṃ spṛśanto'śvāḥ ni ṣeduḥ niṣaṇṇā bhavanti |
tubhyaṃ khātā avatāḥ |
tṛtopamaiṣā |
yathā khātāḥ kūpāḥ abhitaḥ śrotanti tadvata adridugdhāḥ grāvabhirabhiṣutāḥ somāḥ virapśaṃ viśeṣeṇa śabdena stotraṃ yathā bhavati tathā madhvaḥ madhuramayaṃ śrotanti sravanti ||
bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaṃ jāyamāno maho jyotiṣaḥ parame vyoman |
saptāsyastuvijāto raveṇa vi saptaśmiradhamattamāṃsi ||
4 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ | prathamam | jāyamānaḥ | mahaḥ | jyotiṣaḥ | parame | vi'oman | sapta'āsyaḥ | tuvi'jātaḥ | raveṇa | vi | sapta'raśmiḥ | adhamat | tamāṃsi || 4 ||
ayaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ mantrābhimānī devaḥ mahaḥ mahataḥ jyotiṣaḥ dīptasya ādityasya parame niratiśaye vyoman vyomani prathamaṃ jāyamānaḥ prādurbhavan saptāsyaḥ saptacchandomayamukhaḥ tuvijātaḥ bahuprakāraṃ
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha-sa-śobhanayajñam | 2. ta-ātmānamupakṣapayanti | 3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha- sa-'dve iti' nāsti | 4. la-ntra | 5. ta-la-avatāḥ avaṭāḥ | 6. kha-bha-avatāḥ; pa-pha-avaṭāḥ | 7. ta-śabdanama |
saṃbhūtaḥ ᵥraveṇa śabdena ᵥsasaraśmiḥ sarpaṇasvabhāvatejoyuktaḥ ᵥtamāṃsi ᵥvi ᵥadhamata |
‘dhamatirgati- karmā’ ( niru. 6. 2 ) iti niruktam |
vividhaṃ gacchati |
nāśayatītyarthaḥ ||
sa suṣṭubhā sa ṛkatā guṇena valaṃ ruroja phaligaṃ raveṇa |
bṛhaspatirustriyā hanyasūdaḥ kanikradadvāvaśatīrudājata ||
5 ||
saḥ | su'stubhā | saḥ | ṛkatā | guṇena | valam | ruroja | phaligam | raveṇa | bṛhaspatiḥ | ustriyāḥ | hanya'sūdaḥ | kanikradat | vāvaśatīḥ | ut | ājata || 5 ||
ᵥsaḥ ᵥbṛhaspatiḥ ᵥsuṣṭubhā śobhanastutimatā ᵥṛkatā dīptimatā ᵥguṇena aṅgirasāṃ ᵥraveṇa śabdena ca saha ᵥphaligam ||
‘jiphalā viśaraṇe’ ||
phalibhedaḥ |
tena gacchatīti phaligam |
ᵥvalaṃ’ valanāmāna- masuraṃ ᵥruroja babhañja |
dvitīyaḥ saśabdaḥ² pūraṇaḥ |
sa eva devaḥ ᵥustriyāḥ bhoganāmāstrāviṇīḥ ᵥhabhyasūdaḥ kṣīrādihaviṣprerikāḥ ᵥvāvaśatīḥ vāvayamānāḥ gāḥ ᵥkanikradat śabdaṃ kurvan ᵥudājata udghāṭayati sma ||
26 ||
‘evā pitre’ iti bārhaspatye paśau haviṣo yājyā |
‘evā pitre viśvadevāya vṛṣṇe viśve adya maruto viśva ūtī’ ( āśva. śrau. 3. 7 ) iti sūtritatvāt |
agniṣṭome vaiśvadevaśastre evaiṣa dhāvyā |
tathā ca sūtram—‘evā pitre viśvadevāya vṛṣṇe bhā no bhadrāḥ kratavo yantu viśvataḥ’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 18 ) iti³ ||
evā pitre viśvadevāya vṛṣṇe yajñairvidhema namasā havirbhiḥ |
bṛhaspate suprajā vīravanto vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||
6 ||
eva | pitre | viśva'devāya | vṛṣṇe | yajñaiḥ | vidhema | namasā | haviḥ'bhiḥ | bṛhaspate | su'prajāḥ | vīra'vantaḥ | vayam | syāma | patayaḥ | rayīṇām || 6 ||
ᵥpitre pālakāya ᵥviśvadevāya sarvadevatārūpāya |
devānāṃ mantrapratipādyatvādasya ca mantrābhimāni- tvāta viśvadevatvam⁵ |
yadvā |
atra devaśabdaḥ stutyarthaḥ |
sarvaiḥ stutyāya ᵥvṛṣṇe varṣakāya bṛhaspataye devāya ᵥeva evamuktaprakāreṇa ᵥyajñaiḥ tatsādhanaiḥ ᵥhavirbhiḥ ājyatvādilakṣaṇaiḥ ᵥnamasā stutyā ca ᵥvidhema paricarema |
he ᵥbṛhaspate ᵥsuprajāḥ śobhanaputrāḥ ᵥvīravantaḥ vīryopetāḥ ᵥvayaṃ ᵥrayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ ᵥpatayaḥ ᵥsyāma ||
1. ta-taṃ valam | 2. la-tacchabdaḥ | 3. ta-iti vaiśvadevati; na-iti nabeti; la-iti maveti | 4. kha-ga-ṇa-na-bha-sa-vaiśvadevatvam | 5. ta-śobhanaprajāḥ | ṛ. 2-87
sa idrājā pratijanyāni viśvā śuṣmeṇa tasthāvabhi vīryeṇa | bṛhaspatiṃ yaḥ subhṛtaṃ bibharti valguyati vandate pūrvabhājam || 7 || saḥ | it | rājā | prati'janyāni | viśvā | śuṣmeṇa | tasthau | abhi | vīryeṇa | bṛhaspatim | yaḥ | su'bhṛtam | bibharti | valguyati | vandate | pūrva'bhājam || 7 ||
idramādyatrayaṃ purohitapraśaṃsā iti brāhmaṇam (ai. brā. 8. 26) |
athavā sūktasya ṛgdvaya- varjitasya bārhaspatyatvāt bṛhaspatereva stutiḥ |
sa it sa eva rājā mūrdhābhiṣiktaḥ anyaḥ vā rājamānaḥ pratijanyāni |
janyaṃ yuddhaṃ prati balānītyarthaḥ |
pratyarthijanapadāni vā tāni viśvā sarvāṇi śuṣmeṇa balena vīryeṇa śarīrasāmarthyena vā abhi abhigamyākramya tasthau tiṣṭhati |
sa ityuktaṃ ka ityāha |
yaḥ bṛhaspatiṃ bṛhatāṃ mahatāṃ pālayitāraṃ devamuktalakṣaṇaṃ purohitaṃ vā subhṛtaṃ suṣṭhu haviḥ- stotrādinācchādanādinā vā bibharti bharati valguyati mauti |
vandate namaskaroti ca pūrvabhājam itarebhyaḥ prathamaṃ saṃskāraṃ kṛtvā |
evaṃ yaḥ karoti sa eva tathāviti ||
sa itkṣetri sudhita orkasi sve tasmā iḻāṃ pinvate viśvadānīm | tasmai viśaḥ svayamevā namante yasminbrahmā rājani pūrva eti || 8 || saḥ | it | kṣetri | su'dhitaḥ | orkasi | sve | tasmai | iḻā | pinvate | viśva'dānīm | tasmai | viśaḥ | svayam | eva | namante | yasmin | brahmā | rājani | pūrvaḥ | eti || 8 ||
sa it sa eva kṣetri nivasati |
sudhitaḥ suṣṭhu tṛptaḥ san sve svakīye eva orkasi sadane |
sa eva samṛddho nirbādhaḥ san sukhaināste |
tasmā iḻā bhūmiḥ viśvadānīṃ sarvakālaṃ pinvate vardhate phalaiḥ |
tasmai viśaḥ prajāḥ svayameva namante praṇatā bhavanti |
daṇḍanamantareṇa svayameva dattakarā bhavanti |
yasmin rājani brahmā pūrvaḥ prathamaṃ pūjyaḥ san eti gacchati |
vartata ityarthaḥ ||
apratīto jayati saṃ dhanāni pratijanyānyuta yā sarjanyā | avasyave yo varivaḥ kṛṇoti brahmaṇe rājā tamavanti devāḥ || 9 || apratitaḥ | jayati | sam | dhanāni | prati'janyāni | uta | yā | sa'janyā | avasyave | yaḥ | varivaḥ | kṛṇoti | brahmaṇe | rājā | tam | avanti | devāḥ || 9 ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-yaḥ | 2. ta-mahatāṃ mantrāṇām | 3. na-prathamasaṃbhaktāram | 4. ta- it | 5. ta-san | kutra | 6. ca-sarvadā |
sa eva pratīta: anyairapratigata: san yāni dhanāni pratijyāni pratispardhijanasambandhāni uta api ca sajanyā janasambandhāni dhanāni saṃ jayati |
ya: rājā avasyave avasīyase dhanarahitāya rakṣaṇamicchate brahmaṇe brāhmaṇāya vā prīṇanamicchate bṛhaspataye vā variva: dhanaṃ kṛṇoti karoti dadāti |
sa uktaprakāreṇa mahān bhavati |
tam eva devā: bhavanti rakṣanti ||
‘indraśca somam’ iti daśamī tṛtīyasavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsina: prasthitayājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘indraśca somaṃ pibataṃ bṛhaspate ā vo vahantu sasayo raghuṣyada:’ (āśva. śrau. 5. 5 ) iti ||
indra̭śca॒ soma̭m pibataṃ bṛhaspate'si॒nyuñje ma̭nda॒sānā̭ vṛṣaṇvasū | ā vo̭ vi॒śanti॒tvanda̭va: khā॒bhuvo̭'sme ra॒yiṃ sarva̭vīraṃ ni ya̭cchatam || 10 || indra̭: | ca | soma̭m | pibatam | bṛhaspate | asmin | yajñe | ma̭nda॒sānā̭ | vṛṣaṇvasū iti̭ vṛṣaṇ'vasū | ā | vām | vi॒śantṷ | inda̭va: | su'ābhuva̭: | asme iti̭ | ra॒yim | sarva̭vīram | ni | ya̭cchatam || 10 ||
he bṛhaspate tvaṃ indraśca somaṃ pibatam |
kīdṛśau yuvām |
asmin‌yajñe mandasānā hṛṣyantau vṛṣaṇvasū varṣitṛdhanau yajamānebhyo dīyamānadhanāvityartha: |
vāṃ yuvāṃ svābhuva: suṣṭhu sarvato bhavantaḥ |
kṛtsnaśarīravyāpanasamarthā ityartha: |
tādṛśā: indava: somā: viśantu yuvayo: śarīraṃ praviśantu |
asme asmabhyaṃ rayiṃ dhanaṃ sarvavīraṃ sarvaputrādyupetaṃ ni yacchataṃ dattam ||
bṛha̭spata indra॒ vardha̭taṃ na॒: sacā̭ sā vāṃ su॒mati̭rbhūtva॒sme | avi̭ṣṭaṃ dhi॒yo ji̭gataṃ pu॒raṃdhī̭rja॒stama̭ryo va॒nuṣāma̭rātī: || 11 || bṛha̭spate | indra̭ | vardha̭tam | na̭: | sacā̭ | sā | vām | su॒mati̭: | bhūtṷ | asme iti̭ | avi̭ṣṭam | dhi॒ya: | ji̭gatam | pura̭m'dhī: | ja॒jastam | arya̭: | va॒nuṣām | arā̭tī: || 11 ||
he bṛhaspate he indra yuvāṃ na: asmān vardhataṃ vardhayata pravṛddhān kurutam |
sā sumati: yuvoranugrahabuddhi: asme asmāsu sacā saha bhṛtu bhavatu |
dhiya: karmāṇi aviṣṭaṃ rakṣataṃ pāraṃ nayatam |
puraṃdhī: |
puraṃ śarīraṃ dhīyate sthāpyate yābhiritibhiktā: stutī: jigataṃ prabudhyatam |
vanuṣāṃ saṃbhaktānāmasmākam arya: gantrī: arātī: |
śatrūnityartha: |
jajastaṃ yudhyataṃ kṣapayatamithyartha: |
chandasyarātiśabda: strīliṅga: ||
evaṃ stutiṃ nigamayyābhīṣṭaṃ prārthitavān ||
27 ||
vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārda nivārayan |
pumarthāṃsturo deyāddivātīrthamaheśvara: ||
śrīmatsāyaṇamantrīśo dāśatayyāstṛtīyeke |

iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukkapālasāmrājyadhuraṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśe ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye tṛtīyāṣṭake saptamo'dhyāya: samāpta: ||
1. pa-jayati saṃjayet | 2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-avasīyate | 3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-la-iti | tatpāṭhastu | 4. pa-vāṃ yuvām | 5. ta-kṣapayitumityartha: | 6. ga-la-samāpta: ’ nāsti |
|| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat |
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram ||
athāṣṭamaṃ1 vyākhyātumārabhate |
‘ idamu tyat ’ ityekādaśarcaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamuṣo- devatākam |
‘ idamupasthaṃ tu ’ ityanukrāntam |
prātaranuvāke upasye ṛtau traiṣṭubhe chandasyāśvinaśastre idaṃ sūktam |

4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-bha--anujñāpayatītyarthaḥ |
ayaṃ savitrā triḥ tribhedam antarikṣam|
antarā kṣāntaṃ bhavati jagadityantarikṣam|
paribhūḥ paribhavitā' san mahisvanā mahitvena hanvati vyāpnoti|
'vāyuvidyudūruṇyākhyo lokā antarikṣabhedāḥ sa vāyulokaṃ sa varuṇalokam' (kau. u. 1. 3) ityādi śruteḥ|
trīṇi rajāṃsi anātmakāni trīṇi kṣityantarikṣayugalakṣān trīn lokān hanvati|
pūrvamantarikṣasyaivāvāntarabheda uktaḥ iha tu sāmānyākāreṇetyapunaruktiḥ|

atha suṣuptidhyoravāntarabheda ucyate|
tisro divaḥ indraprajāpatityākhyān trīn lokān hanvati|
tathā tisraḥ pṛthivīḥ hanvati|
kṣityavāntarabhedān lokān hanvati|
evaṃ sarvatra vyāso devaḥ tribhirdevaiḥ karmabhiḥ uṣṇavarṣahimākhyaiḥ naḥ asmān manā ātmanaiva svīyānugrahabuddhayaiva abhi rakṣati abhirakṣatu|
na: asmān kṣapābhi: rātribhi: ahabhiśca | sarveṣu dineṣvityartha: | jinvatu prīṇayatu dhanādibhi: | bhasme asmāsu prajāvantaṃ rayiṃ samminvatu vyāpnotu | prāpayatvityartha: || 4 ||
‘abhūheva: ’ iti ṣaḍṛcaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ sāvitram |
anayā triṣṭup śiṣṭāstriṣṭubanta- paribhāṣayā jagatya: |

agniṣṭome vaiśvadeva- śastre sāvitranividvānamidam |
tathā ca sūtram—‘abhūheva ekayā ca daśabhiśca svabhūte ’ ( ābha. śrī. 5. 18 ) iti |
ādyā sāvitragrahasyānuvākyā |
tathā ca sūtram—‘sāvitreṇa graheṇa caranyabhūheva: savitā vanyo nu na: ’ ( ābha. śrī. 5. 18 ) iti ||
abhūheva: savitā vanyo nu naṃ idānīmaha upavācyo nṛbhi: | vi yo ratnā bharjati mānavebhya: śreṣṭhaṃ no anna draviṇaṃ yathā dadhat || 1 || abhūt | deva: | savitā | vanya: | nu | na: | idānīm | aha: | upavācya: | nṛbhi: | vi | ya: | ratnā | bharjati | mānavebhya: | śreṣṭham | na: | anna | draviṇam | yathā | dadhat || 1 ||
sa: savitā deva: abhūt prādurāsīt |
ata: nu kṣaprameva na: asmākaṃ vanya: vandanīyo bhavati |
idānīṃ yāgakāle aha: tṛtīyasavane nṛbhi: asmadīyairhotṛbhi: upavācya: stutya: bhavati |
ya: deva: mānavebhya: manorapatyebhyo yajamānebhyasteṣāmarthāya ratnā ramaṇīyāni dhanāni vi bharjati sa deva: śreṣṭhaṃ praśastyaṃ draviṇaṃ gavādilakṣaṇaṃ dhanaṃ na: asmabhyam atra asmin karmaṇi yathā dadhat dadhātvityartha: |
tathā vanyā upavācyaścābhūditi ||
devebhyo hi prathamaṃ yajñiyabhyo'mṛtatvaṃ suvāsiṃ bhāgamuttamam | ādihāmānaṃ savitarvyūrṇuṣe'nūcīnā jīvitā mānuṣebhya: || 2 || devebhya: | hi | prathamam | yajñiyabhya: | amṛtatvam | suvāsiṃ | bhāgam | uttamam | āt | it | dāmānaṃ | savita: | vi | ūrṇuṣe | anūcīnā | jīvitā | mānuṣebhya: ||2||

hiśabda: prasiddhau |

ādit anantarameva dāmānaṃ haviṣāṃ dātāraṃ he savita: vyūrṇuṣe prakāśayasi |

ṛ. 2-89
vparvatasya indrasakhasya vetannāmakasya vdevasya vmarutāṃ ca vbhrātuḥ rakṣakasya vbhagasya devasya ca vavāsi rakṣaṇāni vā vagni āvṛṇe |
kiṃca vpatiḥ varuṇo devaḥ vjanyāt janasambandhāt vaṃhasaḥ pāpāt vnaḥ asmān vpāt pātu rakṣatu |
vuta api ca vmitraḥ ca vmitriyāt mitrabhāvena vnaḥ asmān vuruṣyet rakṣatu ||
6 ||
nū raudasī ahinā budhnyena stuvīta devīṃ apyebhiriṣṭaiḥ |
samudraṃ na saṃcaraṇe saniṣyavau gharmasvaraso nyo3 arpa vran ||
6 ||
nu | raudasī iti | ahinā | budhnyena | stuvīta | devīṃ iti | apyebhiḥ | iṣṭaiḥ | samudram | na | sam'caraṇe | saniṣyavaḥ | gharma'svarasaḥ | nadyaḥ | arpa | vran || 6 ||
he vrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau yuvām vahinā vbudhnyena ahirbudhnyāmakena devena saha vnu kṣipram vapyebhiḥ āpanīyaiḥ viṣṭaiḥ kāmairnimittabhūtaiḥ vstuvīta stuve |
vsaniṣyavaḥ dhanānāṃ saṃbhaktumicchantaḥ vsaṃcaraṇe saṃcārāya samudramadhyagamanāya vsamudraṃ vna samudramiva |
taṃ yathā stuvanti tadvat |
yasmādete devāḥ vgharmasvarasaḥ dīptadhvanayaḥ vnadyaḥ nadīḥ vapa vvran apavṛṇvantīti parokṣa iva |
athavā te devā nadīrapavṛṇvantu ityāśīḥ |
tasmāt stuve iti sambandhaḥ ||
daivainau devyaditini pātu devatrātā trāyatāmaprayucchan |
nahi mitrasya varuṇasya dhāsimarhāmasi pramiyaṃ sānvagreḥ ||
7 ||
daivaiḥ | naḥ | devī | aditiḥ | ni | pātu | devaḥ | trātā | trāyatām | apra'yucchan | nahi | mitrasya | varuṇasya | dhāsim | arhāmasi | pra'miyam | sānu | agreḥ || 7 ||

agniḥ | īśe | vasuvyasya | agniḥ | mahaḥ | saubhagasya | tāni | asmabhyam | rāsate || 8 ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pha-bha-la-sa- adreḥ sakhasya |
2. pa-devī dyotamāne he |
3. kha-ga-ca-la- ‘samudraṃ na’ nāsti |

7 ||
' mahī dyāvāpṛthivī ' iti saptamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyam |
pūrvatra ' tri- gāyatryantaṃ tu ' ityuktatvāt pañcamyādyāstisro gāyatrya: śiṣṭāstriṣṭubha: |

mahī dyāvāpṛthivī iha jyeṣṭhe rucā bhavatāṃ śucaryadbhirarkai:|
yatsīṃ variṣṭhe bṛhatī viminvanttruvadokṣā praprathānebhiravai: ||
1 ||
mahī iti | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | iha | jyeṣṭhe iti | rucā | bhavatām | śucaryat'bhi: | arkai: | yat | sīm | variṣṭhe iti | bṛhatī iti | vi'minvan | ruvat | ha | ukṣā | praprathānebhi: | evai: || 1 ||
^iha yajñe ^mahī mahatyau ^jyeṣṭhe praśasye ^dyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^rucā dīhyā yukte ^śucayadbhi: dīpyadbhi: ^arkai: mantrai: somādihavirbhirvā yukte ^bhavatām |
^yat yasmāt ^sīṃ sarvata: ^variṣṭhe uttarare ^bṛhatī mahatyau dyāvāpṛthivyau ^viminvan paricchindan sthāpayan ^ukṣā sektā parjanya:
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pha-bha-la- āgacchanti |
^pramathānebhiḥ prathamānaiḥ ^evaiḥ gamanaśīlairharṣirmairvā saha ^havat rauti śabdaṃ karoti|
^ha iti pūraṇaḥ|
tasmāducā bhavatāmiti saṃbandhaḥ|
devī devebhiryajate yajatrairaminatī tasthaturukṣamāṇe|
ṛtāvarī adṛhā devaputre yajñasya netrī śucayadbhirarkaiḥ|
2|
devī iti| devebhiḥ| yajate iti| yajatraiḥ| aminatī iti| tasthatuḥ| ukṣamāṇe iti| ṛtavarī ityṛta'varī| adṛhā| devaputre iti deva'putre| yajñasya| netrī iti| śucayat'bhiḥ| arkaiḥ|| 2||
^devī devyau ^yajate yaṣṭavye ^aminatī prajā ahiṃsavyau ^ukṣamāṇe siñcavyau ^ṛtāvarī ṛtavatyau ^adṛhā adrohyau drohamukurvatyau ^devaputre devotpādityau ^yajñasya ^netrī nirvāhayitryau dyāvāpṛthivyau ^devebhiḥ devaiḥ ^yajatraiḥ yaṣṭavyaiḥ sahite ^śucayadbhiḥ śocayadbhiḥ ^arkaiḥ mantraiḥ ^havirbhirvā yukte ^tasthatuḥ tiṣṭhataḥ syātāmityarthaḥ|
sa itsvapā bhuvaneṣvāsa ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna|
urvī gabhīre rajasī sumeke avaṃśe dhīraḥ śacyā samairat|
3|
saḥ| it| su'apāḥ| bhuvaneṣu| āsa| yaḥ| ime iti| dyāvāpṛthivī iti| jajāna| urvī iti| gabhīre iti| rajasī iti| sumeke iti su'meke| avaṃśe| dhīraḥ| śacyā| sam| airat|| 3||
^sa ^it sa eva ^svapāḥ śobhanakarmā san ^bhuvaneṣu ^āma āste|
^ya ^ime ^dyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^jajāna utpāditavān|
yaśca prajāpatiḥ ^urvī ubhyau vistīrṇe ^gabhīre avicale ^sumeke śobhanarūpe ^avaṃśe utpattirahite anādhāre vāntarikṣe ^dhīraḥ dhīmān ^śacyā kuśalakarmaṇā ^samairat samyak preritavān|
sa eva svapā iti|
nu raudasī bṛhadbhirno varūthaiḥ patnīvadbhiriṣayantī sujoṣāḥ|
urūcī viśvai yajate ni pātaṃ dhiyā syāma rathyaḥ sadāsāḥ|
4|
nu| rodasī iti| bṛhat'bhiḥ| naḥ| varūthaiḥ| patnīvat'bhiḥ| iṣayantī iti| su'joṣāḥ| urūcī iti| viśvai iti| yajate iti| ni| pātam| dhiyā| syāma| rathyaḥ| sadāsāḥ||4||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-satyavatyau| 2. pa-dīpayadbhiḥ| 3. pa-arcanīyaiḥ| 4. pa-śobhana- rūpe rajasī bhuvane |

dhiyetyādi gatam ||
'pra vām' ityādigāyatrastṛco vyūḍhe daśarātre vaiśvadevaśastre dyāvāpṛthivyanividdhānīyatvena prakṣepyaḥ |

upasargaśruterucita- kriyādhyāhāraḥ |
upa gacchāma iti śeṣaḥ ||

yadvā |
tanvā svasvaśarīraikadeśena mithaḥ parasparaṃ punāne śodha- yantyau |

sanāt sadākālam ṛtaṃ yajñam ūhārthe vahathaḥ ||
mahī mitrasya sādhadhustarantī pipratī ṛtam | pari yajñaṃ ni pedhathuḥ || 7 || mahī iti | mitrasya | sādhadhuḥ | tarantī iti | pipratī iti | ṛtam | pari | yajñam | ni | pedhathuḥ || 7 ||

8 ||
'kṣetrasya patinā' ityaṣṭacaṃ dvādaśa sūktaṃ vāmadevasyārṣam |
ādyācaturthīṣaṣṭhīsaptamyo'nuṣṭubhaḥ pañcamī purauṣṇig |

śiṣṭāstisrastriṣṭubhaḥ |
ādyāstisraḥ
1. pa-rathyaḥ rathavanto vayaṃ dhiyā stutyā sadāsāḥ tvāṃ sarvadā bhajamānāḥ syāma bhūyāsma | 2. ta- vyūḍhe | 3. ta-rājathaḥ īśāne bhavataḥ | 4. kha-ga-ca-pha-svasvaśarīraikyādinā | 5. ta-sādhadhaḥ sādhayathaḥ | 6. kha-na-la-sa-āśrayataḥ |
kṣetrapatidevatākāścaturthī śunāsyadevatākā pañcamyaṣṭamyau śunāsīradevatāke ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau sītādevatāke |
tathā cānukramaṇikā—‘ kṣetrasyādyau tisraḥ kṣetrapatyāḥ śunāyaikā parā purauṣṇīk sānanyā ca śunāsī- rābhyāmupāntye sītāyai te'nuṣṭubhāvādyā ca caturthī ca ’ iti ||
kṣetrasya karṣaṇe'nena sūktena pratyacaṃ juhuyājjapedvā |

asoryāme hoturatiriktaukya āśvinaśastrādūrdhvaṃ catvāryatiriktaukyāni |
tatrādyā pari- dhānīyā |

kṣetrasya patinā vayaṃ hitenaiva jayāmasi |
gāmaśvaṃ poṣayitvā sa nau mṛḻātīdṛśe ||
1 ||

2 ||

catvāri | śṛṅgā | trayaḥ | asya | pādāḥ | dve iti | śīrṣe iti | sapta | hastāsaḥ | asya tridhā | baddhaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | roravīti | mahaḥ | devaḥ | martyān | ā | viveśa || 3 ||
yadyapi sūktasyāmisūryādipadevatākatvāt pañcadhāyaṃ mantro vyākhyeyastathāpi niruktānuktanītyā yajñātmakāgneḥ sūryasya ca prakāśakatvena tatparatayā vyākhyāyate|
asya yajñātmakasyāgneḥ catvāri śṛṅgā catvāro vedāḥ śṛṅgasthānīyāḥ|
yadyapyāstambena ‘yajñaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ sa tribhirvedairvidhīyate (paribhā. 1. 3) ityuktaṃ tathāpyātharvaṇasya itarānapekṣayaiva ekābhisādhyānāṃ kṛtsnakarmaṇāmabhidhāyaka- tvāttadapekṣayā catvāri śṛṅgetyuktam|
trayo asya pādāḥ savanāni trīṇyasya pādāḥ|
pravṛtti- sādhanātvāt pādā ityucyante|
dve śīrṣe brahmaudanaṃ pravaryaśca|
iṣṭisomapradhānenyedamityuktam|
sapta hastāsaḥ sapta chandāṃsi|
hastāḥ anuṣṭhānasya mukhyasādhanam|
chandāṃsyapi devatāprīṇanasya mukhyasādhanamiti hastavyavahāraḥ|
tridhā baddhaḥ mantrabrāhmaṇakalpaiḥ10 triprakāraṃ baddhaḥ|
bandhanamasya tanniṣpādyatvam|
vṛṣabhaḥ phalānāṃ varṣitā roravīti|
bhṛśaṃ śabdāyate|
ṛgyajuḥsāmoktyaiḥ śabyāgastutirūpairhrāguptādidharvanibhirasau rauti11|
evaṃ maho devaḥ12 martyān ā viveśa|
martyairyajamānairniṣpādyatvāt praveśa upacaryate|
atra yāskaḥ—‘catvāriśśṛṅgeti vedā vā eta uktāḥ’ (niru. 13. 7) ityādinā niravocat|
tadatrānusaṃdheyam|
atha sūryapakṣe vyākhyāyate— asyādityasya catvāri śṛṅgāṇi catasro diśaḥ|
etāḥ13 śrayaṇārthatvāt14 śṛṅgāṇītyupacaryante|
trayo asya pādāḥ|
trayo vedāḥ pādasthānīyā bhavanti gamanasādhanatvāt|
tathā hi—‘ṛgbhiḥ pūrvāhe divi deva īyate’ ityupakramya ‘vaidairśanyastribhireti sūryaḥ’ (tai. brā. 3. 12. 9. 1) iti hi vedatrayeṇa gatirāmnātā|
dve śīrṣe|
ahaśca rātriśca dve śirasī|
sapta hastāso asya|
sapta raśmayaḥ ṣaḍvilakṣaṇā ṛtava ekaḥ sādhāraṇa iti vā sapta hastā bhavanti|
tridhā baddhastriṣu sthāneṣu kṣityādiṣu agnyādyātmakatvena14 saṃbaddhaḥ|
grīṣmavarṣāhemantādyaistribhirbhedā baddho vā|
vṛṣabho varṣitā roravīti|
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-stuvāmaḥ|
2. ta-digmapāṇi vedacatuṣṭayarūpāṇi vā|
3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa-iva|
4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-uttareṣvapi; la-sa-antareṣvapi|
5. ta-gauravarṇaḥ|
6. ca-pha-sa-pharītyā|
7. ta-sa trayāṇāṃ varṇānām|
8. ta-hastaḥ|
9. na-hastavyāpāraḥ|
10. na-la-sa-mantra- kalpabrāhmaṇe|
11. pa-la-stauti|
12. ta-devaḥ eṣa yajñātmā mahānubhāvo devaḥ|
‘atha’ ityārabhya ‘draṣṭavyam’ ityantasya sthāne ‘evaṃ sūryādipakṣe yuktyā yojyam’ iti pāṭhaḥ pa-pustake vartate|
13. kha-na-evāśravaṇārthatvāt; ga-bha-la-sa-evaśravaṇārthatvāt; ca-ṇa-pha-evāśrayaṇārthatvāt; ta- evāśrayaṇārthatvāt|
14. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-la-sa-anyonyātmakatvena|
śabdaṃ karoti vṛthādidvārā |
sa maho mahān devo martyānā viveśa tanmiyantayā |
' sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaśca ' ( ṛ. saṃ. 1. 115. 1 ) iti hi śrutam ||
evaṃ svabādipakṣe'pi yojyam |
śābdikastu śabdabrahmaparatayā catvāri śṛṅgeti catvāri padajātāni nāmānyāte copasarganipātāśca ityādinā vyācakṣate |
apare svaparathā |
tatsarvamatra draṣṭavyam ||

sūrya ekaṃ jajāna |
